# PART 05 — TONE 5: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*[Engraved Headpiece: An ornamental border depicting the Prophet Moses, King David, the Prophet Isaiah, Christ the Good Shepherd, the Most Pure Virgin Theotokos with the Christ Child, and Saint John Chrysostom]*

*On Saturday, at Little Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 4 verses, and sing 3 Resurrection Stichera of the Octoechos, repeating the first.*

*Tone 5:*

By Thy precious Cross, O Christ, Thou hast put the devil to shame; and by Thy Resurrection Thou hast blunted the sting of sin, and hast saved us from the gates of death. We glorify Thee, O Only-begotten One. *(Twice)*[^5040]

Granting resurrection unto the human race, He was led as a sheep to the slaughter; the princes of hades were terrified before Him, and the sorrowful gates were lifted up; for Christ, the King of glory, entered in, saying unto those in bonds: Come forth! and unto those in darkness: Show yourselves![^5041]

A great wonder! The Creator of things invisible, having suffered in the flesh out of love for mankind, hath risen immortal. Come, ye families of the nations, let us worship Him; for having been delivered from delusion by His tender mercy, we have learned to hymn the one God in three Hypostases.[^5042]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion:*
The prophecies concerning thee have been fulfilled, O pure Virgin: for one among the prophets foretold thee as the gate looking toward the East, through which no one passes save only thy Creator and the Maker of the whole world; and another foretold the bush burned with fire, in that the fire of the Godhead dwelt in thee, yet thou didst remain unconsumed; and another foretold the holy mountain from which a cornerstone was cut without human hand, and smote the image of the spiritual Nebuchadnezzar. Truly great and most glorious is the mystery that is in thee, O Mother of God! Therefore we glorify thee; for through thee salvation hath come unto our souls.[^5043]

*Then: O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: The Lord is King, with its verses. And after Vouchsafe, O Lord, the litany is not said, but immediately we sing on the Aposticha the Resurrection Sticheron.*[^5044]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Sticheron, Tone 5:*
Thee, the incarnate Savior Christ, who wast not separated from the heavens, in the voices of songs we magnify; for Thou didst accept the Cross and death for our race, as Thou art the Lover of mankind...[^5045]

...O Lord; having overthrown the gates of hades, Thou didst rise on the third day: save Thou our souls.[^5046]

*Other Stichera, Prosomoia, to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 5.*
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйся по́стникѡвъ):*
*Verse:* I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.[^5047]
I stretch forth my hands unto thee, and open my defiled lips in supplication, and bend the knee of my heart, and in mind now touch thy most pure feet, O Pure One, and fall down before thee: Heal my ancient, manifold, and incurable ailments; heal my sores by thy goodness; deliver me from enemies visible and invisible; lighten, O Maiden, the heavy burden of my slothfulness, that I may sing and glorify thee, through whom the world hath found great mercy.[^5048]

*Verse:* Give ear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.[^5049]
Rejoice, thou who didst ineffably conceive the Son of God, O All-Immaculate One, and didst bear Him who truly received flesh like unto ours from thy blood, having an intellective and self-governing soul, who unstintingly clothed Himself in Adam, out of mercy and ineffable goodness; whence in two natures Christ is proclaimed unto us, showing forth in Himself the operation of both: Him do thou entreat to grant our souls great mercy.[^5050]

*Verse:* The rich among the people shall entreat thy face.[^5051]
Rejoice, beauty of Jacob, which God chose and loved, gate of the saved, flame-bearing tongs, loosed curse, all-blessed one, womb that contained God, elevation of the fallen, more holy than the Cherubim, exceeding all creation, sight difficult to behold, most novel hearing, ineffable speech, chariot of the Word, cloud from which shone forth the Sun, illuminating us who sit in darkness, and granting great mercy.[^5052]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Let us honor the divinely seemly and venerable Maiden, more honorable than the Cherubim; for the Creator of all, desiring to become man, dwelt in her ineffably. O marvelous things and most glorious mysteries! Who that hath heard this will not marvel? that God becomes man, yet without change in Him; and He passed through the gates of virginity, yet no diminution was left therein, even as the prophet saith: No man shall ever pass through them, save only the Lord God of Israel, who hath great mercy.[^5053]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. And after Our Father: Troparion: "Co-beginningless Word"; Theotokion: "Rejoice, gate of the Lord"; litany, and the little dismissal.*[^5054]`

*On Saturday, at Great Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing Resurrection Stichera, Tone 5:*

By Thy precious Cross, O Christ, Thou hast put the devil to shame; and by Thy Resurrection Thou hast blunted the sting of sin, and hast saved us from the gates of death. We glorify Thee, O Only-begotten One.[^5055]

Granting resurrection unto the human race, He was led as a sheep to the slaughter; the princes of hades were terrified before Him, and the sorrowful gates were lifted up; for Christ, the King of glory, entered in, saying unto those in bonds: Come forth! and unto those in darkness: Show yourselves![^5056]

A great wonder! The Creator of things invisible, having suffered in the flesh out of love for mankind, hath risen immortal. Come, ye families of the nations, let us worship Him; for having been delivered from delusion by His tender mercy, we have learned to hymn the one God in three Hypostases.[^5057]

*Other Stichera, Eastern (by Anatolius), same Tone:*

An evening worship do we offer unto Thee, the never-setting Light, who at the end of the ages didst shine forth unto the world in the flesh as in a mirror, and didst descend even unto hades, dissolving the darkness there, and showing forth the light of the Resurrection unto the nations: O Giver of light, Lord, glory to Thee![^5058]

We glorify Christ, the Author of our salvation; for when He rose from the dead, the world was saved from delusion, the angelic choir rejoices, the delusion of demons is put to flight, fallen Adam is raised up, and the devil is brought to naught.[^5059]

They of the guard were instructed by the lawless men: Conceal the Resurrection of Christ, and take...[^5060]

...pieces of silver, and say that while we slept, the dead man was stolen from the tomb. Who hath seen, who hath ever heard of a dead man being stolen? especially one anointed and naked, leaving His burial wrappings in the tomb? Be not deceived, O Jews! Learn the sayings of the prophets, and understand that He is truly the Redeemer of the world, and all-powerful.[^5061]

O Lord, who didst lead hades captive and trample down death, our Savior, who hast enlightened the world by Thy precious Cross: have mercy on us.[^5062]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion:*
In the Red Sea the image of the unwedded Bride was inscribed of old: there Moses, the divider of the water; here Gabriel, the minister of the wonder. Then Israel traversed the deep dry-shod; but now the Virgin hath borne Christ without seed. The sea remained impassable after the passage of Israel; the Immaculate One remained incorrupt after the birth of Emmanuel. O Thou who art, and who wast before, and hast appeared as man: O God, have mercy on us.[^5063]

*Entrance with the censer. O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon and litany; then Vouchsafe, O Lord. Let us complete our evening prayer:*[^5064]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Stichera, Tone 5:*
Thee, the incarnate Savior Christ, who wast not separated from the heavens, in the voices of songs we magnify; for Thou didst accept the Cross and death for our race, as the Lord who lovest mankind: having overthrown the gates of hades, Thou didst rise on the third day, saving our souls.[^5065]

*Other Stichera according to the Alphabet.*
*Verse:* The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.[^5066]
From Thy pierced side, O Life-giver, Thou didst pour forth streams of remission, life, and salvation unto all; and in the flesh Thou didst accept death, granting immortality unto us; and having dwelt in the tomb, Thou didst set us free, gloriously raising us up with Thyself as God. Therefore we cry aloud: O Lord, Lover of mankind, glory to Thee![^5067]

*Verse:* For He hath established the world, which shall not be shaken.[^5068]
Marvelous is Thy Crucifixion and Thy descent into hades, O Lover of mankind; for having despoiled it, and having gloriously raised up with Thyself as God the ancient prisoners, Thou didst open Paradise and account them worthy to receive it. Therefore unto us also...[^5069]

...who glorify Thy third-day Resurrection, grant cleansing of sins, accounting us worthy to dwell in Paradise, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.[^5070]

*Verse:* Holiness befits Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days.[^5071]
Thou who for our sake didst accept suffering in the flesh, and didst rise on the third day from the dead: heal the carnal passions of our flesh, raise us up from grievous transgressions, O Lover of mankind, and save us.[^5072]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
A temple and a gate art thou, a palace and a royal throne, O all-honorable Virgin, through whom my Deliverer, Christ the Lord, shone forth upon those sleeping in darkness, being the Sun of Righteousness, wishing to enlighten those whom He created in His image by His own hand. Therefore, O All-Hymned One, as thou hast acquired motherly boldness toward Him, unceasingly pray that our souls be saved.[^5073]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. After Our Father: Exclamation: "For Thine is the kingdom":*[^5074]

*Troparion, Tone 5:*
The co-beginningless Word with the Father and the Spirit, who for our salvation was born of the Virgin, let us faithful hymn and worship; for He was well-pleased to ascend the Cross in the flesh, to endure death, and to raise up the dead by His glorious Resurrection.[^5075]

*Theotokion:*
Rejoice, impassable gate of the Lord! Rejoice, wall and protection of those who flee unto thee! Rejoice, storm-free haven and unwedded bride, who didst bear in the flesh thy Creator and God: cease not to intercede for those who praise and worship thy giving birth.[^5076]

*On Saturday, at Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 5.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He who crushes battles with a high arm, Christ hath shaken off; but Israel He saved, as they sang a hymn of victory.*[^5077]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.[^5078]
*Troparion 1:* To offer thee a song according to thy worth, O Lady, we all are at a loss; for thy glory is above all. Nevertheless, O Bride of God, abhor not the supplication offered unto thee with fear and love.[^5079]

*Troparion 2:* We all flee unto the water of thine unfailing fountain, O Virgin Theotokos, crying aloud: O only joy of our race, all-pure one, ask peace for thy churches.[^5080]

*Glory...*
God, who was well-pleased to receive flesh from thee, O Pure One, established thee as a haven for those in perils. Therefore, falling down before thee, we cry aloud: Grant Thy help unto Thy servants.[^5081]

*Both now...*
May thy prayer, O most pure Virgin, be relief unto thy servants, driving away passions, loosing sins, and cleansing all diseases, O Birthgiver of God.[^5082]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thou who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupportable weight, establish Thy Church upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, who alone art good and the Lover of mankind.*[^5083]

*Troparion 1:* Thou art the hope of the earth-born, and their help, and joy, protection, and refuge, O Lady, Mother of Life. Therefore we pray thee: Send down thy help unto all who sing unto thee, O Most Pure One.[^5084]

*Troparion 2:* We who are infirm and held fast by grievous temptations, O merciful Creator, God of all, bring unto Thee in prayer Thy most pure Tabernacle, Thy Mother, O Savior: loose the cords of our transgressions.[^5085]

*Glory...*
O thou who alone didst bear the Sustainer, Creator, and Lord of all, while still remaining a virgin: grant salvation from on high, O Bride of God, unto Thy servants who truly glorify thee as Mother of God.[^5086]

*Both now...*
Fill me with living waters, O Lady, who didst pour forth divine water unto the world; and tame by thy divine calm the grievous torrents of my iniquities and the waves of my heart.[^5087]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving Thy divine emptying beforehand, O Christ, Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling: For the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed ones art Thou come.*[^5088]

*Troparion 1:* As one who art higher than all creatures, God gave thee unto those on earth as the cause of mediation with Him, O all-hymned Theotokos.[^5089]

*Troparion 2:* Knowing thee to be the Church of God, O Virgin Mother, we who honor thee earnestly pray: Shut not against thy servants, O Theotokos, the doors of thy mercy.[^5090]

*Glory...*
Knowing Thee, O Mother unwedded, to be in truth the all-adorned garment of God, we who honor Thee ask: Clothe us in the garment of remission.[^5091]

*Both now...*
At thy giving birth, O Most Pure One, the whole world was filled with joy, from the time when Gabriel proclaimed from on high unto thee, O Virgin Mother Mary, the great "Rejoice!"[^5092]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee I rise early and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.*[^5093]

*Troparion 1:* Truly we are chastened, yet not according to the full measure of the debt of our transgressions; but, O all-pure Virgin Mother, turn away all the wrath of thy Son from us.[^5094]

*Troparion 2:* O Pure One, who without wedlock didst bear God, who alone brought forth light out of darkness: earnestly entreat Him to send down divine light unto thy servants.[^5095]

*Glory...*
With the censing of thy prayer, O Pure One, and as the divine bride from Lebanon whom Solomon foretold, make fragrant thy servants, O Mother of the Creator.[^5096]

*Both now...*
Having borne Christ without seed, who is our righteousness and redemption, thou hast set free the nature of our forefather from the curse, O Theotokos.[^5097]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Tame the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art tenderhearted.*[^5098]

*Troparion 1:* O Lady Theotokos, who didst bear the Creator: ask remission for thy servants, and raise us up with strength to hymn thee.[^5099]

*Troparion 2:* Be thou a help unto us thy servants, O pure Lady, who faithfully entreat thee, as thou art merciful; and raise us up with strength to hymn thee.[^5100]

*Glory...*
As thou hast worthily the power to act, O pure Lady, look upon us with thy merciful eye, and bring us, thy servants, up from corruption.[^5101]

*Both now...*
Unceasingly pouring forth streams of compassions unto those who ask, O Good One, rain down also upon me the light of the commandments of thy Son, O All-Immaculate One.[^5102]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*
O All-Hymned Virgin, have mercy on us who flee in faith unto thee who art compassionate, and who ask thy fervent protection; for thou art able to save all, as thou art the good Mother of the God on high, ever embracing us with thy maternal prayers, O Virgin full of divine joy.[^5103]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the children as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!*[^5104]

*Troparion 1:* O unfathomable Wisdom of God, O Christ: show compassion unto Thy servants, for the sake of her who bore Thee, as they unceasingly sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5105]

*Troparion 2:* We entreat Thy goodness, O Lord: heal our wounds, for the sake of her who bore Thee, as with fear we sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5106]

*Glory...*
Look upon us with thy merciful eye, O Mother of God, and deliver thy servants from every affliction, as in faith they sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5107]

*Both now...*
Doing evil, O Lady, we fell away from thee; but we straightway found thy help, O Most Pure One, when we cried: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5108]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto the Son and God, begotten of the Father before the ages, and in the latter times incarnate of the Virgin Mother: ye priests, sing; ye peoples, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages!*[^5109]

*Troparion 1:* O our Lady, Giver of good things: grant unto thy servants healing of passions, that unceasingly we may hymn thee, O Virgin, and supremely exalt thee unto the ages.[^5110]

*Troparion 2:* Having ineffably borne the Deliverer, O Pure One, thou didst unaccountably suckle Him while remaining a virgin: Him do thou entreat for those who hymn thee and glorify thee unto all the ages.[^5111]

*Glory...*
Unto thee, the radiant lampstand of the Deliverer, weaving a most fair choir, we sing: All ye works of the Lord, unceasingly sing the Virgin Mary, and supremely exalt her unto the ages![^5112]

*Both now...*
O pure Ewe-lamb, Virgin Mother and Maiden! Make me pure from carnal passions, that I may be delivered from the snares of the deceiver, as I hymn thee, O Full of divine joy.[^5113]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Rejoice, O Isaiah! The Virgin hath conceived in her womb and borne a Son, Emmanuel, both God and...*[^5114]

*...man, the Orient is His name: magnifying Him, we call the Virgin blessed.*[^5115]

*Troparion 1:* The hymnody indeed is finished, but unceasing is our hope toward Thee, the Creator, O Master Christ, as also is Thy grace; but from both sides grant mighty strength unto Thy servants through the prayers of her who bore Thee.[^5116]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art strength unto the ailing, and near unto the suffering, O Pure One, as the true Mother of Life; therefore, fleeing unto thee, we have found transformation of all sorrowful things, O Lady, and by thy protection we are saved.[^5117]

*Glory...*
Beholding thy divine form in image, O Lady, we gaze upon thee therein as clearly present—wholly hating the madness of the heretics upon the earth—and falling down before it, we receive healing.[^5118]

*Both now...*
As an abyss of healings and an ocean of graces, O Pure One, have we sinners come to know thee. Therefore we pray thee: Deliver from all necessities, O only All-Pure One, those who flee unto thy protection.[^5119]

*It is truly meet; and the rest as usual, and the dismissal.*[^5120]

*On Sunday morning, at the Midnight Office:*
*Canon to the Holy and Life-Creating Trinity (whose acrostic is: "The Fifth Rule to the Thrice-Radiant Light"). The work of Metrophanes.*[^5121]

*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He who crushes battles with a high arm, Christ hath shaken off; but Israel He saved, as they sang a hymn of victory.*[^5122]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Trinity, our God, glory to Thee![^5123]
*Troparion 1:* Hymning the dominion of the unitary and thrice-radiant form, we cry aloud: Enlighten our mind, O almighty God, and raise it up unto Thine ineffable glory, O Master.[^5124]

*Troparion 2:* On high, the intelligible adornments of the angels unceasingly hymn Thee with thrice-holy songs: the Unit numbered in three, and the Trinity of one likeness, supersubstantial, all-powerful.[^5125]

*Glory...*
Grant unto my soul the sweetest, light-producing divine drink of Thy love, O Trinity, light-originating Unity, and divine cleansing compunction, O Master of all creation, plenteous in mercy.[^5126]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
As rain upon the fleece without sound, O Virgin, the divine rain came down from heaven into thy...[^5127]

...womb, and saved all withered human nature, O Most Pure One.[^5128]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thou who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend its unsupportable weight, establish Thy Church upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, who alone art good and the Lover of mankind.*[^5129]

*Troparion 1:* Having devised the intelligible essences, Thou didst establish them as unceasing singers of Thy Godhead, O thrice-radiant God and Creator of all! Yet receive also the supplication and prayer of us who are made of clay and born of earth, as Thou art tenderhearted.[^5130]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou who by nature art insusceptible of any change: unto us who are subject to change and who hymn the unfathomable fountain of Thy goodness, grant forgiveness of transgressions and salvation, as Thou art tenderhearted.[^5131]

*Glory...*
We glorify the Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, in the unchanging form of the Godhead: Thee, the unitary and thrice-shining Lord of all, even as the Prophets and the Apostles were clearly taught by Thee.[^5132]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou didst appear unto Moses in the bush, as the Angel of Great Counsel of the Almighty, foreshadowing Thine Incarnation from the Virgin, O Word of God, whereby Thou hast refashioned us and led us up unto the heavens.[^5133]

*Lord, have mercy (3 times).*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Co-beginningless Word" (Собезначáлное Сло́во):*
Thou art merciful, O indivisible Trinity; for Thou hast mercy upon all, as Thou art all-powerful, all-bountiful, compassionate, and plenteous in mercy. Therefore we flee unto Thee who are weighed down by many sins, crying aloud: Cleanse Thy servants, and deliver all from every torment.[^5134]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
O All-Holy Virgin, have mercy on us who in faith flee unto thee who art tenderhearted, and who now ask thy fervent protection; for thou art able, as thou art good, to save all, being the Mother of the God on high, ever employing thy maternal prayers, O Virgin full of God's grace.[^5135]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving Thy divine emptying beforehand, O Christ, Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling: For the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed ones art Thou come.*[^5136]

*Troparion 1:* Mystically was Daniel instructed in the thrice-radiant unity of the one Lordship, beholding Christ the Judge going unto the Father, and the vision revealing the Spirit.[^5137]

*Troparion 2:* Account us who hymn Thee with clay lips, O supersubstantial God, threefold in Hypostases yet unitary in nature, worthy of angelic glory.[^5138]

*Glory...*
One authority and one Lordship in three properties we indivisibly glorify: O Father, and Son, and Spirit, enlighten us Thy servants.[^5139]

*Theotokion:*
The dense and overshadowed mountain, which Habakkuk foresaw of old, out of which the Holy One came forth, manifested the unsearchable birth of thy conception, O Virgin.[^5140]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee I rise early and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.*[^5141]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou who out of goodness didst create man and fashion him according to Thine image: dwell in me, O my thrice-radiant God, as Thou art good and tenderhearted.[^5142]

*Troparion 2:* Do Thou guide me, O thrice-sunned Unity, unto the divine paths of salvation, and fill me with Thy radiance, as by nature Thou art God of unmeasured power.[^5143]

*Glory...*
O indivisible Light of one nature, divided in personal characters, thrice-shining and never-setting: enlighten my heart with Thy rays.[^5144]

*Theotokion:*
When the Prophet beheld thee of old, O pure and all-immaculate one, as the gate looking toward the never-setting Light, he straightway recognized thee as the dwelling-place of God.[^5145]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Tame the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art tenderhearted.*[^5146]

*Troparion 1:* O thrice-radiant Godhead existing hypostatically, Thou art unitary, as Thou art of one likeness and equal in operation, both in essence and in will.[^5147]

*Troparion 2:* Sufficiently did the prophet declare: In Thy light, O Father, we shall see by the Spirit the light of the Son, the one thrice-sunned God.[^5148]

*Glory...*
An uncompounded Essence in three properties, possessing one authority and...[^5149]

...dominion: for thereby all creation is sustained and renewed.[^5150]

*Theotokion:*
Deliverance from transgressions and from perils, O Master God, unitary and thrice-radiant, send down unto Thy singers, through the prayers of the Mother of God.[^5151]

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Co-beginningless Word" (Собезначáлное Сло́во):*
Let us glorify the thrice-sunned Light, and now worship the simple Trinity, which hath enlightened and had mercy on us, and delivered the whole human race from corruption, freeing the entire world from the delusion of idols, and granting unto us the Kingdom.[^5152]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
At a loss before all things, I flee unto thee, the hope of all and refuge of the sinful and humble, crying aloud: I have sinned, and in the evils of insensibility I abide, wretch that I am; have mercy on me before the end, turn me back, and deliver me from every torment, unworthy though I be.[^5153]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the children as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!*[^5154]

*Troparion 1:* As Thou hast an abyss of mercy, O Lord, and an unsearchable ocean of compassions, have mercy on those who sing of Thee alone, the thrice-radiant God of all.[^5155]

*Troparion 2:* Hymning Thee as the incomprehensible, unitary, and thrice-radiant God and Lord, we cry aloud unto Thee: Grant unto Thy servants cleansing of sins.[^5156]

*Glory...*
Equally honoring the Hypostases in one dominion, we indivisibly distinguish the essence of God the Father, and the Son, and the Most Holy Spirit.[^5157]

*Theotokion:*
Thou didst bud forth a branch co-beginningless with the Father, the Flower of the Godhead, an offspring co-everlasting, O Virgin, which gives life unto all men.[^5158]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the children in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages!*[^5159]

*Troparion 1:* That Thou mightest of old clearly reveal the threefold Hypostasis of the one Lordship, Thou didst appear, O my God, in the form of men unto Abraham, who sang of Thy unitary dominion...[^5160]

*Troparion 2:* Account me worthy, O inaccessible Light, bountiful Father, and Word, and Spirit, to gaze upon Thy beneficent rays, that I may ever well please Thee, O Lord of all.[^5161]

*Glory...*
Holy is God the Father, eternal; holy also is the Son, begotten of the Father; holy also is the Life-creating Spirit, proceeding from the Father, and manifested through the Son.[^5162]

*Theotokion:*
Thou hast shone forth upon us, O All-Hymned One, Christ the Lord, One of the thrice-sunned glory, mystically instructing all to sing unto the one Godhead in three Persons unto the ages.[^5163]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Rejoice, O Isaiah! The Virgin hath conceived in her womb and borne a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, the Orient is His name: magnifying Him, we call the Virgin blessed.*[^5164]

*Troparion 1:* Human utterances cannot worthily hymn Thee, O beginningless Unity; nevertheless, daring as far as is possible from faith, O God-originating and enthroned Trinity, we offer glory and praise unto Thy dominion.[^5165]

*Troparion 2:* With equal glory do the Cherubim and Seraphim praise Thee, the monarchical, thrice-radiant God, with most pure lips; with them receive also us sinners, O Lord, who magnify Thy dominion.[^5166]

*Glory...*
Isaiah beheld Thee upon the Cherubic throne, and the Seraphim standing round about Thee, covering their faces with their wings, and crying aloud: Holy, Holy, Holy, O Thrice-Holy God, glorified in three properties![^5167]

*Theotokion:*
As a pure and immaculate virgin, thou didst bear a Son, who delivers us from temptations, God immutable; but entreat Him even now to grant us remission of transgressions.[^5168]

*Then the Trinity Hymns of Gregory of Sinai: It is truly meet; and the rest of the Midnight Office.*[^5169]

*At Matins:*
*After the Six Psalms: God is the Lord, and hath appeared unto us:*
*we sing in Tone 5 the Resurrection Troparion: "The Co-beginningless Word with the Father" (twice). Glory... To the Saint from the Menaion; Both now... Resurrection Theotokion of the same Tone as the Troparion of the Saint. Then the customary reading of the Psalter.*[^5170]

*After the First Reading of the Psalter, Resurrection Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*
Let us praise the Cross of the Lord, let us honor His holy burial with hymns, and let us supremely glorify His Resurrection; for as God He raised the dead together with Himself from the tombs, having despoiled the dominion of death and the might of the devil; and upon those in hades He made light to dawn...[^5171]

...and upon those in hades He made light to dawn.

*Verse:* Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.[^5172]
O Lord, after Thy third-day Resurrection and the adoration of the Apostles, Peter cried aloud unto Thee: The women took boldness, but I was afraid; the thief theologized, but I denied Thee. Wilt Thou then still call me to be a disciple, or wilt Thou show me once again to be a fisher of the deep? But receive me in repentance, O God, and save me.[^5173]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Dread is the wonder of thy conceiving, and ineffable the manner of thy giving birth, made known in thee, O pure Ever-Virgin! Thy glory, O Theotokos, spread abroad unto all for the salvation of our souls, astonishes my mind and confounds my thought.[^5174]

*After the Second Reading of the Psalter, Resurrection Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*
O Lord, Thou wast called dead, who didst put death to death; Thou wast laid in a tomb, who didst empty the tombs; above, soldiers guarded the tomb; below, Thou didst raise up the dead from the ages. O all-powerful and unsearchable Lord, glory to Thee![^5175]

*Verse:* I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works.[^5176]
O Lord, in the midst of the condemned the lawless nailed Thee, and with a spear they pierced Thy side, O Merciful One! For Thou didst accept burial, who didst break down the gates of hades, and didst rise on the third day. The women ran to see Thee, and proclaimed the Resurrection unto the Apostles. O supremely exalted Savior, whom the angels hymn: O blessed Lord, glory to Thee![^5177]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Rejoice, holy mountain traversed by God! Rejoice, living and unburnt bush! Rejoice, only bridge of the world unto God, leading the dead unto eternal life! Rejoice, incorrupt Maiden, who without knowing man didst bear the salvation of our souls![^5178]

*Then: Blessed are the undefiled; after this, the Troparia: The assembly of the angels; Little Ektenia.*[^5179]

*Hypakoe, Tone 5:*
Perplexed in their minds by the angelic vision, and enlightened in their souls by the divine Resurrection, the Myrrh-bearing Women proclaimed good tidings unto the Apostles: Announce ye among the nations the Resurrection, for the Lord works together with you by wonders, granting unto us great mercy.[^5180]

*Hymns of Degrees, Tone 5 (the verses of which we repeat as we sing):*
*First Antiphon:*
When I am in affliction, David-like I sing unto Thee, O my Savior: Deliver my soul from a deceitful tongue.[^5181]

Blessed is the life of those in the wilderness, winged as they are with divine love.[^5182]

*Glory...*
By the Holy Spirit all things are sustained, both visible and invisible; for being self-ruling, He is truly One of the Trinity.[^5183]

*Both now... the same.*[^5184]

*Second Antiphon:*
Let us lift ourselves up unto the mountains, O my soul; go thither, whence help comes.[^5185]

May Thy right hand, touching me also, O Christ, preserve me from all deceit.[^5186]

*Glory...*
Theologizing unto the Holy Spirit, let us say: Thou art God, life, yearning, light, mind; Thou art goodness, Thou reignest unto the ages.[^5187]

*Both now... the same.*[^5188]

*Third Antiphon:*
Concerning those who said unto me: We will go into the courts of the Lord: being filled with great joy, I send up prayers.[^5189]

In the house of David dread things are accomplished; for fire is there, burning every shameful mind.[^5190]

*Glory...*
Unto the Holy Spirit belongs the life-originating dignity, from whom every living creature is animated, as in the Father, and together with the Word.[^5191]

*Both now... the same.*[^5192]

*Prokeimenon, Tone 5:*
Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up, for Thou reignest unto the ages.
*Verse:* I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart:
*Let every breath praise the Lord. Resurrection Gospel. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ. Psalm 50, and the rest in order.*[^5193]

*Resurrection Canon, Tone 5.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He who crushes battles with a high arm, Christ hath shaken off; but Israel He saved, as they sang a hymn of victory.*[^5194]

*Refrain:* Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord![^5195]

*Troparion 1:* The thorn-bearing assembly of the Hebrews, O Benefactor, not having kept motherly love toward Thee, crowned Thee, O Christ, who didst loose the primal ancestor from the condemnation of thorns.[^5196]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst raise me up who fell into the pit, bowing down, O sinless Life-giver; and having without experience of sin endured my foul corruption, O Christ, Thou hast made me fragrant with the myrrh of Thy divine essence.[^5197]

*Theotokion:*
The curse is loosed, sorrow hath ceased; for the blessed and full of grace hath dawned joy upon the faithful, bearing Christ the light-bearing blessing unto all the ends of the earth.[^5198]

*Second Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection, Tone 5.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Unto God the Savior, who guided the people in the sea with dry feet, and drowned Pharaoh with his whole host: unto Him alone let us sing, for He is glorified.*[^5199]

*Troparion 1:* Unto Him who willingly was nailed in the flesh upon the Cross, and who by the Tree delivered him who fell through the ancient sentence: unto Him alone let us sing, for He is glorified.[^5200]

*Troparion 2:* Unto Christ who rose from the tomb as a corpse, and raised up with Himself him who fell, and adorned him with sitting at the right hand of the Father: unto Him alone let us sing, for He is glorified.[^5201]

*Theotokion:*
O most pure Mother of God: unceasingly pray unto God, who was incarnate of thee without being separated from the bosom of the Father, to save from every affliction those whom He created.[^5202]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos (whose acrostic is: "Having born the Light, enlighten me, O Virgin").*
*Same Tone. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Horse and rider into the Red Sea:*
*Troparion 1:* Entreat Christ, who dwelt within thee, O Most Pure One, and who enlightened the world with the rays of His Godhead, to enlighten all who hymn thee, O Virgin Mother.[^5203]

*Troparion 2:* As thou art adorned with the beauty of virtues, O Full of Grace, by the ray of the Spirit thou hast bestowed the beauty-working splendor of Him who adorned all things, O All-Pure One.[^5204]

*Troparion 3:* The bush on Sinai that prefigured thee of old was not burned, O Virgin, when joined with fire; for thou didst bear as a virgin, and didst remain a virgin, beyond understanding, O Virgin Mother.[^5205]

*Katavasia: I shall open my mouth, and they shall be filled with the Spirit:*[^5206]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who by Thy command didst establish the earth upon nothing, and didst suspend what is gravitationally unsupported: establish Thy Church upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, who alone art good and the Lover of mankind.*[^5207]

*Troparion 1:* Gall indeed did they bring unto Thee, O Christ, who in the wilderness wrought wonders for them, they who sucked honey out of the rock; and vinegar instead of manna did the ungrateful children of Israel return unto Thee in recompense.[^5208]

*Troparion 2:* They who of old were sheltered by the radiant cloud laid the Life, Christ, in a tomb; but having risen by Thine own sovereign power, Thou didst bestow upon all the faithful the mystical overshadowing brilliance of the Spirit from on high.[^5209]

*Theotokion:*
Thou, O Mother of God, didst without wedlock bear Him who shone forth from the incorrupt Father without maternal travails; therefore we orthodoxly proclaim thee to be the Theotokos, for thou didst bear the incarnate Word.[^5210]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*By the power of Thy Cross, O Christ, establish my mind to sing and glorify Thy saving Resurrection.*[^5211]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst rise from the tomb, O Christ, delivering from mortal corruption those who hymn Thy voluntary crucifixion, O Life-giver.[^5212]

*Troparion 2:* The Myrrh-bearing women hastened to anoint Thy body with myrrh, O Christ, and finding it not, they returned, singing of Thy rising.[^5213]

*Theotokion:*
Entreat unceasingly, O Pure One, Him who was incarnate from thy womb, to deliver from the deceit of the devil those who hymn thee as the pure Virgin.[^5214]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who didst establish the earth upon nothing:*

*Troparion 1:* As the ladder whereby the Most High came down to us to restore corrupted nature, thou wast clearly seen by all, O pure one; for through thee the All-Good One was well-pleased to converse with the world.[^5215]

*Troparion 2:* The mystery preordained of old, O Virgin, and foreseen before the ages by God who knows all things, having reached its fulfillment in these latter years within thy womb, O All-Immaculate One, hath been made manifest.[^5216]

*Troparion 3:* The condemnation of the ancient curse hath been loosed through thy mediation, O all-pure Virgin! For from thee the Lord hath appeared unto all...[^5217]

...and as the All-Good One hath poured forth blessing, O thou sole adornment of mankind.[^5218]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk cried out with trembling unto Thee, O Christ, prophetically: Thou hast come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.*[^5219]

*Troparion 1:* Prefiguring Thy most pure Cross, O Good One, which mortifis the sinful tasting, Thou didst sweeten with wood the waters of Marah, which were most bitter.[^5220]

*Troparion 2:* The Cross instead of the tree of knowledge, and gall instead of sweet food, didst Thou accept, O my Savior; and instead of the corruption of death, Thou didst pour out Thy divine blood.[^5221]

*Theotokion:*
Without wedlock thou didst conceive incorruptly in thy womb, and without pain didst thou give birth; and after giving birth thou wast preserved a virgin, having given birth to God in the flesh.[^5222]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*I heard the report of the power of the Cross, that paradise was opened thereby, and I cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^5223]

*Troparion 1:* When the Cross was planted upon the Place of the Skull, the bars and the eternal gatekeepers were shattered, and they cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^5224]

*Troparion 2:* When the Savior descended unto those bound as one dead, those dead from the ages rose with Him, and cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^5225]

*Theotokion:*
A virgin gave birth, and knew not maternal things; yet a mother indeed she is, and a virgin she remained: hymning her, we cry aloud: Rejoice, O Theotokos![^5226]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving Thy divine emptying:*

*Troparion 1:* With heart and mind, with soul and lips confessing thee in all piety to be truly the Theotokos, O Pure One, I embrace the fruit of salvation and am saved, O Virgin, by thy prayers.[^5227]

*Troparion 2:* He who created all things out of non-existence was well-pleased, as Benefactor, to be created from thee, O Pure One, for the salvation of those who sing to thee in faith and love, O All-Immaculate One.[^5228]

*Troparion 3:* The transcendent choirs sing of thy childbearing, O All-Immaculate One, rejoicing in the salvation of those who understand thee to be truly...[^5229]

...the Theotokos, O undefiled Virgin.[^5230]

*Troparion 4:* Isaiah called thee the rod from which budded forth for us the fair blossom, Christ God, for the salvation of those who in faith and love flee unto thy protection.[^5231]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee I rise early, and unto Thee I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou alone art tenderhearted.*[^5232]

*Troparion 1:* The Lord of glory hangs voluntarily dishallowed in inglorious appearance upon the Tree, ineffably providing divine glory for me.[^5233]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast clothed me in incorruption, O Christ, having incorruptibly tasted mortal corruption in the flesh, and shone forth from the tomb on the third day.[^5234]

*Theotokion:*
Having given birth without seed unto Christ, our righteousness and redemption, thou hast set free the nature of our forefather from the curse, O Theotokos.[^5235]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Rising early, we cry unto Thee, O Lord: Save us, for Thou art our God, and we know none other beside Thee.*[^5236]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stretch out Thy palms upon the Tree, O our Savior, calling all unto Thyself, as the Lover of mankind.[^5237]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst despoil hades by Thy burial, O my Savior, and by Thy Resurrection Thou didst fill all things with joy.[^5238]

*Troparion 3:* Having risen from the tomb on the third day, O Life-giver, Thou didst pour forth imperishable immortality unto all.[^5239]

*Theotokion:*
We hymn thee as Virgin after childbirth, O Theotokos: for thou didst bear God the Word in the flesh unto the world.[^5240]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*O Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment:*

*Troparion 1:* All the prophets clearly foreannounced thee who wast to be the Mother of God, O pure Theotokos; for thou alone wast found pure and utterly immaculate.[^5241]

*Troparion 2:* We know thee to be the radiant cloud of living water, O Pure One, who didst rain down Christ, the shower of incorruption, upon us who were in despair.[^5242]

*Troparion 3:* Knowing thee to be all-fair and immaculate, sealed with virginity, God who made His abode within thee loved thee purely, as He alone is tenderhearted.[^5243]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Calm the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art tenderhearted.*[^5244]

*Troparion 1:* Having slipped into corruption, O Master Christ, by tasting of the forbidden food, the forefather was led up unto life by Thy passion.[^5245]

*Troparion 2:* As the Life, Thou didst descend unto hades, O Master Christ, and unto him who was corrupted by corruption Thou didst pour forth resurrection.[^5246]

*Theotokion:*
A virgin gave birth, and having given birth remained pure, holding upon her arm Him who upholds all things, as truly having carried Him as Virgin-Mother.[^5247]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The abyss surrounded me, the sea-monster became my tomb; but I cried aloud unto Thee, the Lover of mankind, and Thy right hand saved me, O Lord.*[^5248]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stretch out Thy palms, gathering together the far-scattered assemblies of Thy nations, O Christ our God, by Thy life-bearing Cross, as the Lover of mankind.[^5249]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst despoil death and shatter the gates of hades; and Adam, having been loosed from bonds, cried out unto Thee: Thy right hand hath saved me, O Lord![^5250]

*Theotokion:*
We worthily glorify thee as the unburnt bush, and mountain, and animate ladder, and heavenly gate, O glorious Mary, the boast of the Orthodox.[^5251]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Calm the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest:*

*Troparion 1:* He who is the Cause of all, and who gave being unto all, in being incarnate according to our nature, had thee as the cause, O all-immaculate Mother of God.[^5252]

*Troparion 2:* We know thee, O All-Immaculate Lady, to pour forth a fountain of soul-nourishing healings unto those who faithfully flee unto thy glorious protection.[^5253]

*Troparion 3:* Thou hast borne for us the Cause of salvation, the Life-giver, who grants eternal redemption unto those who proclaim thee to be truly the Theotokos.[^5254]

*Kontakion, Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*
Unto hades, O my Savior, didst Thou descend, and didst shatter the gates as almighty; the dead as Creator didst Thou raise up with Thyself; and the sting of death didst Thou break, and Adam was delivered from the curse, O Lover of mankind;...[^5255]

...therefore we all cry aloud: Save us, O Lord![^5256]

*Ikos:*
The women, having heard the words of the angel, laid aside their lamentation, being joyful and trembling, for they had seen a wonder. And behold, Christ drew near unto them, saying: Rejoice, be of good cheer, I have overcome the world and loosed the prisoners! Hasten therefore unto the disciples, announcing unto them: I go before you into the city of Galilee to preach. Therefore we all cry unto Thee: Save us, O Lord![^5257]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the children as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God!*[^5258]

*Troparion 1:* Clothed in flesh, as with bait on a hook, by Thy divine power Thou didst draw down the serpent, leading up those who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5259]

*Troparion 2:* He who gave substance to the impenetrable composition of the earth, the Incomprehensible One, is covered in the tomb in the flesh; unto whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5260]

*Theotokion:*
One hypostasis indeed in two natures, O All-Immaculate One, hast thou borne, God incarnate, unto whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5261]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Thou who didst save the hymn-singing children in the fiery furnace: Blessed is the God of our fathers!*[^5262]

*Troparion 1:* He who by the Tree of the Cross loosed the idolatrous delusion: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^5263]

*Troparion 2:* He who rose from the dead and raised up those in hades with Himself: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^5264]

*Troparion 3:* By Thy death, O Christ, Thou didst destroy the dominion of death: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^5265]

*Theotokion:*
He who was born of the Virgin and revealed her as Theotokos: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^5266]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers:*

*Troparion 1:* The Uncircumscribed One, remaining immutable, was united hypostatically unto flesh, as He is tenderhearted, within thee, O Most Holy One, He who alone is the blessed God of our fathers.[^5267]

*Troparion 2:* We sing in unison of thee as the all-immaculate Bride, the Theotokos and Lady, and the throne of thy Creator, unto whom...[^5268]

...we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5269]

*Troparion 3:* Having been cleansed by the Spirit, O Virgin, thou wast the Mother of the King of all, of Him who created thee, unto whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5270]

*Troparion 4:* The Lord hath saved me, O all-pure Mother of God, having clothed Himself with the garment of flesh from thee; unto whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^5271]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the children in the furnace, weaving a universal choir, sang: All ye works, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages!*[^5272]

*Troparion 1:* Concerning the voluntary cup of Thy saving passion, Thou didst pray as though it were involuntary; for Thou bearest two wills according to each of Thy substances, O Christ, unto the ages.[^5273]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy all-creating descent, O Christ, hades was despoiled and vomited forth all those who were slain of old by deceit, who supremely exalt Thee unto all the ages.[^5274]

*Theotokion:*
Thee who beyond understanding didst give birth in a god-man manner by the Word unto the Lord and didst remain a virgin, all of us works bless, O Virgin, and supremely exalt unto all the ages.[^5275]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The Son and God, begotten of the Father before the ages, and in the latter times incarnate of the Virgin Mother: ye priests, sing; ye peoples, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages!*[^5276]

*Troparion 1:* Christ God, who willingly stretched out His palms upon the Cross and shattered the bonds of death: ye priests, sing; ye peoples, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages![^5277]

*Theotokion:*
Thou wast shown to be higher than the Cherubim, O pure Theotokos, having carried in thy womb Him who is borne upon them; whom, together with the Bodiless Ones, we men glorify unto all the ages.[^5278]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto Thee, the Creator of all, in the furnace:*

*Troparion 1:* Now the grief of our forefather hath ceased, since thou hast received joy, O Mother of God. Therefore unceasingly we hymn thee, O Virgin, and supremely exalt thee unto all the ages.[^5279]

*Troparion 2:* The assembly of the Bodiless Powers sings with us of thy incomprehensible childbearing, O Virgin, having joined in one choir with love, and supremely exalting Him unto the ages.[^5280]

*Troparion 3:* The transparent stream of immortality came forth from thee, O Maiden: the Lord of all, washing away defilement from those who sing to thee in faith and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^5281]

*Troparion 4:* Truly we confess thee, O Virgin, to be the divine and light-bearing throne and the tables of grace, as having received the Father's Word, whom we supremely exalt unto all the ages.[^5282]

*Then we sing: "My soul magnifies the Lord," with the refrain: "More honorable than the Cherubim."*[^5283]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Rejoice, O Isaiah! The Virgin hath conceived in her womb and borne a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, the Orient is His name: magnifying Him, we call the Virgin blessed.*[^5284]

*Troparion 1:* Fallen man didst Thou assume, O Master Christ, being wholly united with him from the virginal womb, yet partaking in no sin; and Thou didst set him wholly free from corruption by Thy most pure sufferings.[^5285]

*Troparion 2:* By the God-flowing blood poured out from Thy most pure and life-creating side, O Master Christ, idolatrous sacrifice hath ceased, and the whole earth offers unto Thee the sacrifice of praise.[^5286]

*Theotokion:*
Neither a bodiless God, nor again a mere man, did the pure and immaculate Maiden bring forth; but a perfect man, and truly perfect God, whom we magnify together with the Father and the Spirit.[^5287]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Thee, the Mother of God beyond mind and word, who in time didst ineffably give birth unto Him who is timeless, we the faithful magnify with one accord.*[^5288]

*Troparion 1:* Thee who didst undergo the suffering upon the Cross and by death didst crush the power of hades, we the faithful magnify orthodoxly.[^5289]

*Troparion 2:* Thee who on the third day didst rise from the tomb, despoiled hades, and enlightened the world, we the faithful magnify with one accord.[^5290]

*Theotokion:*
Rejoice, O Theotokos, Mother of Christ God! Entreat Him whom thou didst bear to grant remission of transgressions unto those who sing to thee in faith.[^5291]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Rejoice, O Isaiah! The Virgin hath conceived in her womb:*

*Troparion 1:* From thy pure blood flesh was supernaturally curdled for the Creator of all and only-begotten Son of the Father, not from man, but without seed—rational and animate, O Theotokos Ever-Virgin.[^5292]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast brought to a halt the recurrent, unrestrainable assault of death, having in the flesh truly given birth beyond understanding unto eternal Life; touching which with bitter lips, hades was brought to naught, O most holy Virgin-Mother.[^5293]

*Troparion 3:* Thy Son, sitting upon the sovereign throne, placed thee at His right hand, O Pure One, shining with the light of the golden fringes of divine virtues, rendering honor unto thee as unto His Mother, O All-Immaculate One.[^5294]

*Troparion 4:* Beyond understanding is thy childbearing, O Mother of God! For without man was the conception within thee, and virginal was the birth; for He who was born is God: whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin.[^5295]

*After the Katavasia, the Little Ektenia.*[^5296]
*Then: "Holy is the Lord our God." The Morning Exapostilarion.*[^5297]

*AT THE PRAISES*
*Resurrection Stichera, Tone 5.*[^5298]

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, though the tomb was sealed by the transgressors, Thou didst come forth from the tomb, just as Thou wast born of the Theotokos. Thine incorporeal angels understood not how Thou didst become incarnate; the soldiers guarding Thee perceived not when Thou didst rise: for both things were sealed unto those who would examine them, yet the marvels were revealed unto those who worship the mystery in faith; unto us who hymn it, grant joy and great mercy.[^5299]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, who didst crush the eternal bars and tear asunder the bonds: Thou didst rise from the tomb, leaving behind Thy grave-clothes as a testimony of Thy true three-day burial; and Thou didst go before into Galilee, Thou who wast guarded in the cave. Great is Thy mercy, O incomprehensible Savior! Have mercy on us and save us.[^5300]

*Sticheron 3:* O Lord, the women ran unto the tomb to see Thee, Christ, who didst suffer for our sake; and having arrived, they found an angel sitting upon the stone that had rolled away in fear, and he cried unto them, saying: The Lord is risen! Say unto the disciples that He is risen from the dead, saving our souls.[^5301]

*Sticheron 4:* O Lord, just as Thou didst come forth from the sealed tomb, so didst Thou enter while the doors were shut unto Thy disciples, showing them the bodily sufferings which Thou didst endure, O longsuffering Savior; as being of the seed of David, Thou didst endure wounds; but as Son of God, Thou didst set the world free. Great is Thy mercy, O incomprehensible Savior! Have mercy on us and save us.[^5302]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian:*[^5303]

*Sticheron 5:* O Lord, King of the ages and Creator of all, who for our sake didst accept crucifixion and burial in the flesh, that Thou mightest set us all free from hades: Thou art our God, and beside Thee we know none other.[^5304]

*Sticheron 6:* O Lord, who can recount Thy surpassing marvels, or who shall declare Thy fearful mysteries? For having become man for our sake as Thou Thyself didst will, Thou didst manifest the dominion of Thy power; for by Thy Cross Thou didst open paradise unto the thief, and by Thy burial Thou didst shatter the bars of hades, and by Thy Resurrection Thou didst enrich all things. O tenderhearted Lord, glory to Thee![^5305]

*Verse:* Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor for ever.[^5306]

*Sticheron 7:* The Myrrh-bearing women, having reached Thy tomb very early, sought Thee, to anoint with myrrh the immortal Word and God; and having been instructed by the words of the angel, they returned with joy, to announce clearly unto the Apostles that Thou art risen, O Life of all, and hast granted cleansing and great mercy unto the world.[^5307]

*Verse:* I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvels.[^5308]

*Sticheron 8:* Unto the Jews the guards of the God-receiving tomb said: Oh, your vainly-counselled scheme! Having attempted to guard the Uncircumscribed One, ye labored in vain; desiring to hide the Resurrection of the Crucified One, ye have shown it clearly. Oh, your vainly-counselled council! Why again do ye take counsel to hide that which cannot be hidden? Rather hear from us and be willing to believe the truth of what has happened: the lightning-bearing angel, descending from heaven, rolled away the stone, through fear of whom we were seized with deadness; and calling out unto the stout-hearted Myrrh-bearing women, he said: Do ye not behold the mortification of the guards, the breaking of the seals, and the emptying of hades? Why do ye seek as dead Him who hath brought to naught the victory of hades and crushed the sting of death? Go quickly and proclaim the good tidings of the Resurrection unto the Apostles, crying without fear: Truly the Lord is risen, possessing great mercy![^5309]

*Glory... Morning Evangelical Sticheron.*[^5310]
*Both now... Most blessed art thou...*[^5311]
*Great Doxology. Troparion: "Today salvation is come to the world..." Ectenias and dismissal.*[^5312]

*AT THE LITURGY*
*The Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^5313]

*Troparion 1:* The thief upon the cross, having believed Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom![^5314]

*Troparion 2:* Unto Him who made life bud forth for our race upon the tree of the Cross, and dried up the curse that came from the tree, as Savior and Creator, let us sing in unison.[^5315]

*Troparion 3:* By Thy death, O Christ, Thou didst destroy the power of death, and didst raise up with Thyself those dead from the ages, who sing of Thee as the true God and our Savior.[^5316]

*Troparion 4:* Having come unto Thy tomb, O Christ, the venerable women sought to anoint Thee with myrrh, O Life-giver, and an angel appeared unto them, crying: The Lord is risen![^5317]

*Troparion 5:* When Thou wast crucified, O Christ, in the midst of two condemned thieves, one indeed, blaspheming Thee, was justly condemned; but the other, confessing Thee, made his dwelling in paradise.[^5318]

*Troparion 6:* The utterance of Thine Apostles filled all the earth, O Christ, and drove away the night of godlessness, illuminating all the faithful with the light of understanding.[^5319]

*Glory... Triadikon:*
*Glory...* We worship the Trinity in Unity, unoriginate and equal in honor, and we glorify Thee with the ranks on high, crying in a threefold manner: Holy art Thou, O unoriginate God![^5320]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
*Both now...* Rejoice, O animate temple of God, and gate impassable! Rejoice, O unburnt and fiery throne! Rejoice, O Mother of Emmanuel, Christ our God![^5321]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^5322]

*On Sunday Evening:*[^5323]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 5.*[^5324]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5325]

*Sticheron 1:* I weep and lament bitterly, beholding the fearful retribution of the Word, having from my deeds no answer nor even a slight defense against my guilt, wretched...[^5326]

...wretched as I am! Therefore I pray: before the untimely end overtake me, before the fearful judgment, before I am about to receive condemnation, where the fire is unquenchable, and the outer darkness, where the worm and gnashing of teeth consume those who have sinned; grant unto me deliverance from my transgressions, O my Christ, and great mercy.[^5327]

*Sticheron 2:* Having despised Thy laws and the Scriptures, I, wretched one, have rejected Thy commandments. O my God, my Creator, how indeed shall I escape the torment there? Therefore before mine end, O Savior, grant me forgiveness, bestowing a shower of tears and true compunction, as the most good God, driving far from me the demonic hosts who seek to cast me down into the abyss of hades. Therefore I pray Thee: take not away Thy all-powerful hand from me.[^5328]

*Sticheron 3:* Woe is me! How have I been darkened in mind? How have I departed from Thee, and, wretched one, have slaved for sin, and surrendered my whole passionate self unto carnal pleasure that dwelt in passionate me? And now I await my departure from life and the torment that is to be. O most good Lord! Grant unto me tearful repentance and remission of my countless transgressions, I faithfully pray Thee, who grantest great mercy unto the world.[^5329]

*Other Stichera, to the Bodiless Powers.*[^5330]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5331]

*Sticheron 1:* Honorable initiates of the Godhead, of the thrice-radiant and consubstantial Divinity, who incessantly offer a hymn with bodiless voices and fiery tongues: offer our supplications and prayers which we send up from defiled lips, and ask remission of our transgressions; for He who clothed Himself in our weakness knows it, and, being merciful by nature, He will accept your entreaties made on behalf of servants who have sinned, granting great mercy unto the world.[^5332]

*Sticheron 2:* All ye angelic multitude that draw near unto my Master and stand with fear before His divine throne, and are filled with unutterable light: guide me, who have gone astray in the darkness of sin, unto the saving light, by the command of the compassionate God; drive away the dark and alien gloom of the deceitful demons through your protection and grace; for it does not endure the coming of light, but is driven away.[^5333]

*Sticheron 3:* Radiant angels of God, who stand before the divine throne of grace, receiving true humility and enlightenment from the divine light: look down upon us from the heavens, ye lovers of mankind, who are held in a tempest of grievous things, and...[^5334]

...suffering calamities, and sleeping in darkness. Come ye therefore, O Archangels, unto our help, and deliver us from the snares of the malicious enemy; for unto your protection we all flee, O most praised ones.[^5335]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
A cherubic throne in truth, having become higher than the Angels; for within thee, O Pure Lady, the divine Word made His abode, desiring to renew our image; having come forth from thee as the Flesh-bearer, since He is merciful, He accepted the Cross and passion for our sake, and as God He granted the Resurrection. Therefore, as through thee our condemned nature hath been transformed, giving thanks in faith unto the Creator, we pray that through thy prayers we may obtain forgiveness of transgressions and great mercy.[^5336]

*O Joyful Light: Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*[^5337]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5338]
*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, I cease not from sinning; though vouchsafed Thy love for mankind, I understand it not: overcome my perplexity, O only Good One, and have mercy on me.[^5339]

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the handmaid look unto the hands of her mistress, so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.[^5340]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, I both fear Thy dread and cease not from doing evil. Who at the judgment-seat fears not the judge? Or who, desiring to be healed, provokes the physician to wrath as do I? O long-suffering Lord, have compassion on my weakness, and have mercy on me.[^5341]

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; especially hath our soul been filled; let the reproach be to those that are at ease, and contempt to the proud.[^5342]

*Martyrikon:* Having disregarded all earthly things, and manly dared unto torments, ye missed not the blessed hopes; but ye became heirs of the heavenly kingdom, O most praised Martyrs! Having boldness toward the God who loves mankind, ask peace for the world, and for our souls great mercy.[^5343]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
A fearful and most glorious, and great mystery! He who cannot be contained was contained in a womb, and the Mother remained again a virgin after giving birth; for from her she bare God incarnate. Unto Him let us cry aloud, unto Him let us say a song, chanting with the Angels: Holy art Thou, O Christ...[^5344]

...our God, who for our sake didst become man: glory to Thee![^5345]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion of the Saint; Glory... Both now... Theotokion in the Tone of the Troparion; ectenia and dismissal.*[^5346]

*On Sunday at Small Compline:*[^5347]
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^5348]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^5349]
Horse and rider in the Red Sea, He who crushes wars with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5350]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.[^5351]

*Troparion 1:* A marvel is accomplished in thee, O Virgin, in truth dreadful: that in thy womb thou didst hold Him who is by no means containable, and didst give birth ineffably, remaining ever-virgin.[^5352]

*Troparion 2:* Rain down upon me, O Lady, remission of transgressions, who didst ineffably contain the heavenly rain that came unto thee, and didst remain a Virgin after incomprehensible childbearing.[^5353]

*Glory...* O only Blessed and Joy-endowed one, who didst bear unutterable joy unto men: take away the sorrows of my soul, I pray, and make glad my heart.[^5354]

*Both now...* All desire and the sweetness of life is He who shone forth from thee according to His exceedingly great goodness, O all-immaculate Virgin: pray Him to save me, who incessantly glorify thee.[^5355]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* He who established the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and suspended it without restraint, though weighed down: upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, establish Thy Church, O only Good and Lover of mankind.[^5356]

*Troparion 1:* The things ordained in shadow have passed away; for thou didst bear Christ the Lawgiver, O Most Pure Lady, who established for us the grace of purification and illumination, and delivered us from the curse, O all-immaculate Virgin.[^5357]

*Troparion 2:* From thee, O Pure Lady, was God born, taking flesh, and He who was before invisible became visible in the flesh. Him therefore fervently beseech, O Maiden, to deliver from enemies visible and invisible me who radiantly glorify thee.[^5358]

*Glory...* A grievous wave of passions whelms me around, a deep of evil spirits holds me fast, a tempest of sins troubles my...[^5359]

...heart. O Birth-giver of God, do thou establish me, who radiantly sing of thee.[^5360]

*Both now...* Having the divine Gabriel as marshal of ranks, reverently in faith and unison we bring the "Rejoice!" faithfully unto the unwedded Mother and Theotokos, through whom we are delivered from dangers, afflictions, and diseases.[^5361]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Having perceived Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk cried with trembling unto Thee, O Christ: Thou art come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.[^5362]

*Troparion 1:* O Virgin who didst inwardly weave from thy virginal blood the garment of flesh for Him who arrays heaven with clouds: clothe me with the robe of incorruption, who was of old stripped naked by deceit.[^5363]

*Troparion 2:* In thy womb, O Virgin, the ineffable palace of the King and God was built, in which having dwelt, He made us churches.[^5364]

*Glory...* Have compassion, being compassionate, upon my most wretched soul, O all-immaculate Birth-giver of God, which through passions and sin hath been grievously blackened and benumbed.[^5365]

*Both now...* As the scepter of an incorruptible kingdom hast thou shone forth from the root of Jesse, and without a husband thou barest a Child, O Pure Lady, being daughter of Adam and of David: God, and Creator, and Lord together.[^5366]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Clothed with light as with a garment, unto Thee I rise early, and unto Thee I cry: enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only compassionate One.[^5367]

*Troparion 1:* Remaining an incorrupt virgin after giving birth, thou leadest unto life all men who had corrupted, and illuminest them with incorruption, O All-immaculate.[^5368]

*Troparion 2:* Anew hast thou borne as a babe, O Most Pure Lady, Him who timelessly shone forth from the Father before the ages; pray Him for peace, O Bride of God.[^5369]

*Glory...* May I find thee a helper, O Virgin, snatching me away in the hour of the trial of judgment, when I shall stand before the judgment-seat of Him who was born of thee.[^5370]

*Both now...* "Thou art all fair, my companion, and immaculate from Lebanon," O Virgin Bride of God: by the Holy Spirit was the Incarnation of the Son of God foreknown in thee.[^5371]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, calm the sea of passions, and bring me up from corruption, for Thou art compassionate.[^5372]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing not a man, thou didst bear Emmanuel, O Theotokos, who had compassion on our lowliness; therefore as is ever due, we glorify thee.[^5373]

*Troparion 2:* Beyond mind, O Most Holy Lady, hast thou borne the timeless Creator beyond word, who delivers from every corruption those who sing of thee as Theotokos.[^5374]

*Glory...* Having borne the good Benefactor and Creator, O most holy Lady who lovest good: make blessed my afflicted soul.[^5375]

*Both now...* A new praise and an ancient one we offer thee, O All-pure Lady; for we have nothing better unto praising than "Rejoice!", which with Gabriel we sing to thee.[^5376]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (20 times). Glory... Both now...*[^5377]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*
I now weep for myself before the judgment, considering my wicked and grievous deeds, and the deep of transgressions that holds me from youth and oppresses my mind; but through thine intercession, O Pure Lady, grant me remission, and vouchsafe me to attain salvation.[^5378]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the children as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5379]

*Troparion 1:* Deliver me, O Most Pure Lady, from evil despondency, and from the darkening of passions, and from everlasting condemnation, that in faith I may glorify thee.[^5380]

*Troparion 2:* Slay my passions, O thou that didst bear Life; raise me up, O Bride of God, who lie in the grave of insensibility, that with love I may glorify thee.[^5381]

*Glory...* Encompassed with flesh hast thou borne the bodiless God, O All-immaculate, who delivers us who sing with fear: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5382]

*Both now...* Having found thy pure prayer as a gratuitous healing, O Virgin, we ask singing: transformation for the soul and health for the body.[^5383]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, the Almighty Creator, the youths in the furnace, weaving a universal choir, sang: All ye works, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^5384]

*Troparion 1:* O door of light, open unto me the light-bearing doors of repentance, showing every upright path of righteousness that leads into the portals of the divine will.[^5385]

*Troparion 2:* Anew on earth, O Virgin Mother, hast thou borne as a Child the Son who is co-unoriginate with the Father...[^5386]

...who by His ineffable mercy made Himself like unto us who were corrupted under sin.[^5387]

*Glory...* All-elect, all-adorned, whom God loved, whom He chose, hast thou appeared, O ever-glorified Virgin. Therefore we sing of thee, O Pure Lady, unto all ages.[^5388]

*Both now...* "All pure and filled with divine glory is my companion," said the Spirit of God, foretelling thee, O Most Pure Lady; whom in songs we hymn, singing: It is fitting for thee to rejoice, who barest the God-man.[^5389]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah, a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5390]

*Troparion 1:* Reject not now the sighings of those who flee unto thee, O All-pure Lady, but look upon us who are in afflictions; turn weeping into joy, and compunction into gladness, for us who praise thee in songs.[^5391]

*Troparion 2:* An immovable foundation art thou, O Pure Lady, who didst bear the Savior who established the earth upon the waters by His divine commands; pray Him to establish upon Himself us who radiantly call thee blessed.[^5392]

*Glory...* Beyond mind is thy childbearing, O Theotokos; for without a man was the conception in thee, and virginal was thy childbearing, for God is He who was born; whom magnifying, we call thee blessed who gavest Him birth.[^5393]

*Both now...* Dread art Thou, O Lord, and who indeed shall then withstand Thy threatening, O Christ, only King, when Thou executest judgment? Therefore spare and save me, O Savior, through the acceptable supplications of her who bare Thee.[^5394]

*It is truly meet: and the rest as usual.*[^5395]

*On Monday at Matins:*[^5396]
*After the 1st Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5397]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When the Judge sits, and the Angels stand by, the trumpet sounds, and the flame burns: what wilt thou do, O my soul, being led unto judgment? Therefore before the end cry aloud unto the Judge: O God, cleanse me, and save me.[^5398]

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^5399]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Let us all watch, and meet Christ with abundance of oil and radiant lamps, that we may be accounted worthy of entering the bridal chamber; for he who is found outside the doors shall cry in vain unto God: Have mercy on me.[^5400]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
More holy than the Cherubim, and higher than the Seraphim, O Most Pure Lady: confessing thee in truth as Theotokos, we sinners have an advocate, and find salvation in time of temptations. Therefore cease not to pray for us, O strength and refuge of our souls.[^5401]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5402]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* O soul, the things here are temporal, but the things there are eternal; I behold the judgment-seat, and the Judge upon the throne, and I tremble at giving answer. Therefore turn with haste; the judgment is unsparing.[^5403]

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^5404]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* I lie upon a bed of many transgressions, robbed of the hope of my salvation; for the sleep of my slothfulness procures torment for my soul. But do Thou, O God, born of the Virgin, raise me up unto Thy singing, that I may glorify Thee.[^5405]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^5406]

*Martyrikon:* The memory of the passion-bearers shines today, for it hath a radiance from heaven; the angelic choir keeps festival, and the human race celebrates with them. Therefore they pray unto the Lord that He have mercy on our souls.[^5407]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
With the Angels heavenly things, with men earthly things, in a voice of rejoicing we cry unto thee, O Theotokos: Rejoice, O door of heaven most spacious! Rejoice, O only salvation of the earth-born! Rejoice, O pure one, endowed with grace, who didst bear God incarnate![^5408]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5409]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезна́чальное Сло́во):*[^5410]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* I tremble at the most dread judgment-seat, and I fear the just sentence of the Judge; for my conscience ever reprovs me; and living in negligence, I am perplexed and held fast with fear. By your holy entreaties deliver me from the fire, O Archangels, who are the guardians of the faithful.[^5411]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Me who am bowed down by irrational passions, and have prodigally spent my life...[^5412]

...call back, O Savior, as the prodigal, and receive me, and spread out for me the Father's embrace through the mercy of Thy compassions, and by the prayers of the Bodiless Powers vouchsafe me mine ancient portion.[^5413]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Fervent and invincible Protectress, certain and unashamed hope, wall and shelter and haven of those who flee unto thee, O pure Ever-Virgin: pray thy Son and God, with the Angels, to give peace to the world, and salvation, and great mercy.[^5414]

*Canon.*[^5415]
*(Whose acrostic is: Have compassion, O Word, on him who hath sinned much against Thee). A creation of kyr Joseph.*[^5416]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^5417]
Let us sing unto the Lord, who wrought marvelous wonders in the Red Sea, a song of victory: for He is glorified.[^5418]

*Refrain:* Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.[^5419]

*Troparion 1:* Dragged and enticed, O Christ, by many deceits of the stranger: turn me back and have compassion on me, as Thou art all-powerful.[^5420]

*Troparion 2:* O Christ who didst open the ears of the deaf: open, I pray, the deafened ears of my soul, that I may give ear unto Thy words.[^5421]

*Martyrikon 1:* Being newly-revealed stars of Christ the Sun of righteousness, O Martyrs: drive away the darkness from our hearts.[^5422]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having appeared as fiery darts kindled by the divine coals of the Holy Spirit, the passion-bearers shatter all the arrows of the serpent.[^5423]

*Theotokion:* O door of divine glory, open unto me the doors of repentance, and snatch my humble soul away from the gates of hades, I pray.[^5424]

*Second Canon, to the Bodiless Powers.*[^5425]
*(Whose acrostic is: A fifth song to the Angels). A creation of Theophanes.*[^5426]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^5427]
Horse and rider in the Red Sea, He who crushes wars with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5428]

*Refrain:* Holy Archangels and Angels, pray God for us.[^5429]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Initiates of the light-giving Godhead, and shining with primary radiance from His beams: entreat the Master, O Angels, to enlighten my soul with light.[^5430]

*Troparion 2:* Having boldness as ye all stand before the exalted throne: deliver from dangers those who piously sing of you, O protectors of the heavenly rank, O Chief Commanders.[^5431]

*Theotokion:* The curse is dissolved, and sorrow hath ceased; for the Blessed...[^5432]

...and Grace-endowed one hath shone forth joy unto the faithful, blossoming Christ, the blessing for all ends of the earth.[^5433]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* God is King over the nations; God sits upon His holy throne; and let us sing unto Him with understanding as to King and God.[^5434]

*Troparion 1:* Thou who willest not that anyone should perish, O good Lord: have compassion on me who am perishing, and save me by the nod of Thy mercy, O All-compassionate One.[^5435]

*Troparion 2:* Whatsoever I have sinned knowingly and unknowingly, unto Thee, O Christ Lord who knowest all things, I come and fall down before Thee: receive me as the prodigal.[^5436]

*Martyrikon 1:* That ye might put to death dwelling sin, O Martyrs, and show the enemy dead, ye disregarded bodily death, O blessed ones.[^5437]

*Martyrikon 2:* Diversified by sufferings, O Martyrs, and adorned with garments dyed in blood, ye stand as crown-bearers before the King of all.[^5438]

*Theotokion:* O Virgin Mother, who didst give flesh to God: receive the voices of those who ever cry unto thee, and deliver us from diverse circumstances.[^5439]

*Second Canon, to the Bodiless Powers.*
