*Ode 3. Heirmos:* He who established the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and suspended it without restraint, though weighed down: upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, establish Thy Church, O only Good and Lover of mankind.[^5440]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Thou who by a word didst most wisely establish the choirs for the celestial powers, demonstrating Thy goodness of boundless might: through their intercessions establish Thy Church, O only Good and Lover of mankind.[^5441]

*Troparion 2:* Adorning the Angels with unutterable brightness, O Christ, and through them establishing Thy Church, O Merciful One: through them enlighten my wretched soul, I pray Thee, O Master, not remembering my countless sins.[^5442]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast unwedded Mother of God who enlightens the bodiless choirs to sing incessantly the one Godhead in three Hierarchies and Lordships, O most pure and all-praised Virgin.[^5443]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* The works of Thy dispensation, O Lord, struck terror into the prophet Habakkuk; for Thou didst go forth for the salvation of Thy people; Thou art come to save Thine anointed.[^5444]

*Troparion 1:* The deeds which I have committed in life are wicked and grievous; from them, O Christ my God, deliver me, granting me sincere repentance.[^5445]

*Troparion 2:* Every honorable commandment have I despised, Thy fear have I cast away, O Christ, and I dread Thine unavoidable judgment-seat, whereon do not condemn me, O Compassionate One.[^5446]

*Martyrikon 1:* Stretched with thongs from every side in truth, and exceedingly exhausted with wounds, and scraped with iron claws, O Word, Thy passion-bearers rejoiced in faith.[^5447]

*Martyrikon 2:* In no wise was the evil one able to shake you, O Passion-bearers, from your divine stance; therefore unto the many who are shaken, O noble ones, ye appeared as a divine steadfastness.[^5448]

*Theotokion:* The Lord takes flesh from thy pure blood, granting repentance unto those who honor thee, O All-praised Lady, through thy good intercession, since He is compassionate and the only Lover of mankind.[^5449]

*Second Canon, to the Bodiless Powers.*
*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Having perceived Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk cried with trembling unto Thee, O Christ: Thou art come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.[^5450]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Thou hast established the angelic brightnesses, graciously illuminating them with deifying beams, as mighty in strength, fulfilling Thy word, O Lover of mankind.[^5451]

*Troparion 2:* Let us, the faithful, lay aside earthly wisdom of the flesh, conforming ourselves unto the life of the Bodiless Ranks, and let us give wings unto our mind in the Spirit.[^5452]

*Theotokion:* Be thou a protectress unto me, O All-immaculate, a refuge and haven, driving away the tempest of passions, who didst surpass the angelic choirs in beauty beyond compare.[^5453]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O true Light, Christ God, unto Thee my spirit rises early from the night: show Thy face upon me.[^5454]

*Troparion 1:* Awake, O soul, awake from heavy sleep, from my grievous sin, and be illuminated by the light of repentance.[^5455]

*Troparion 2:* Let us renew our soul with diligence, and let us be watered with the showers of compunction, that we may sprout an ear of corn of repentance.[^5456]

*Martyrikon 1:* Having been sharpened by the coals of love, un-forged swords did ye appear, O Passion-bearers, cutting down the ranks of the enemies.[^5457]
*Martyrikon 2:* Ye were not enslaved by the enemy, but rather enslaved him, O Passion-bearers, and were shown to be close friends unto Christ.[^5458]
*Theotokion:* O Virgin, blessed among women, grant thy mercies unto thy people; for thou hast been shown to be the Mother of the Merciful One.[^5459]
*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Covering Thyself with light as with a garment, I awake early unto Thee and cry to Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Compassionate One.[^5460]
*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Going about all the ends of the earth, ye bring and preserve the Master's benefactions unto the faithful, O all-glorious Archangels.[^5461]
*Troparion 2:* Submitting unto Thy word, O Word of God and Father, the glorious adornments of the heavenly ranks are illuminated by the light of Thy splendor.[^5462]
*Theotokion:* Present all my desire unto Thyself, O pure one who beyond words didst bear the sweetness of desire, unto those who know thee as the Theotokos.[^5463]
*Ode 6. Heirmos:* From the whale Thou didst deliver the Prophet; bring me also up from the depth of sins, O Lord, and save me.[^5464]
*Troparion 1:* There is no sin in this life that I alone have not committed, wretch that I am; O only Sinless One, have compassion on me.[^5465]
*Troparion 2:* Let us take wing with the sail of zeal, and let us all anticipate through repentance unto the haven of salvation, that we may be saved.[^5466]
*Martyrikon 1:* By the suffering of preachers, Apostles, and Martyrs the creation is illuminated; by them also enlighten us, O Lover of mankind.[^5467]
*Martyrikon 2:* Let us all honor the Martyrs, vessels that contained the divine ray and venerable light-pourings.[^5468]
*Theotokion:* O Virgin, protectress of the faithful, pray the Lord that thy servants may be delivered from every sin.[^5469]
*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, tame the sea of passions, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.[^5470]
*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Holily adorned, the chosen angelic assemblies are enlightened by light-giving...[^5471]

...radiances, manifestly perfected by the divine-working illumination.[^5472]
*Troparion 2:* Richly adorned with thrice-radiant rays, O Angels and Archangels, enlighten my wretched soul in godly form by your prayers.[^5473]
*Theotokion:* Thou didst conceive, O Most-pure One, the Creator and God of all, Whom the Angels, standing before Him in all reverence, behold rejoicing with trembling.[^5474]
*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Blessed art Thou, O God, who beholdest the abysses and sittest upon the throne of glory, praised and all-glorious.[^5475]
*Troparion 1:* Blessed art Thou, O God, who according to Thy compassion receivest all who repent, praised and all-glorious.[^5476]
*Troparion 2:* Heal my many passions, O compassionate Christ, Thou who knowest my weakness, praised and all-glorious.[^5477]
*Martyrikon 1:* Strengthened by divine power, the choir of passion-bearers overcame the enemies, crying: Praised and exalted above all unto the ages.[^5478]
*Martyrikon 2:* O Word, who didst strengthen the all-wise passion-bearers to endure many-woven torments, by their prayers have compassion upon all.[^5479]
*Theotokion:* Blessed art Thou, O God, who having dwelt in a virginal womb didst save man, praised and exalted above all unto the ages.[^5480]
*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the youths, who sang in unison: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5481]
*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Manifesting His immeasurably mighty power, Christ appointed you Archistrategoi and taught you to sing: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5482]
*Troparion 2:* O Thou who adornest the innumerable multitude of the incorporeal ranks with goodness, vouchsafe the assemblies of the faithful to sing unto Thee: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5483]
*Theotokion:* O Virgin, who didst pour forth passionlessness unto all the faithful, now establish me who am shaken by passions, as I sing in faith: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5484]
*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The Creator of creation, at Whom the Angels tremble, hymn, O ye people, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^5485]
*Troparion 1:* Having become dead through transgressions, make me alive, O Lord, that I may glorify Thee unto all ages.[^5486]

*Troparion 2:* Having enlightened me by repentance, deliver me from the darkness of sin, O Lord, that I may glorify Thee unto all ages.[^5487]
*Martyrikon 1:* Passion-bearers, Martyrs, ye trampled upon the flame of deception, having most gloriously received dew from heaven.[^5488]
*Martyrikon 2:* Like fertile soil, O holy ones, ye truly bore fruit a hundredfold unto Christ the Arbiter of the contest.[^5489]
*Theotokion:* From thee did God shine forth, O all-hymned Virgin, and by divine knowledge He enlightened the darkened.[^5490]
*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, hymn the Lord and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^5491]
*Troparion 1 (Twice):* The angelic assemblies now draw me to sing with hymns and heartfelt desire, with whom I sing: All ye works, hymn the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^5492]
*Troparion 2:* Ye ministers of the most holy and thrice-solar ray, pray that they may be saved who sing in faith: Hymn the works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^5493]
*Theotokion:* O Gate of light, Virgin Mother Maiden, illuminate with thy light those who sing in faith: Hymn the works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^5494]
*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee, who art blessed among women and blessed by God, the human race magnifies in hymns.[^5495]
*Troparion 1:* Be merciful unto me, O Lord, who have sinned foolishly beyond number, and vouchsafe me Thy Kingdom, O Word.[^5496]
*Troparion 2:* Even as Thou didst save the Ninevites of old when they repented, O only Savior, so also save us who hymn Thee, by Thy mercy.[^5497]
*Martyrikon 1:* Having surrendered your flesh unto all manner of wounds, ye preserved your soul unvanquished, O Passion-bearers of the Lord, partakers of divine glory.[^5498]
*Martyrikon 2:* Day-shining stars, illuminating the souls of all upon earth, did ye appear, O passion-bearers of the Lord of all.[^5499]
*Theotokion:* Like a fiery throne thou bearest Him who upholds all things by His nod, and with thy breasts thou nourishest, O Virgin, Him who nourishes all.[^5500]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah, the Virgin held in womb and bore a Son, Emmanuel, God and man, Dayspring is His name; Whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5501]
*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Having been leaders of the angelic orders, O most radiant Michael, and thou, Gabriel, who wast the all-true preacher of the divine Incarnation: preserve all who sing of you, O glorious ones.[^5502]
*Troparion 2:* O Thou who out of rich giftings overflowest Thy treasures, Thou hast brought forth the angelic ranks; coming with them as Judge and King of all, save me, O Master, who flee unto Thy mercy.[^5503]
*Theotokion:* Intelligences, Archangels, Authorities, Thrones, Cherubim, Powers, and Seraphim, radiant Angels, Principalities, and Dominions, with trembling serve Thy Son, O all-immaculate, pure, all-blessed Theotokos.[^5504]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5505]
*Sticheron 1:* Overlook the multitudes of my transgressions, O Lord born of the Virgin, and cleanse all my sins, granting me thoughts of conversion, as the only Lover of mankind, I pray, and have mercy on me.[^5506]
*Verse:* We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad. In all our days let us be glad for the days wherein Thou didst humble us, for the years wherein we saw evils. And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.[^5507]
*Sticheron 2:* Woe is me! Unto whom have I been likened? Unto the barren fig tree; and I fear the curse together with the cutting down. But O heavenly Husbandman, Christ God, show my dried-up soul to be fruitful, and receive me as the prodigal son, and have mercy on me.[^5508]
*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and prosper Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou prosper.[^5509]
*Martyrikon:* Blessed is the host of the heavenly King: for although the passion-bearers were earth-born, yet they hastened to attain unto the angelic dignity; they took no heed of their bodies, and through sufferings were vouchsafed the honor of the Incorporeal Ones: therefore, by their prayers, O Lord, send down upon us great mercy.[^5510]
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O Joy-endowed one, intercede by thy prayers, and entreat...[^5511]

...for our souls a multitude of compassions, and cleansing of many transgressions, we pray.[^5512]
*It is good to give praise: Trisagion, and after Our Father: Troparion of the Saint, if there be one; if not, we say the Troparion of the day. Then the Ectenia and the First Hour, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^5513]
*On Monday, The Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^5514]
*Troparion 1:* The thief on the cross having believed Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom.[^5515]
*Troparion 2:* Soul-corrupting robbers, having met me on the path of this life, have wounded me; but unto Thy compassion do I now flee: heal me, O Christ, and save me, I pray.[^5516]
*Troparion 3:* The heavenly choirs hymn Thee, the God of all; by their holy intercessions, O Master, overlook my many evils and save me, I pray.[^5517]
*Martyrikon:* Numbered among the angelic choirs, O sufferers of Christ, and filled with unwaning illumination, consume the foul-smelling passions of my heart.[^5518]
*Glory:* Like Angels, let us who are earth-born sing a thrice-holy hymn unto God who is hymned in the highest: Holy art Thou, O unoriginate Father, Son, with the Spirit.[^5519]
*Both now:* Having received in thy womb the joy of the Angels, O Pure One, fill my soul that mourns over wicked deeds with joy, guiding me unto the light.[^5520]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner

*On Monday Evening:*[^5521]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5522]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5523]
*Sticheron 1:* Woe is me, who have angered Thee, my merciful God and Lord! How often did I promise to repent, O Christ, and was found a senseless liar: for I defiled the first garment of baptism, and I abandoned my covenant which was unto Thee; and this second testament again, which I confessed unto Thee before Angels and...[^5524]

...men, being clothed in mournful form, which I have laid aside. Furthermore, O Savior, leave me not to perish utterly.[^5525]
*Sticheron 2:* What manner of answer, O wretched soul, wilt thou make in the Day of Judgment? Or who shall deliver thee from the condemnation of eternal fire and other torments? No one, unless thou thyself make the Compassionate One propitious, leaving behind thy wicked deeds and acquiring a life pleasing unto God, weeping at every hour wherein thou sinnest by deed, and word, and thought: praying unto Christ that He grant unto thy soul complete forgiveness thereof.[^5526]
*Sticheron 3:* Let not sinful habit that drags me down hold me, O Savior, neither let the demon possess me, constantly warring and bringing me down unto his will: but snatch me from his dominion by Thy strong hand, O Almighty One! And reign within me, Thou Lover of mankind! Vouchsafe me to belong wholly unto Thee, and to live according to Thy will, O Word, and to have rest in Thee, and that I may find cleansing, and salvation, and great mercy.[^5527]
*Other Stichera to the Forerunner.*[^5528]
*Tone and podoben the same:*[^5529]
*Sticheron 1:* Heal me who am wholly bowed down unto the earth and incurably crushed, O Forerunner and Martyr of Christ, who flee in faith unto thy divine protection, O Blessed One! Therefore also snatch me from the dreadful day wherein I must stand before the judgment seat and be given over unto torments; deliver me then from standing there, I pray thee, as having unashamed boldness, O Wise One, entreating Him who grants great mercy unto the world.[^5530]
*Sticheron 2:* Being the lamp of the Sun, O wise one, most radiant Forerunner of Christ: shine upon me the ray of light, when the darkness of immeasurable evils has fallen upon me, I pray thee, and raise me up from the pit of sin, guiding me by thy sweet voice; thou who of old didst loose the voice of thy father by thy birth, now set right my voice, I pray thee, that in faith and love I may give glory unto God and the Savior, the Lover of mankind, who grants great mercy unto the world.[^5531]
*Sticheron 3:* A divine temple wast thou of God the Giver of life unto all, O blessed Prophet, Forerunner, and Preacher, having Him living in thy heart. Pray Him unceasingly: that those who flee unto thy holy temple and honor thee, O most rich John, may be temples of the Spirit, we pray, and draw near unto God, that we may honor in hymns thy protection and fervent prayer, from Whom thou wast witnessed to be greater than all men, O Most-honored One.[^5532]
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Wholly from infancy was I seen doing sins without ceasing; grievously was I wounded in mind, and having continued in them with love through much habit. And now I...[^5533]

...mourn, lamenting my cruel perdition. O Lady, despise me not who perish evilly; but having had compassion, deliver me from every assault and passion by thy protection, that at least in old age I may repent unto God.[^5534]
*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon and the rest:*[^5535]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5536]
*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, I cease not from sinning; though vouchsafed Thy love for mankind, I understand it not: overcome my perplexity, O only Good One, and have mercy on me.[^5537]
*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the handmaid look unto the hands of her mistress, so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.[^5538]
*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, I both fear Thy dread and cease not from doing evil. Who at the judgment-seat fears not the judge? Or who, desiring to be healed, provokes the physician to wrath as do I? O long-suffering Lord, have compassion on my weakness, and have mercy on me.[^5539]
*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.[^5540]
*Martyrikon:* Having armed themselves with the shield of faith, and having strengthened themselves with the sign of the Cross, Thy holy ones, O Lord, bravely gave themselves over unto torments, and cast down the pride and deceit of the devil. By their supplications, as the almighty God, send down peace upon the world, and great mercy unto our souls.[^5541]
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Assuage the pains of my much-sighing soul, thou who didst wipe away every tear from the face of the earth; for thou drivest away the diseases of men and dispersest the sorrows of sinners: for thee have we all acquired as hope and confirmation, O most holy Virgin Mother.[^5542]
*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion of the Saint; Glory... Both now... Theotokion in the Tone of the Troparion; ectenia and dismissal.*[^5543]
*On Monday at Small Compline:*[^5544]
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^5545]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Horse and rider in the Red Sea, He who crushes wars with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5546]
*Refrain:* Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.[^5547]
*Troparion 1:* Grant me a contrite spirit, O Good One, humility of heart, purity of mind, correction of life, and remission of transgressions, and...[^5548]

...fountains of tears, O All-immaculate One.[^5549]
*Troparion 2:* Behold my distress and humiliation, and heal the bruises of ancient transgressions, and grant me time for repentance and confession of sins.[^5550]
*Glory:* I weep for myself before my departure, considering the abyss of mine evils, O All-immaculate One. Therefore I pray thee: Entreat thy Son to deliver me from torment.[^5551]
*Both now:* Having time for repentance, depart from every malice, O my soul, and cry with tears unto thy Creator: O my God, save me by the supplications of her who gave birth unto Thee.[^5552]
*Ode 3. Heirmos:* He who established the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and suspended it without restraint, though weighed down: upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, establish Thy Church, O only Good and Lover of mankind.[^5553]
*Troparion 1:* The authors of my passions have encompassed me, O Most-pure One, and have filled me with shame, who was created according to the image and likeness of God; but deliver me from their harm, who in compunction sing unto thee.[^5554]
*Troparion 2:* Deceitfully the enemy strives to ensnare me, desiring to show me as fuel for the eternal flame, O Most-pure One! But destroy his craftiness and counsels, that rejoicing I may glorify thee.[^5555]
*Glory:* Into the lowest pit have the enemies of the righteous deceitfully cast me because of my many transgressions; and as one who is now helpless and wholly wounded, I call upon thy prayer: O all-immaculate Lady, save me.[^5556]
*Both now:* In slothfulness have I spent my life, wretch that I am, and now have I drawn near unto the gates of death, and trembling at the assaults of the enemy, I cry unto thee: Deliver me from these temptations, that being saved I may glorify thee.[^5557]
*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Understanding Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk foresightedly, O Christ, with trembling cried unto Thee: For the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed ones art Thou come.[^5558]
*Troparion 1:* I have been wounded with the arrows of sin, and I now bear about the bruise of my whole body; therefore I cry unto thee, O Most-pure One: By the swiftness of thy prayers, heal the wounds of my soul.[^5559]

*Troparion 2:* Have mercy, O Pure One, upon thy servants; for thee have we acquired as intercessor with God, and we pray that we may be delivered from every necessity and eternal torment.[^5560]
*Glory:* Hope, and shield, and confirmation art thou unto me, O Good One, and deliverance from grievous things, and enlightenment of my soul, and praise, and wall, and dominion.[^5561]
*Both now:* Profligately have I squandered the wealth of good works which Christ as good granted unto me; despise me not therefore, O all-pure Maiden, who perish of hunger.[^5562]
*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Covering Thyself with light as with a garment, I awake early unto Thee and cry to Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Compassionate One.[^5563]
*Troparion 1:* Behold, O Lady, the weakness of my humble soul, and the infirmity of my flesh, and the captivity of my mind, and save me who have no hope.[^5564]
*Troparion 2:* Pray God, Whom thou didst bear, ever to save all of us who confess thee as Theotokos and glorify thy childbearing, O Most-pure One.[^5565]
*Glory:* Who blesses thee not, O All-immaculate One? For thou didst ineffably give birth unto the Creator of the whole world, the Savior Christ and only Master.[^5566]
*Both now:* Send down upon me streams of tears, O Most-pure One, wherewith having washed away the defilements and wounds of my sins, I may sing the multitude of thy goodness.[^5567]
*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, tame the sea of passions, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.[^5568]
*Troparion 1:* I have cast myself into the depth of transgressions and iniquities; but stretch forth thy hand, O All-immaculate One, and bring me up from the hell of despair.[^5569]
*Troparion 2:* Thou knowest the multitude of my transgressions and the thoughts that trouble me; make haste therefore, and deliver me from them, O Most-pure One.[^5570]
*Glory:* Spare me Thy servant, O Master Christ, by the prayers of her who purely gave birth unto Thee, when Thou art about to judge the world which Thou hast created.[^5571]
*Both now:* Into the depth of transgressions hath my pure soul been dragged down, O Lady, having become food for demons; therefore, beyond all hope, save me.[^5572]

*Lord, have mercy (20 times). Glory... Both now:*[^5573]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*[^5574]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезна́чальное Сло́во):*[^5575]
*Sessional Hymn:* Quickly heal the much-painful passions of my soul, and the infirmities of my flesh; stay the wanderings of my mind, O All-immaculate One, and vouchsafe me, O Theotokos, in quietness of thought to offer pure prayers unto the King of all, and to entreat remission of transgressions.[^5576]
*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the youths, who sang in unison: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5577]
*Troparion 1:* When I take into my mind the multitude of mine unseemly deeds and transgressions, O All-immaculate One, I fear and am in dread; from which free me by thy prayers, O Virgin.[^5578]
*Troparion 2:* The sleepless worm and unquenchable fire consume me continually and eat away my soul; let me not experience them, O most holy Theotokos.[^5579]
*Glory:* From the outer darkness and dreadful torment deliver me Thy servant, O all-immaculate Virgin, who cry unto Thy Son: O God, blessed art Thou.[^5580]
*Both now:* My flesh indeed have I defiled with shameful passions, and my mind have I darkened with foul thoughts; but have compassion, O Pure One, and save me thine unprofitable servant.[^5581]
*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, hymn the Lord and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^5582]
*Troparion 1:* Unto thee, O Theotokos, do I offer the infirmity of my humble soul, and the weakness of my heart, and the deception of my mind do I make known, asking thy help, O Virgin.[^5583]
*Troparion 2:* Be merciful unto Thy servants, O Word, through the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee, and save those who sing: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^5584]
*Glory:* O thou who didst bear the Lover of mercy, have mercy upon all who sing in faith: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^5585]
*Both now:* Deliver me from the outer darkness and the unsleeping worm, O good Virgin! For thou didst bear the Creator of the world, who does all things whatsoever He pleases, by thy prayers.[^5586]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah, a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5587]

*Troparion 1:* God, whom thou didst ineffably bear, O pure Sovereign Lady, beseech diligently to deliver us from necessities and afflictions, and from the fearful judgment that is to come, and vouchsafe unto us the radiance of His Saints.[^5588]

*Troparion 2:* More than all have I sinned, having disobeyed Thy life-giving commandments, O Christ, and I have become like unto irrational beasts; but by the prayers of Thy Mother, O Word, pluck me not from life unrepentant.[^5589]

*Glory...* As having borne the Abyss of mercy, the Word of God, have mercy, O Good One, on the souls of all who flee beneath thy protection; for thee have we all acquired as an unashamed advocate before God.[^5590]

*Both now...* Ask for us cleansing and forgiveness of transgressions, and transformation of all grievous things, and now an upright and light-bearing way of life, that we may glorify thy great mercy, O Mother of God.[^5591]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual.*[^5592]

*On Tuesday at Matins:*[^5593]
*After the 1st Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5594]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When the Judge sits and the Angels stand by, the trumpet sounds and the flame burns, what wilt thou do, O my soul, being led unto judgment? For then thy grievous deeds shall stand before thee, and thy secret transgressions shall be exposed; therefore before the end cry aloud unto the Judge: O God, cleanse me and save me.[^5595]

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^5596]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Let us all watch and meet Christ with abundance of oil and radiant lamps, that we may be accounted worthy of entering the bridal chamber; for he that is caught outside the doors shall cry in vain unto God: Have mercy on me.[^5597]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* A strange mystery of the Virgin! For from her without seed was born and in the flesh appeared the Lord, salvation unto the world. O Joy of all, Lord, glory to Thee![^5598]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5599]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When thy deeds shall be uncovered, O my soul, and ten thousands of Angels...[^5600]

...stand by: what ashamed answer wilt thou find before the Judge, unless before the end thou cry aloud weeping: I have sinned, O good Lord, have mercy on me.[^5601]

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^5602]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* I lie upon a bed of many transgressions, robbed of the hope of my salvation; for the sleep of my slothfulness procures torment for my soul. But do Thou, O God born of the Virgin, raise me up unto Thy singing, that I may glorify Thee.[^5603]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^5604]

*Martyrikon:* The miracles of Thy holy martyrs hast Thou granted us as an indestructible wall, O Christ God; by their entreaties destroy the counsels of the nations, and strengthen the scepters of the kingdom, as the only good One and Lover of mankind.[^5605]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Show forth thy speedy protection, and help, and mercy upon thy servant, and calm the waves of vain thoughts, O Pure One, and raise up my fallen soul, O Theotokos; for I know, I know, O Virgin, that thou canst do all things whatsoever thou desirest.[^5606]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5607]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезна́чальное Сло́во):*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* The tempest of despair overwhelms me, who have slipped into the deep of sin; but anticipate me, O Christ, as all-powerful, Helmsman of all, and direct me unto the quiet haven of dispassion by the prayers of Thy Forerunner, in Thy tenderheartedness, O Savior, and save me.[^5608]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Elizabeth was freed from barrenness, and the Virgin remained again a virgin when she conceived in her womb at Gabriel's voice; but the Forerunner John leaps in the womb, foreseeing God and the Master being incarnate in the virginal womb for our salvation.[^5609]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O divine Flower that sprouted from the root of Jesse, ark and lampstand, all-golden urn, holy table bearing the Bread of Life: as His Mother, entreat Thy Son and God together with the holy Forerunner, to have compassion and to save those who confess thee as Theotokos.[^5610]

*Penitential Canon.*[^5611]
*(Whose acrostic is: Grant us cleansing of transgressions, O Christ.)*[^5612]
*A composition of Joseph.*[^5613]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Earth upon which the sun never shone nor looked; the deep which the wide expanse of heaven never saw naked: Israel traversed dryshod, O Lord; and Thou leddest him into the mountain of Thy sanctuary, as he praised and sang a song of victory.[^5614]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^5615]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me cleansing of the things committed by me, O Savior, and give me relief before I depart hence. Cleanse my many sores, O Lord, who cleansed the lepers; and vouchsafe me to stand blameless before Thee, when Thou comest to judge the living and the dead.[^5616]

*Troparion 2:* The corruption that lies upon the eyes of my soul, preventing me from beholding Thy rays, which Thou didst spread out having appeared on earth, O unwaning Sun: cleanse this, O Savior, and grant me, O tenderhearted Lord, to contemplate the light of Thy graces.[^5617]

*Martyrikon 1:* Having been watchful guardians of Christ's commandments, ye lulled to sleep all the malice of the enemy, O blessed sufferers. Therefore, I pray, raise me up unto divine watchfulness of repentance, who am weighed down with the sleep of sin.[^5618]

*Martyrikon 2:* Entangled in the flesh with the taunting enemy, O Martyrs, ye overcame him by the weapon of the Cross, and drowned him in the flow of your blood, and received crowns of victory from God, chanting and singing a song of victory.[^5619]

*Theotokion:* Deliver me from evil habit, O pure Virgin! Establish me upon the rock of the commandments, shaken as I am by the wiles of the ancient supplanter, O Sovereign Lady; and vouchsafe me to be well-pleasing unto Christ, chanting and singing a song of victory.[^5620]

*Second Canon, to John the Forerunner.*[^5621]
*(Whose acrostic is: O Baptist, accept this supplication.)*[^5622]
*A composition of Joseph.*[^5623]
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He who crushes wars with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5624]

*Troparion 1:* A cleansed manner of life and an immaterial life didst thou show forth, O Forerunner, in a material body. Therefore we pray thee: make those who bless thee in faith imitators of thyself.[^5625]

*Troparion 2:* Thou who didst plunge the Abyss of tenderheartedness, Christ, into the streams of the river: beseech Him, O Forerunner, I pray, to dry up the abyss of my evils, and to enlighten my understanding.[^5626]

*Troparion 3:* O Forerunner of the Savior, interceding on my behalf for causes of repentance, ask that compunction be given me, washing away the mire of foul...[^5627]

...sin, praying the Lover of mankind, I beseech thee.[^5628]

*Theotokion:* Ineffably without pain didst thou bear Him whom the Father begat incorruptibly before the ages, O all-hymned Sovereign Lady; entreat Him to save from every harm those who flee unto thee.[^5629]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* My heart that is tossed by the waves of this life, O Lord, do Thou establish, guiding it into a quiet haven as God.[^5630]

*Troparion 1:* Promising to repent unto Thee, O God, and sinning again, what shall become of me? How shall I appear when Thou judgest the earth?[^5631]

*Troparion 2:* Let us bring supplications unto the Lord; let us sigh, let us shed tears here to cleanse defilements, that we may be delivered there.[^5632]

*Martyrikon 1:* With mortified mind the transgressors of the law wounded the victorious martyrs who loved life, as they confessed Christ.[^5633]

*Martyrikon 2:* The ranks of martyrs were numbered with the choirs of spiritual Angels, having become equal to the Angels by the grace of the Holy Spirit.[^5634]

*Theotokion:* Open now unto me the gates of repentance, O Virgin, gate of light: and close the entrances of passions of my humble soul.[^5635]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5636]
*Ode 3. Heirmos:* He who established the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and suspended it without restraint, though weighed down: upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, establish Thy Church, O only Good and Lover of mankind.[^5637]

*Troparion 1:* Ever pouring forth healings unto those who draw near in faith unto thy temple, O marvelous Forerunner: heal the passions of my heart, I pray thee, which have all-wretchedly grown within me through heedlessness, O all-wealthy one.[^5638]

*Troparion 2:* I sigh, and am ever held fast by lamentations, as I contemplate Thy judgment without bribe, O only most righteous Judge; wherein, by the entreaties of Thy Baptist, preserve me uncondemned, O Lord my God.[^5639]

*Troparion 3:* Having become mediator of the New and the Old [Covenant], O glorious Forerunner, by divine intercessions renew by repentance me who am grown old through many sins, I cry unto thee, that in praise I may honor thee.[^5640]

*Theotokion:* Holy Virgin Mother, only all-immaculate one, deliver us from the stain of transgressions; enlighten our...[^5641]

...thought, sanctify our hearts, and deliver us all from everlasting condemnation, we pray.[^5642]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard, O Lord, the report of Thee, and I was afraid; I understood Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^5643]

*Troparion 1:* Having disregarded Thy words that enlighten, O Lord, I have done dark deeds; and I dread Thy fearful judgment there.[^5644]

*Troparion 2:* With the sail of divine fear let us wing the ship of the soul, and reach the haven of repentance, having escaped the tempest of evils.[^5645]

*Martyrikon 1:* Distilling sweetness, O Martyrs, ye were revealed as divine mountains and a God-planted paradise, having the Lord as the Tree of Life.[^5646]

*Martyrikon 2:* With the arrows of patience and endurance, O Holy Ones, ye shot down the taunting demons, and received crowns of glory.[^5647]

*Theotokion:* The Most Pure Sovereign Lady, advocate of sinners, divine restoration of the fallen, as having given birth unto God, is glorified.[^5648]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5649]
*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Having perceived Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk cried with trembling unto Thee, O Christ: Thou art come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed.[^5650]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast taken up thy dwelling in the kingdom on high, as a true soldier of Christ the King, O Baptist: beseech Him incessantly to have compassion on the people that honor thee.[^5651]

*Troparion 2:* The Lord sanctified thee from the womb, foreseeing the thankfulness of thy heart, O Blessed One: entreat Him to sanctify us all, we pray.[^5652]

*Troparion 3:* Unto the dead didst thou preach the good tidings of the coming of Him who was put to death for our sake; beseech Him, O Forerunner, to make alive and save me also, who have been put to death by sins.[^5653]

*Theotokion:* Have mercy on me, O only All-immaculate One, who through exceedingly great goodness didst ineffably give birth unto the merciful God; and deliver me from eternal torment.[^5654]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* My wretched soul, that battles in the night against the darkness of passions, anticipate and have compassion upon; and shine forth, O Sun of righteousness, day-bright rays within me, so as to divide the night from the light.[^5655]

*Troparion 1:* From works there is no salvation for me; for much upon earth have I sinned, wretched one, and I tremble at Thy fearful judgment-seat, when Thou art about to judge the transgressors of Thy commandments, O God.[^5656]

*Troparion 2:* How have I become foolish? How have I been darkened, doing evil deeds? How did I not understand Thy fear, O Christ? I have bowed down unto the earth, and have become like irrational beasts; but turn me, O God of all.[^5657]

*Martyrikon 1:* The cloud of martyrs dispersed the clouds of bitter torments; and they illuminated the day of true understanding, and destroyed the gloom of polytheism, and attained unto the unwaning Light.[^5658]

*Martyrikon 2:* Sanctify my mind by the prayers of Thy holy martyrs, O Christ, I pray, and show me full of enlightenment and a partaker of everlasting glory, that with glory I may hymn Thee, O Savior.[^5659]

*Theotokion:* God by an ineffable Word hast thou borne unto us, O Virgin Mother, who grants repentance unto all that have sinned much, through thy good intercession, O refuge and haven of the faithful.[^5660]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5661]
*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Clothed with light as with a garment, unto Thee I rise early, and unto Thee I cry: enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only tenderhearted One.[^5662]

*Troparion 1:* A barren womb brought thee forth, O Forerunner, who showest hearts that are barren of good deeds to be fruitful in children, through thy fruitful words; therefore we call thee blessed.[^5663]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast blossomed forth as a fragrant lily, O ever-blessed one, in the wildernesses; therefore I cry unto thee: O Forerunner, drive far from my soul all foul stench of wickedness.[^5664]

*Troparion 3:* Thou stoodest in the midst of the Law and of grace, O all-wise one; therefore I cry aloud: Have compassion upon me who am overcome by the law of sin, O Forerunner, wretchedly suffering in perils.[^5665]

*Theotokion:* Inpassable gate of glory, O Most Pure Lady, open unto me the gates of repentance, mediating for me divine entrances and the resting-place there.[^5666]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* As Thou didst deliver the prophet from the beast, O Lord, so also lead me up from the depth of uncontrollable passions, I pray, that I may look again toward Thy holy temple.[^5667]

*Troparion 1:* Behold the time of turning, and I lie prostrate ever backward, held fast continually by much insensibility; loose the darkening of my heart, O Word, and have compassion on me.[^5668]

*Troparion 2:* Groaning as once the publican, have compassion on me, O tenderhearted Christ; and as the harlot vouchsafe me warmly to shed tears, that I also may wash away the mire of my many transgressions.[^5669]

*Martyrikon 1:* Great martyrs of Christ, snatch me who have sinned greatly from the great flame that is in Gehenna, awaiting me there, that greatly I also may glorify your memory ever.[^5670]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having contended well, Thy sufferers were radiantly crowned by Thy life-originating right hand, O God and Lord; by their honorable entreaties save all Thy people.[^5671]

*Theotokion:* Tabernacle of sanctification that received the greeting "Rejoice", honorable ark, lampstand of divine light, Table of the Bread of Life, living Palace of the Word: show me to be a temple of the Spirit.[^5672]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5673]
*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, calm the sea of passions, and bring me up from corruption, for Thou art tenderhearted.[^5674]

*Troparion 1:* Shining with ineffable rays, O Forerunner of Christ, by thine active prayer enlighten the hearts of those that praise thee with piety.[^5675]

*Troparion 2:* Held fast by the sleep of slothfulness, O Forerunner of Christ, enlighten me with grace, and raise me up zealously to do the divine desires.[^5676]

*Troparion 3:* From every hostile affliction deliver us, who have acquired thee as a divine advocate and intercessor before the Sovereign Lady, O Blessed One.[^5677]

*Theotokion:* A tempest of sin troubles me, O Most Pure Theotokos! Make haste to deliver me, guiding me unto the haven of repentance, O All-immaculate One.[^5678]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The prayer of the youths quenched the fire, bedewing the furnace, proclaiming the miracle, neither burning nor consuming the hymn-singers of the God of our fathers.[^5679]

*Troparion 1:* My lawless deeds and mine unrighteousnesses, my countless transgressions, O Christ, forgive, and deliver me from the torment that is...[^5680]

...to come, according to the multitude of Thy tender mercies, O God.[^5681]

*Troparion 2:* Like the prodigal have I now wasted the wealth which I received, and I melt with hunger, deprived of divine food; accept me repenting, O Savior, and save me.[^5682]

*Martyrikon 1:* Dead did ye make the contending enemy by members being put to death through many torments, O worthy-of-wonder Martyrs of the Lord! Therefore we faithful piously sing praise unto you.[^5683]

*Martyrikon 2:* The demonic hosts, the whole multitude of tormentors, O Martyrs, ye wounded with arrows, and by patience and valor ye now passed over unto true life.[^5684]

*Theotokion:* A saving haven hast thou appeared unto all, O Pure Lady, calming the storm of passions, and leading all the humble on earth unto quietness, O pure Mother of God.[^5685]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5686]
*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the children as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5687]

*Troparion 1:* In words we pray thee, O Forerunner of the Word: as thou didst loose thy father's voice at thy birth, loose the snares of our transgressions.[^5688]

*Troparion 2:* All-radiant Sun, shine forth upon me the saving radiance of repentance, and deliver me from the darkness of passions that trouble my darkened heart.[^5689]

*Troparion 3:* A barren soul have I acquired, and a childless heart; O divine offspring of a barren woman, entreat incessantly Christ's Baptist, that fruits of repentance may sprout in me.[^5690]

*Triadikon:* Equal to the Begetter we glorify the Son, and the Holy Spirit, divinely singing to the undivided Trinity: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5691]

*Theotokion:* A young Child hast thou borne, O All-immaculate One, Christ who effects our renewal, who were grown old through the ancient transgression.[^5692]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The assembly of Angels, the congregation of men, the King and Creator of all, ye priests sing, ye Levites bless, ye peoples supremely exalt unto all ages.[^5693]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, the wounds of my soul have become foul and corrupted, O Christ, and I have suffered and been brought completely low; by the remedies of repentance, O Savior, heal me.[^5694]

*Troparion 2:* Deceitfully stealing upon me, the most crafty serpent filled me with evils; and sighing I cry: Cast me not away, O Word, who am condemned and brought low.[^5695]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye turned not aside from the better station, but cast down the enemies of the Cross who thought to trip you, O all-praised ones, having completely conquered, O most wise ones.[^5696]

*Martyrikon 2:* You, O all-praised Martyrs of the Lord, neither fire, nor sword, nor wild beasts, nor famine, nor breaking on wheels, nor any other torment was able to separate from Christ the Lover of mankind.[^5697]

*Theotokion:* Praise of Angels, and salvation of men, and surety be thou unto me, O Mother of God: that I may turn and receive forgiveness of the sins previously committed in knowledge and in ignorance.[^5698]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5699]
*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, the Almighty Creator, the youths in the furnace, weaving a universal choir, sang: All ye works, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^5700]

*Troparion 1:* With the streams of the Jordan thou washest the assembly of the people, preaching repentance, O great Forerunner. Therefore I cry unto thee: Dry up the flow of my passions, sending me fountains of tears.[^5701]

*Troparion 2:* Be thou in all trembling, O soul, as thou contemplatest the judgment-seat of the Almighty, crying: O Compassionate One, for the sake of Thy Baptist, have compassion and save me, and deliver me from torments.[^5702]

*Troparion 3:* Unclean lips and a defiled tongue do I move unto supplication to thee, holy Forerunner: hasten quickly to help me, who am continually shaken by all kinds of assaults of the deceiver.[^5703]

*Triadikon:* Rain down upon us, O Father, and Son, and Spirit, consubstantial Trinity, forgiveness of transgressions: that having obtained perfect salvation, we may supremely exalt Thee unto all ages.[^5704]

*Theotokion:* Thou hast raised us up by thy lofty childbearing from the pit of fallings, O thou who art full of God's grace! Therefore with thankful voices in faith we hymn thee, O Maiden, unto all ages.[^5705]

*Then we sing: "My soul magnifies the Lord:"*[^5706]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* For He that is mighty has done great things to thee, having shown thee a pure Virgin after childbearing, as having given birth without seed unto her Creator; therefore, O Theotokos, we magnify thee.[^5707]

*Troparion 1:* That I may magnify Thy long-suffering, O Jesus, be long-suffering yet toward me, and cut me not down as the barren fig tree, I cry unto Thee, that I may bring Thee fruits of repentance.[^5708]

*Troparion 2:* How terrible art Thou, O only Sovereign and Mighty One! And who shall stand against Thy fearful threatening, when Thou art about to sit Thyself in judgment? Wherein preserve me uncondemned.[^5709]

*Martyrikon 1:* Bodily pains ye take away by the pains of your torments, O sufferers of the Lord; therefore heal the most grievous passions of my soul, as being most skilled physicians.[^5710]

*Martyrikon 2:* More than the rays of the sun shines the shrine of your relics, with beams of divine grace, and it enlightens hearts and illuminates souls of those that praise you in faith, O passion-bearers.[^5711]

*Theotokion:* A light-resembling cloud leading the new people unto the land of promise hast thou truly been revealed, O one full of God's grace, and the gate that leads unto life; therefore, O Theotokos, we magnify thee.[^5712]

*Canon to the Forerunner.*[^5713]
*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah, a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5714]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, the beauty of thy temple is known as heaven upon earth, O Forerunner of Christ; coming into which, thou enlightenest with divine rays those that now call thee blessed in it throughout all days.[^5715]

*Troparion 2:* As being the true friend of the Master, O Baptist, strengthen me unswervingly to love Him with an upright disposition, O all-blessed one, and to abhor corrupting passions that drag me down unto destruction.[^5716]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast not a reed shaken by contrary winds, O most wise one, but our divine establishment and the unshakable strengthening of the Church; which by thy prayers preserve unmoved, quelling every scandal.[^5717]

*Troparion 4:* The Creator's coming is at the doors! Why then dost thou not weep for thyself, O wretched soul, living in heedlessness? Arise and cry aloud unto the Lord: Spare me, O Savior, by the prayers of the Forerunner, as Lover of mankind.[^5718]

*Theotokion:* A light-bearing chariot of the Sun hast thou truly been revealed, of Him who shone forth from thy side, O All-immaculate, Pure One, and destroyed the grievous darkness of deceit; therefore as is due, in faith we call thee blessed.[^5719]

*It is truly meet: and the Little Ectenia. Then the Photagogicon and the customary Psalms.*[^5720]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5721]

*Sticheron 1:* Overlook the multitudes of my transgressions, O Lord born of the Virgin, and cleanse all mine iniquities; grant me a mindset of repentance, as the only Lover of mankind, I pray, and have mercy on me.[^5722]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: - - - And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^5723]

*Sticheron 2:* Woe is me! Unto what have I likened myself? Unto the barren fig tree; and I fear the curse together with the felling: but O heavenly Husbandman, Christ God, show my dried-up soul to be fruitful, and receive me as the prodigal son, and have mercy on me.[^5724]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and prosper Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou prosper.*[^5725]

*Martyrikon:* Thy Passion-bearers, O Lord, emulators of the Angelic ranks, endured torments as though bodiless, having unanimous hope of the enjoyment of promised blessings; by their prayers, O Christ God, grant peace unto Thy world, and great mercy unto our souls.[^5726]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Hope of the hopeless, Life of the despairing, Protection of those who flee unto thee, O holy Lady, Virgin Theotokos: send down thy help upon us.[^5727]

*It is good to give praise unto the Lord: Trisagion; after Our Father: troparion, ectenia, and the First Hour.*[^5728]

*On Tuesday, The Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^5729]

*Troparion 1:* The thief on the cross having believed Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom.[^5730]

*Troparion 2:* Even as Thou didst justify, O Christ, the harlot who wept from her whole soul, so also me, O Master...

...who am despairing, snatch me from all torment, O Good One, I pray.[^5731]

*Troparion 3:* John who ran before Christ and prepared the good paths, let us unanimously call blessed, that by his divine prayers we may be delivered from transgressions.[^5732]

*Martyrikon:* Ye who with fervent soul drank Christ's cup, O Martyrs, deliver us from turbid sins and diseases by the rain-showers of your divine prayers, O holy ones.[^5733]

*Glory... Triadikon:* O incomprehensible God, almighty Trinity and Unity, by the prayers of Thy Forerunner save me, delivering me from darkness and from the fire that lies before me.[^5734]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Ever defiled by evil deeds, O Virgin, I pray thee, the undefiled Mother of the Master: cleanse me from every defilement, O Lady.[^5735]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross

*On Tuesday Evening:*[^5736]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^5737]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5738]

*Sticheron 1:* Thou wast lifted up as a lamb upon the Cross, and didst cast down the haughtiness of the deceiver; having been slain, Thou didst sanctify the whole earth with Thy blood; and pierced with a spear, Thou didst immediately command the flaming sword to turn its back to me, O Christ, and grant entry into Paradise, and that I might fearlessly enjoy the Tree of Life. Therefore, being saved by Thy Passion, I cry rejoicing: Glory to Thy divine Cross, whereby we have been delivered from the ancient curse, and embrace the blessing from the Tree, and great mercy.[^5739]

*Sticheron 2:* Desiring to bring an end to the pains and reproaches of men, O most good Lord, my Savior, Thou didst endure a reproachful crucifixion and didst taste gall, O Innocent One, taking away our wicked bitterness; and being wounded, O Word, by the piercing of Thy side, Thou healest our wounds as Master. Therefore also we sing of Thy glorious willing condescension, and bowing down we venerate...

...the spear, and the sponge, and the reed, whereby Thou hast granted peace and great mercy unto Thy world.[^5740]

*Sticheron 3:* Desiring to tear up to the end the all-grievous handwriting of Adam the first-formed, O Lover of mankind, as Adam's Creator Thou wast affixed with nails, O sinless Christ; and having had Thy divine side wounded with a spear for our sake, Thou commandest the flaming sword, O Word, not to bar the entry unto us, Thy servants. Therefore, giving glory unto Thee, we sing of Thy dominion, and we venerate Thy Cross, and we hymn Thee, O Lover of mankind, whereby every nature has found a painless inheritance, and great mercy.[^5741]

*Other Stichera to the Theotokos, the same Tone:*[^5742]

*Sticheron 1:* How did the lawless Hebrew multitude condemn Thee, O Jesus, to be crucified upon the Cross, having no compassion on Thee who art compassionate? the Virgin cried weeping of old, when she beheld lifted up upon the Cross Him whom she impassibly bare from her womb: What has the lawless synagogue done unto Thee, O my longed-for Child, my most dearly beloved Son? Make haste and save those who in faith glorify Thy crucifixion, and who magnify me as Thou didst promise, as the only all-good One.[^5743]

*Sticheron 2:* A ransom for our sake, and a great price, even Thy most pure blood, didst Thou deign to give, O my sinless Christ, desiring that all should obtain salvation. Therefore Thy Mother, beholding Thee nailed, lamented and tore her hair, saying: Child, all-immaculate Lamb, who desirest to deliver the world by Thy precious blood, how hast Thou set from mine eyes, O Savior, never-setting Sun, who grantest enlightenment, and peace, and great mercy unto all![^5744]

*Sticheron 3:* Truly a Cherubic throne higher than creation hast thou been; for into thee, O Pure One, desiring to renew our form, the divine Word entered, and having come forth from thee bearing flesh, as the Compassionate One, He accepted the Cross and Passion for our sake, and as God granted the Resurrection. Therefore, giving thanks to the Creator as having reconciled our condemned nature, unto Him and unto thee we cry: Grant us remission of transgressions and mercy, by thy prayers.[^5745]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding of old her Lamb hastening unto the slaughter, the Ewe-lamb followed fervently, crying these things unto Him: Whither goest Thou, my sweetest Child, O Christ? For what cause dost Thou run this course so swiftly without slackening, O Long-suffering One? O Jesus most desired, sinless and most merciful Lord, give a word unto Thy handmaid, my all-beloved Son! Disdain me not in silence, O Compassionate One, who marvelously bare Thee; O God the Giver of life, grant great mercy unto the world.[^5746]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening:*[^5747]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*[^5748]

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, of old under Moses the Prophet, the mere image of Thy Cross when revealed conquered Thine enemies; but now having the Cross itself, we ask for help: strengthen Thy Church, and grant victory unto our Emperor *(Name)*, as unto Constantine, for the sake of Thy great mercy, O Lover of mankind.[^5749]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: - - - so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^5750]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy Cross, O Christ, even though visible as wood in substance, is yet clothed with divine power; and visibly manifest unto the world, it spiritually works our salvation. Bowing down before it, we glorify Thee, O Savior: have mercy on us.[^5751]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^5752]

*Martyrikon:* Pray for us, holy Martyrs, that we may be delivered from our transgressions; for unto you grace is given to pray for us.[^5753]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*

*Tone 5, Podoben: "O venerable father" (Преподо́бне ѻ҆́тче):*[^5754]

Having taken on my entire form, the Creator and God clothed Himself, desiring to reshape the form of Adam that had fallen of old; He was lifted up upon the Cross willingly as guilty, now spreading out His hands, healing the hands that of old reached slackly toward the tree of eating. Beholding Him, the All-pure One cried aloud: What is this Thine ineffable long-suffering, O my Son? I cannot endure to see Thee lifted up upon the Cross, who holdest the ends of the whole earth in Thy hand![^5755]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion of the Saint; Glory... Both now... Theotokion in the Tone of the Troparion; ectenia and dismissal.*[^5756]

*On Tuesday at Small Compline.*[^5757]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^5758]

*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He that shatters battles with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5759]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^5760]

*Troparion 1:* Receiving the prayers of thy servants brought faithfully unto thee from the depth of the heart, O Theotokos, and offering them unto thy Son, grant remission of transgressions...

...brought unto thee, O Theotokos, and offering them unto thy Son, grant remission of transgressions.[^5761]

*Troparion 2:* Truly being a benefactress, enriching the earth-born, and the door of salvation leading unto life, O Virgin Mother of God, cease never standing in intercession for those who pray to thee.[^5762]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Now inclined by the supplications of thy singers, as being a mighty help toward salvation, delivering them from calamities by thy prayers, O Lady, preserve intact those who honor thee.[^5763]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Making haste quickly and interceding fervently, O most holy Virgin, having compassion deliver us who are sorely possessed by cruel circumstances, and set us free from sorrow.[^5764]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou who by Thy command didst establish the earth upon nothing, and didst suspend its weight hanging uncontained, establish Thy Church upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, the only Good One and Lover of mankind.[^5765]

*Troparion 1:* Having illuminated the dark cloud of my heart by the wakeful, divine prayer of thy light, O Mother of God, show me to be a son of the day enlightened by virtues, O guide of light for our souls.[^5766]

*Troparion 2:* Desiring ever to hymn thee worthily, yet at a loss, we bring praise from humble lips; graciously accept it, and be attentive to the supplications of those who pray to thee in faith.[^5767]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Showing barren souls to be fruitful and cleansing what is grown wild by thy prayer, do thou, O Parent of God, show also my soul to bear fruit: faith and an undefiled life.[^5768]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* How is He who holds the ends of the whole earth in His hand embraced by maternal arms? and He is nourished with milk from the breasts, who satisfies every living thing with goodwill, and He who is rich in outpouring becomes poor by being incarnate.[^5769]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Having understood Thy divine emptying, Habakkuk farsightedly cried out unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: For the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed ones art Thou come.[^5770]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee as our only Mediatress unto God, who looses the debts of sins...

...unto the guilty, O All-pure One, we honor thee with praises and hymns.[^5771]

*Troparion 2:* Having dwelt in thee, God showed thee to be a sanctified temple, O all-immaculate Virgin; pray Him unceasingly to preserve Thy servants unharmed.[^5772]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Hear the prayer of the poor who place their hope in thee, O Theotokos, and make haste to deliver us from misfortunes by thy maternal bold intercession.[^5773]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Having acquired thee, O Mother of God, as sweet consolation in afflictions, help in perils, and protectress in misfortunes, we are delivered from every oppression.[^5774]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Thou that coverest Thyself with light as with a garment, I awake early unto Thee and cry to Thee: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Compassionate One.[^5775]

*Troparion 1:* Having in time brought forth in circumscribed flesh Him who is truly uncircumscribed by nature, the First Principle: pray Him unceasingly for thy servants.[^5776]

*Troparion 2:* Thou drawest near quickly unto those in distress, ever helping and guiding toward salvation those who call upon thee in temptations, O All-pure One.[^5777]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Showing the merciful God to be propitious by nature through thy prayers, grant unto thy servants a peaceful life and deliverance from transgressions, O All-immaculate One.[^5778]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* O thou who barest the live Coal whom Isaiah beheld, burn up for me the matter of sins, O All-immaculate One, and enlighten me, I pray.[^5779]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, tame the sea of passions, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art compassionate.[^5780]

*Troparion 1:* Having raised us up who were slain by the passions of sin, make us alive by thy prayers, O Lady, as having given birth unto the Destroyer of corruption.[^5781]

*Troparion 2:* Having thee as our unfailing hope, an invincible rampart against enemies, and steadfast help, we shall not fear plots against us.[^5782]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Ever stand by the earth-born in afflictions, O Pure One, and preserve unharmed from the cruel wounds that afflict thy servants.[^5783]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Enlighten my soul darkened by the cloud of thoughts...

...that assault me, O Bride of God, through the plotting of the man-slaying demon.[^5784]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*[^5785]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:* By the Cross of thy Son, O woman full of God's grace, all idol deception was abolished, and demonic might was trampled down. Therefore as is due, we the faithful ever hymn and bless thee, and confessing thee truly as Theotokos, we magnify thee.[^5786]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the youths, who sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5787]

*Troparion 1:* Receive the petitions of thy faithful servants, O Lady, taking away perils and saving from sorrows those who sing in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5788]

*Troparion 2:* O thou who didst bear God the Worker of good things for us, entreat Him by maternal supplications, O Pure One, to show mercy unto those who boast in thee.[^5789]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Having acquired thee, O Lady, as an invincible protectress and an unshakable foundation, and being clothed with thy protection, we overcome the machinations of the archer that are against us.[^5790]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Behold the lowliness of thy servant, O Lady, and set me free from the eternal fire, as I unceasingly sing: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5791]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, the Almighty Creator, the youths in the furnace, weaving a universal choir, sang: All ye works, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^5792]

*Troparion 1:* Weaving praise unto thee, O Virgin, with words and thoughts together, we the faithful cry aloud: Sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^5793]

*Troparion 2:* Solomon fore-inscribed thee in all thy beauty as the neighbor of God, as being nearest unto Him, O Virgin, delivering those who are in the world by maternal bold intercession.[^5794]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Having thee after God as the hope of salvation in perils, we the faithful cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^5795]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Receive a song from humble lips, O all-hymned Virgin, of those who cry aloud unto thee: Rejoice, medicine of those sick in body, and salvation of despairing souls.[^5796]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah, a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5797]

*Troparion 1:* Unto thee we pour out our prayer that comes from affliction: have mercy on us sinners, that we may not be put to shame in the day of temptation, who call upon thee the all-merciful Theotokos.[^5798]

*Troparion 2:* Despise not the kinship of dust, O pure Lady, but be a helper who shields, strengthening in sorrows, and delivering all the infirm; for in thee do we place our hope, who art ready to save us.[^5799]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* O God, hear the prayer of Thy people now, and deliver us from the harm of the enemy; behold, we bring unto Thee as true propitiation her who bare Thee, whom in love we call blessed.[^5800]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Under the dominion of thy might, O Lady, all of us earth-born diligently flee, asking for mercy and thy merciful compassions; having poured them forth, sanctify us all.[^5801]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual.*[^5802]

*At Wednesday Matins:*[^5803]

*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^5804]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The Place of the Skull became Paradise; for no sooner was the Tree of the Cross planted than it immediately brought forth the cluster of life unto our joy, even Thee, O Savior. Glory to Thee.[^5805]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for it is holy.*[^5806]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Our Savior and Redeemer who was crucified willingly as He knows and as He was well pleased, let us the faithful hymn and glorify; for He nailed the sins of men upon the Cross, delivering the human race from deception, and has made them worthy of the Kingdom.[^5807]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When she who bare Thee without travail beheld Thee lifted up upon the Tree, she lamented with weeping and cried aloud: Woe is me, sweetest Son! I am wounded in soul, beholding Thee upon the Cross between two thieves, nailed as a malefactor.[^5808]

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^5809]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thou who didst endure crucifixion of Thine own will, and didst deliver men from corruption...

...O Savior: we the faithful hymn and bow down before Thee, for Thou hast enlightened us by the power of the Cross; and in fear we glorify Thee, O merciful Lover of mankind, as the Giver of life and Lord.[^5810]

*Verse: But God is our King of old, He has wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^5811]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The Tree of Thy Cross, O our Savior, has been revealed as salvation unto the world; for thereon Thou wast nailed willingly, and didst deliver the earth-born from the curse. O Joy of all, Lord, glory to Thee.[^5812]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^5813]

*Martyrikon:* O Lord, Thy Passion-bearers having emulated the cup of Thy passions, abandoned the fairest things of life, and became partakers with the Angels; by their supplications grant peace and great mercy unto our souls, O Christ.[^5814]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* By the Cross of thy Son, O woman full of God's grace, all idol deception was abolished, and demonic might was trampled down; therefore as is due, we the faithful ever hymn and bless thee, and confessing thee truly as Theotokos, we magnify thee.[^5815]

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns:*[^5816]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезна́чальное Сло́во):*[^5817]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The prophetic utterances are now fulfilled, Thy counsel of old has reached its fulfillment; for Thou willingly, O King of all, didst impoverish Thyself in the flesh, O Christ, and didst accept for our sake to ascend the Cross and endure death; therefore we glorify Thy condescension beyond understanding, O Word.[^5818]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Beholding Thee nailed and asleep upon the Cross, the angelic ranks were terrified, O Jesus, All-King! and immediately the demonic regiments took flight, and the bars of hades were shattered, and the tyranny of death was overthrown, and the dead that were in the tombs were raised.[^5819]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee upon the Cross, O Christ, hanging willingly between two thieves, Thy Mother was torn in her maternal womb and said: O sinless Son, how art Thou unjustly nailed upon the Cross as a malefactor? Thou who desirest to make alive the human race, as Compassionate.[^5820]

*Canon to the Precious Cross.*[^5821]

*(Whose acrostic is: Raising up the world, O Christ, Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree.)*[^5822]

*The work of Joseph.*[^5823]

*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Horse and rider into the Red Sea, He that shatters battles with a high arm, Christ did shake off; and Israel He saved, as they sang a song of victory.[^5824]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^5825]

*Troparion 1:* Him who is understood in incomprehensibility, incarnate and manifest unto the world in the flesh, who of old willed it, the lawless Hebrew assembly lifted up upon the Tree.[^5826]

*Troparion 2:* When the Hebrews, hanging upon Thee, lifted Thee up upon the Tree of the Cross as a fruitful vine, O Christ, then didst Thou distil the wine of gladness, O Word, taking away all intoxication of evils.[^5827]

*Martyrikon 1:* Your endurance falls not under the laws of praise, O Martyrs; for beyond every human nature ye endured pains, and rejoicing ye settled in painless rest.[^5828]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having sailed through the waves of torments by the pilotage of the Word, O all-lauded sufferers of Christ, ye reached the heavenly havens, now enjoying divine tranquility.[^5829]

*Theotokion:* Beholding the Lord born of thy womb unjustly lifted up upon the Tree, O Virgin, thou didst weep, and didst hymn His truly ineffable condescension.[^5830]

*Another Canon to the Theotokos.*[^5831]

*(Whose acrostic is: A fifth song is now sung unto the Virgin.)*[^5832]

*Tone 5. The same Heirmos:*[^5833]

*Troparion 1:* O Door of divine glory, who didst open the doors of Paradise, open unto me also the doors of repentance, I pray, and enlighten my mind to sing of thee, O thou full of God's grace.[^5834]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast stayed the assault of death, having given birth unto Him who holds sway over life and death, O All-immaculate One! Pray Him, therefore, to stay the transgressions that slay my soul, and to save me.[^5835]

*Troparion 3:* As the one beauty of Jacob from among the generations, the Word co-unoriginate with the Father chose thee, and was incarnate of thy blood. O Lady, save me by thine intercessions.[^5836]

*Troparion 4:* As the heavenly rain upon the fleece, having descended upon thee, hast thou brought forth, O All-pure One; therefore dry up the torrential downpours of my passions, I pray thee, O Mother Virgin.[^5837]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou who by Thy command didst establish the earth upon nothing, and didst suspend its weight hanging uncontained, establish Thy Church upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, the only Good One and Lover of mankind.[^5838]

*Troparion 1:* Thou art crucified, and Paradise is opened again, and the thief enters rejoicing before all; Thou diest, O my Jesus, and the deceiving enemy is put to death, while dead Adam is brought to life. Glory to Thy great mercy.[^5839]

*Troparion 2:* Thou witherest the flame of sin, being crucified upon the Tree for goodness' sake, O Jesus; Thou dissolvest deception, being bound; stripped naked, Thou clothest man with a robe of glory. Glory to Thy great tender-heartedness.[^5840]

*Martyrikon 1:* As brightly ever-shining stars, the Martyrs drive away the darkness of impiety; by their supplications, O Christ, drive off the night of my wicked deeds, and enlighten my darkened heart.[^5841]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having lawfully suffered, O divinely glorious Martyrs, ye were crowned with wreaths through lawful faith, and wisely turned away from the counsels of the lawless, and received divine nourishment and the dwelling of Paradise.[^5842]

*Theotokion:* The angelic choirs sing of thee, O All-hymned One, as excelling them beyond question; for thou hast in the flesh given birth unto God, who destroyed the curse of the tree by the Tree, and poured forth blessing.[^5843]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5844]

*Troparion 1:* Wounded by the arrows of sin, heal me entirely by the medicine that is in thee, O Pure One, and deliver me from the diseases that encompass me, O thou who by thy childbearing didst exchange the diseases of the human race.[^5845]

*Troparion 2:* Cast down by thy protection, O Lady, the invisible enemies that afflict all my humble heart and seek to put me to death, that they may remain ineffective, filled with shame.[^5846]

*Troparion 3:* Fill me with living waters, O Lady, who didst pour forth divine water unto the world; dry up the grievous torrents of mine iniquities, and calm the waves of my heart by thy divine tranquility.[^5847]

*Troparion 4:* The things shadowily performed by the Law have passed away; for thou hast given birth unto Christ the Lawgiver, who ordains for us the grace of cleansing and enlightenment, and who delivers...

...from the curse, O all-immaculate, pure Virgin.[^5848]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Understanding Thine emptying, O Christ, Habakkuk cried out foresightedly unto Thee with trembling: Unto the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed, art Thou come.[^5849]

*Troparion 1:* Wrought complete deliverance for souls, O Christ, Thou didst commit Thy most holy soul into the hands of the Father, hanging willingly upon the Tree.[^5850]

*Troparion 2:* The unjust judge condemned Thee, the righteous Judge, O Jesus, to die hanging upon the Tree, that Thou mightest justify us who had unjustly bowed down unto the enemy.[^5851]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye were accounted worthy to behold the good things from the ages, O Martyrs, having manifestly partaken of many perils, and tribulations, and innumerable torments upon the earth.[^5852]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye passed through the winter of temptations, and reached the spring of heavenly recompense, and were numbered among the Angelic choirs, O honorable Martyrs.[^5853]

*Theotokion:* The sword of the divine Passions passed through thy soul, O Blessed One, when thou didst behold thy Son crucified, and committing His soul into the hands of the Father.[^5854]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5855]

*Troparion 1:* From works there is no salvation for me; therefore unto thy shelter do I flee with hope, O all-immaculate Virgin: save me, the despairing one, by thy prayers.[^5856]

*Troparion 2:* O pure receptacle of the Light, honorable vehicle of the Sun: illuminate my heart darkened by the gloom of wicked evils, and save me, I pray, O Lady.[^5857]

*Troparion 3:* O Maiden who didst weave a garment from thy virginal blood for Him who clothes heaven with clouds: clothe me with the garment of incorruption, who am stripped naked through deception.[^5858]

*Troparion 4:* Having taken thee as a lily from the valleys of life, the Creator breathed forth from thee spiritual fragrance unto the world, O most holy Virgin, Bride of God.[^5859]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee do I rise early and unto Thee do I cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Compassionate One.[^5860]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stand to be judged, O righteous Judge, Christ, condemning enmity in the flesh; and struck with a reed...

...and Thou didst sign complete freedom for me.[^5861]

*Troparion 2:* When the sun beheld Thee hanging in the flesh upon the Tree, O Christ, it turned its light into darkness, and the earth was shaken, and the rocks were split asunder.[^5862]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye sanctified the ends of the earth; for as fulfillers of the divine Law, O all-lauded Martyrs, suffering ye inherited holiness.[^5863]

*Martyrikon 2:* Let the most noble and divinely radiant Martyrs be honored, who were clothed with divine glory and stripped bare the malice of the enemy.[^5864]

*Theotokion:* Thee, the all-immaculate Mother of God, do we call blessed, for whose sake the curse was made barren, and deliverance and blessing were given.[^5865]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5866]

*Troparion 1:* May I find thee as a helper, O Virgin, delivering me in the judgment hour of condemnation, when I shall stand before the tribunal of Him who was born of thee.[^5867]

*Troparion 2:* Overcome as I am by the law of sin, O Pure One, and often deceived by the deceits of the stranger, and cast down into the precipices of transgressions: turn me back.[^5868]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst give birth unto the live Coal whom Isaiah beheld: consume the matter of my transgressions, O All-immaculate One, and enlighten me, I pray.[^5869]

*Troparion 4:* Having lent flesh from thy blood unto Christ, O Virgin: cleanse my carnal passions utterly, and show me the path of dispassion.[^5870]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Calm the sea of passions raging with a soul-corrupting tempest, O Master Christ, and bring me up from corruption, as Thou art Compassionate.[^5871]

*Troparion 1:* Lifted up upon the Tree, O Long-suffering One, Thou didst cast down all the haughty risings of the enemy, and didst save him that was fallen, for the sake of Thy great goodness.[^5872]

*Troparion 2:* When the souls of the righteous of old perceived Thee committing Thy soul upon the Tree, O Master Word, they were loosed from eternal bonds.[^5873]

*Martyrikon 1:* As steadfast diamonds, ye endured every kindling of torments with patient disposition, and having humbled yourselves, ye cast down the enemy.[^5874]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having become emulators of Christ's...

...passions having become, ye endured every torment, O Passion-bearers of the Lord; therefore ye were accounted worthy of heavenly splendors.[^5875]
