*Theotokion:* Marvelous is thy childbearing, which works great miracles and is glorified among the saints, O Theotokos, only all-marvelous Lady.[^5876]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5877]

*Troparion 1:* Let not the depth of despondency swallow me up, O Lady, and let not the wave of sin cover me; but by thy prayer may I be saved, O only Pure One.[^5878]

*Troparion 2:* The lampstand of the Law prefigured thee, O All-pure One, who gavest birth unto the Light that enlightened all things; therefore I cry unto thee: Enlighten me who am darkened.[^5879]

*Troparion 3:* O all-pure and good-loving Lady, who didst give birth unto the good Benefactor and Creator: make good my afflicted soul.[^5880]

*Troparion 4:* Thou hast given birth unto the Cause of salvation, the Giver of life, who truly grants eternal deliverance unto us who proclaim thee as Theotokos.[^5881]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame and bedewed the youths, as they sang in unison: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5882]

*Troparion 1:* The law-transgressing council crowned Thee with thorns, O immortal King, who cuttest down from the root the thorny weeds of deception. Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5883]

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest clothe me with the garment of incorruption, O Word, Thou wast stripped naked willingly; and Thou endurest spittings, and crucifixion, and Passion, being impassible by essence.[^5884]

*Martyrikon 1:* Conformed unto the Passions of Him that rules over all, O Saints, ye are truly sons and heirs of the unshakable Kingdom.[^5885]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye brought no honor unto senseless idols of wood, honoring Him who stretched out His hands upon the Tree as King and Master of all.[^5886]

*Theotokion:* Strange is thy childbearing, O All-immaculate One! For thou hast given birth unto God, who by the Tree withered the flame of deception, and enlightened the world.[^5887]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5888]

*Troparion 1:* I sing of thee, O All-hymned One! For seedlessly thou hast given birth unto the supremely hymned God, O most holy Virgin, who deifies those that sing: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5889]

*Troparion 2:* Put my passions to death, O thou who didst give birth unto Life; raise me up who lie in the tomb of insensibility, that with love I may glorify thee, O Bride of God.[^5890]

*Troparion 3:* Look upon the entreaties of thy servants, O Lady, delivering from perils and saving from tribulations those who sing in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^5891]

*Troparion 4:* Thou hast given birth unto the Incalculably Powerful One who bare our infirmity, O Pure One; entreat Him to heal my soul that is grievously enfeebled.[^5892]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee, Creator of all, the youths in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^5893]

*Troparion 1:* O most high God, Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, being given gall to drink, O Sweetness of life! Pierced with a spear, Thou didst slay the serpent that cast Adam down in Paradise.[^5894]

*Troparion 2:* Thou loosest me from the bonds of sin, O Word, being willingly bound, and with eternal bonds binding the apostate enemy, O Savior; therefore do I glorify Thy Passions unto the ages.[^5895]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye were revealed as partakers of the immaterial light, being secondary lights, O holy Martyrs, taking away the darkness of deception, and divinely enlightening the hearts of all the faithful.[^5896]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye became sons of the free Jerusalem above, O Martyrs, enlightening the church of the firstborn, and supremely exalting Christ unto the ages.[^5897]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst stand by the Cross, O Maiden, beholding nailed Christ, unto whom thou hadst given birth, O Lady; therefore thou didst cry aloud: Leave me not childless, O unoriginate Son and Word of the unoriginate Father.[^5898]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5899]

*Troparion 1:* O most fair and divinely radiant Virgin, having beautified me with the vision of good things, enlighten me, that I may cry: Sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^5900]

*Troparion 2:* O Gate of light, open unto me the light-radiant gates of repentance, showing every right path of righteousness that leads unto the entrance of the divine will.[^5901]

*Troparion 3:* Ineffably hast thou given birth unto the Holy Word who sanctifies the faithful, O Pure One; entreat Him, O Most Holy One, to sanctify now my humble soul, which is defiled by evil.[^5902]

*Troparion 4:* A bright stream and fountain of dispassion proceeded from thee, O holy Lady, even the Lord of all; therefore I cry unto thee, O Pure One: Dry up the streams of my wicked deeds with the showers of thy prayers.[^5903]

*My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^5904]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah: a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^5905]

*Troparion 1:* As a lamb Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree, O Master Christ, crushing the jaws of the spiritual wolf, and snatching Thy rational sheep from his mouth, and bringing them, O Master, unto the Father.[^5906]

*Troparion 2:* With a crown of thorns wast Thou bound, O Christ, King of kings, destroying the kingdoms of the evil one, and cutting down the thorns of deception from the root; therefore in faith we glorify Thee, O Good One.[^5907]

*Martyrikon 1:* As the sun, your steadfast resistance shone forth, and destroyed all the enemy's mist, O invincible Martyrs, enlighteners of all the faithful, unbending pillars of piety, praised in glory.[^5908]

*Martyrikon 2:* A divinely gathered company, a heavenly army, a chosen assembly, a holy tabernacle were ye revealed, O all-lauded Martyrs of the Savior, destroying the strongholds of the evil one by divine grace.[^5909]

*Theotokion:* Thou hast given birth unto the Husbandman, the Planter of piety, who plants true understanding upon the earth, and destroys the curse that sprang from the garden; whom magnifying, we call thee blessed, O Virgin.[^5910]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos:*[^5911]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast given birth unto the unshakable Foundation, the Savior, who by divine command founded the earth upon the waters, O Pure One; entreat Him to establish us who purely call thee blessed.[^5912]

*Troparion 2:* Make me to walk unerringly and without deceit in the path of humility of the divine commandments, O Pure One, driving off the turmoil of demons and the assaults of passions, and granting me tranquility.[^5913]

*Troparion 3:* Seeing me held fast by the slumber of negligence, the enemy assails me unceasingly, hoping to steal me away with voluptuous sleep; but do thou thyself...

...preserve me, O Pure One, by thy sleepless prayer, O Virgin Mother.[^5914]

*Troparion 4:* As one self-condemned, I ponder the multitude of my sins and the fearful tribunal of the Judge, whereat I am to be judged; but O thou who didst give birth unto God the Judge, keep me uncondemned at that time, O Theotokos.[^5915]

*It is truly meet: and the Little Ectenia. Then the Photagogicon and the customary Psalms.*[^5916]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^5917]

*Sticheron 1:* No sooner was the Tree of Thy Cross planted, O Christ, than idol deception was driven away and grace blossomed forth; for no longer is there the tyranny of condemnation, but victory of salvation has been revealed unto us. For the Cross is our boast, the Cross is our establishment, the Cross is our rejoicing.[^5918]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: - - - And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^5919]

*Sticheron 2:* For our sake wast Thou led to the sacrifice, O Christ, as a sheep, and as a guileless lamb, Emmanuel, unto voluntary slaughter, and wast numbered with the transgressors. Come, ye kindreds of the nations, sing and bow down before Him who was hanged upon the Cross, the Life unending.[^5920]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and prosper Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou prosper.*[^5921]

*Martyrikon:* The holy Martyrs, having contended upon earth, endured frost, were delivered unto fire, and the water received them. Their voice is: We passed through fire and water, and Thou broughtest us out into refreshment. By their prayers, O Christ God, have mercy on us.[^5922]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*[^5923]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5924]

Standing by the Cross, O Jesus, she who bare Thee lamented grieving, crying aloud: I cannot bear to see Him nailed upon the Tree whom I brought forth, having escaped pains as a virgin; and how am I now held fast by sorrow, and am torn in heart, I the immaculate one? For now is fulfilled the word that Simeon spoke: A sword shall pierce through thine own heart. But now, O my Son, arise, and save those that sing of Thee.[^5925]

*It is good to give praise unto the Lord: Trisagion; after Our Father: troparion...*

...ectenia, and the First Hour, and the dismissal.[^5926]

*On Wednesday, The Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^5927]

*Troparion 1:* The thief on the cross having believed Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom.[^5928]

*Troparion 2:* Having ascended the Cross, Thou didst shake all the multitude of demons, and didst cast down the destructive power of the tormentor, O Master Christ, and didst save mankind.[^5929]

*Troparion 3:* Pierced in Thy ribs, Thou didst pour forth streams of remission, O Christ; and having nailed Thy hands upon the Cross, Thou didst utterly destroy the passionate mind of men.[^5930]

*Martyrikon:* Having become emulators of Christ's Passions, O blessed ones, ye submitted unto every torment, O Passion-bearers of the Lord; therefore ye were accounted worthy of heavenly splendors.[^5931]

*Glory... Triadikon:* Upon the Cross, Thou who art indivisible from the Father and the divine Spirit didst endure voluntary passion, O Christ, taking away all our corruptive passions.[^5932]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Standing by the Cross, she who is full of God's joy, beholding her Son wounded, was wounded in soul; yet she sang of His great dispensation in truth.[^5933]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas

*On Wednesday Evening:*[^5934]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 5:*[^5935]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^5936]

*Sticheron 1:* Having traversed all the earth, ye sowed the divine teachings, carrying only the Word as a lamp and all wealth, O disciples of the Lord, whereby ye put kings and tormentors to shame, and tore apart the fury of philosophers and orators as cobwebs, calling all unto the knowledge of the Creator, and taking away the dark demonic services. Therefore I pray: Deliver me from irrational passions by your prayers.[^5937]

*Sticheron 2:* From the turmoil of temptations, and the grievous falsehood of dishonorable heresies, from demonic evil counsel, and the bitter fellowship of men, and the tempest, O All-blessed ones, from unquenchable fire, and the eternal worm, the gnashing of teeth, and every other torment: deliver all of us by your supplications unto God; and entreat Him that, for our temperance and labors, we may receive the recompense of virtues, the inheritance of the heavenly Kingdom, and great mercy.[^5938]

*Sticheron 3:* Clearly receiving all brightness as a secondary trinitarian light of the ineffable dispensation, as much as was truly possible for human nature, the all-lauded Ten-and-Two were revealed perfectly leading the equally zealous assembly, having with them the Seventy-and-Two, and enlightening all the ends of the world that were darkened by the gloom of evil-minded heresies, entreating Christ, who grants great mercy unto the world.[^5939]

*Other Stichera to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas, Tone 5. The same Podoben (то́йже подо́бенъ):*[^5940]

*Sticheron 1:* Rejoice, O sacred head, pure dwelling-place of virtues, divine rule of blameless priesthood, great and manifest shepherd, bearing the bright name of victory, merciful bender unto those who pray, inclining unto the supplications of the infirm, ready deliverer, saving refuge unto all who with faith ever honor thy glorious memory: entreat Christ to send down great mercy upon us.[^5941]

*Sticheron 2:* Rejoice, O most sacred mind, pure abode of the Trinity, pillar of the Church, establishment of the faithful, helper of the afflicted, star who by the flashes of acceptable prayers dost ever disperse the darkness of temptations and afflictions, O Hierarch Nicholas, calm haven wherein those tossed by the surges of life find salvation when they flee thither: entreat Christ to send down great mercy upon our souls.[^5942]

*Sticheron 3:* Rejoice, rule among hierarchs, inexhaustible deep of divine miracles, beauty of the Church, bright star! Illumining each one of us with thy sacred flashes, O Blessed One, most holy one, enlightened by all-radiant lightnings, unconquerable pillar, degree of faith: entreat Christ to grant great mercy unto our souls, as we celebrate thy honorable memory in love.[^5943]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, seal of the Prophets, and proclamation of the divinely speaking Apostles! For truly thou hast given birth unto the incarnate God beyond understanding and word, O Pure One, through whom having received our primal nobility, and...

...enjoying the food of Paradise, we honor thee with hymns as the mediatress of such brightness and acceptable intercessor. Enriched by thee, O All-pure One, we are accounted worthy of the ever-existing life of thy Son, who richly grants great mercy.[^5944]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*[^5945]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 5:*[^5946]

*Sticheron 1:* Disciples of the Savior, having been eyewitnesses of mysteries, ye proclaimed Him who is invisible and has no beginning, saying: In the beginning was the Word. Ye were not created before the Angels, nor were ye taught by men, but by the Wisdom from on high; therefore having boldness, pray for our souls.[^5947]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: - - - So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion upon us.*[^5948]

*Sticheron 2:* Let us praise the Apostles of the Lord in unison with hymns; for having clothed themselves with the armor of the Cross, they abolished idol deception, and were revealed as victorious crown-bearers. By their prayers and of all the Saints, O God, have mercy on us.[^5949]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; especially has our soul been filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^5950]

*Martyrikon:* With insatiable love of soul, ye did not deny Christ, O holy Martyrs, who having endured grievous and diverse wounds of passions, cast down the audacity of the tormentors, and having preserved the faith unbending and uninjured, passed over unto heaven; therefore having boldness unto Him, ask that great mercy be granted unto us.[^5951]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* As is due, we the faithful call thee blessed, O Virgin Theotokos, and we glorify thee as an unshakable city, an insurmountable rampart, a firm intercessor, and refuge of our souls.[^5952]

*Then, Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: and the rest.*[^5953]

*At Wednesday Small Compline:*[^5954]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 5:*[^5955]

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God the Savior, who guided the people in the sea with dryshod feet, and drowned Pharaoh with his entire army, unto Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.[^5956]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^5957]

*Troparion 1:* Light-bearing tabernacle of Christ the King, illuminate my mind...[^5958]

...darkened by the deceit of the adversary, and blinded by the darkness of my transgressions.[^5959]

*Troparion 2:* Free my humble soul from wicked thoughts, O Mother of God, and make it a dwelling-place for God, that I may worthily glorify thee always.[^5960]

*Glory...:* When by God's command I am about to depart from temporal life, O All-pure One, show me to be higher than demonic hands, granting me Angels as companions.[^5961]

*Both now...:* Why, O my soul, hast thou spent all thy life in great despondency? Hasten therefore henceforth, and cry unto the Mother of the Lord: Cleanse and save me, O Mother of God.[^5962]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* By the power of Thy Cross, O Christ, establish my thought, to sing and glorify Thy three-day Resurrection.[^5963]

*Troparion 1:* Raise me up out of the pit of passions and torments, who sing of thee without doubting, O all-blessed Theotokos.[^5964]

*Troparion 2:* Rend the sackcloth of my boundless transgressions, and gird me with the gladness of virtue, O thou who art full of God's grace.[^5965]

*Glory...:* Grant me a drop of tears, O Pure One, that I may drive away the perplexity of my heart, and diligently sing of thee.[^5966]

*Both now...:* Cast down the arrogance of bodiless enemies, O All-immaculate One, and quickly deliver me from their torment.[^5967]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard the report of the power of the Cross, that Paradise was opened by it, and I cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^5968]

*Troparion 1:* Why hast thou likened thyself, O soul, unto the barren fig tree, in nowise fearing felling and the eternal flame? Make haste therefore before the end, and arise.[^5969]

*Troparion 2:* What tongue shall declare the boundless sea of the evils I have committed, and the depth of my transgressions? Save me, O all-immaculate Virgin, who am in despair.[^5970]

*Glory...:* I weep for myself when I come to my senses, because of my many transgressions and the unquenchable fire; and I entreat thee to grant me time for repentance, O Pure One.[^5971]

*Both now...:* Let not the perverse enemy seize my wretched soul like a lion; but by thy power crush his soul-destroying teeth, O Good One.[^5972]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early we cry unto Thee, O Lord: Save us! For Thou art our God, for beside Thee we know no other.[^5973]

*Troparion 1:* Look down and give ear, O Lady, unto my voice, and deliver me, I pray, from eternal condemnation.[^5974]

*Troparion 2:* I am wounded with the arrows of sin, and I cry unto thee: Heal the wounds of my heart, O All-pure One.[^5975]

*Glory...:* Have mercy on me, O only compassionate Lover of mankind, by the prayers of Her who bare Thee; for Thou art my God and Lord.[^5976]

*Both now...:* Have mercy on me, O only All-hymned One, I beseech thy goodness, and vouchsafe me mercy.[^5977]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The abyss encompassed me, the sea-monster was a grave unto me; but I cried unto Thee, the Lover of mankind, and Thy right hand saved me, O Lord.[^5978]

*Troparion 1:* As a roaring lion, the deceitful one seeks to tear me apart and make me food for his malice, O Pure One; but deliver me from his harm.[^5979]

*Troparion 2:* Cleanse, O Lover of mankind, by the prayers of Her who purely bare Thee, and deliver Thy world from every affliction, and vouchsafe it eternal glory.[^5980]

*Glory...:* The abyss of transgressions has encompassed me, bringing me down into the depth of despair; I pray thee, O Pure One, raise me up from nethermost Hades, who glorify thee.[^5981]

*Both now...:* My life is filled with transgressions and all slothfulness; therefore, turn me unto repentance before my end, O Pure One, and save me, O All-hymned One.[^5982]

*Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...:*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:*
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Co-beginningless Word" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*
*Rejoice, firm, God-conquering wall! Rejoice, all-wretched downfall of the barbarians! Rejoice, uplifting of the faithful, Orthodox people, O Theotokos, who truly place our hope in thee: delivering us by thy prayers from all afflictions and godless enemies.*[^5983]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Thou who didst save the hymn-singing Children in the furnace of fire: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^5984]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me by thy goodness, O Lady, forgiveness of involuntary and voluntary evils.[^5985]

*Troparion 2:* My mind fails under the assaults of the impiety of wicked thoughts; help me, O Good One.[^5986]

*Glory...:* Heal now by the remedies of thy prayers, O Theotokos, the wounds of my spiritual passions.[^5987]

*Both now...:* Grant me a contrite soul and a humble mindset, O Good One, that I may glorify thee.[^5988]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him who before the ages was begotten of the Father as Son and God, and in these latter times was incarnate of the Virgin Mother, O ye priests, praise; O ye peoples, supremely exalt unto all ages.[^5989]

*Troparion 1:* Having ended my life in slothfulness, wretch that I am, and drawn near unto the end of my life, I cry unto thee, O Theotokos: Snatch my humble soul from the nets of the adversaries.[^5990]

*Troparion 2:* Tormented by bodily diseases, by grievous wounds of soul, and by the assault of passions: grant me the power of thy kingdom, as the only compassionate one.[^5991]

*Glory...:* Being tempest-tossed in the deep of life and submerged by demonic waves, save me, O Pure One, and by divine striving lead me into a life pleasing unto God, O Maiden.[^5992]

*Both now...:* Gabriel, being sent, entering as into a bridal chamber, O Virgin, cried out, saying: Rejoice, O most glorious palace of Christ, the King of all, who having taken up abode within thee, deified all earthly beings.[^5993]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who beyond mind and word art the Mother of God, who in time didst ineffably bear the Timeless One, we the faithful unanimously magnify.[^5994]

*Troparion 1:* Thee who ineffably didst bear God, have we acquired as intercessor, and immovable wall, and salvation of souls, and fountain of miracles.[^5995]

*Troparion 2:* Be merciful unto me, O Word of the true God, in the day of judgment, through the supplications of Her who bare Thee, and number me with those at Thy right hand.[^5996]

*Glory...:* Deliver me, O Pure One, who have placed my hope in thee, from the eternal fire, and from the sleepless worm, and from every torment.[^5997]

*Both now...:* When my soul is about to be separated from my wretched body, deliver me from the torment of invisible enemies, O Bride of God.[^5998]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion; after Our Father: troparia.*[^5999]

*On Thursday at Matins:*[^6000]

*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns to the Apostles, Tone 5:*[^6001]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* As eyewitnesses and servants of the Word, let us praise the all-wise Apostles with spiritual songs...

...and songs, all ye earthly-born: for they diligently pray unto Christ for us who sing of their sacred memory and bow down before their relics.[^6002]

*Verse: Their sound has gone forth into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world.*[^6003]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In unison let us praise the Apostles, as eyewitnesses of the Word and divine preachers, and spiritual fishers of the nations, who manifestly brought us unto the knowledge of Christ: having delivered the human race from deception, they made them worthy of the Kingdom.[^6004]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* I am condemned by the judgment of my conscience, and pondering the judgment before the judgment, I, wretch that I am, tremble, remembering the multitude of my wicked deeds; but unto thee, the invincible one, I cry with compunction, and as my protectress and shelter: deliver me from that judgment, and save me by thy prayers.[^6005]

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^6006]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* In unison let us praise the Apostles, who proclaimed unto all the Orthodox teaching of the Lord, and drove away the heretical gloom: having made spiritual enlightenment shine forth in the world by the teaching of grace, they pray that we may be saved.[^6007]

*Verse: The heavens declare the glory of God, and the firmament proclaims the work of His hands.*[^6008]

*And speak the same Sessional Hymn again.*[^6009]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^6010]

*Martyrikon:* At the achievements of the holy Martyrs, the Heavenly Hosts were exceedingly astonished: how, having fought well in a mortal body, they invisibly conquered the bodiless enemy by the power of the Cross! And they pray unto the Lord that He have mercy on our souls.[^6011]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Having found thee, O Theotokos, as a haven, and a wall, and a refuge, and hope, and shelter, and fervent intercession, we the faithful flee unto thee, and cry diligently, and call out faithfully: Have mercy, O Theotokos, on those who place their hope in thee, and deliver us from transgressions.[^6012]

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^6013]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "Co-beginningless Word" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Disciples of the Savior, divine Apostles, who sowed the saving word unto the ends of the earth, and enlightened those sitting in darkness and the shadow of death: enlighten my soul that is darkened by the blackness of passions, through your supplications, O all-praised ones.[^6014]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord, the unwise who proclaimed the ineffable mystery of Thine Incarnation put philosophers to shame; fishermen silenced orators, and became the all-wise teachers of the nations, having enlightened the ends of the world with the light of divine knowledge; through them grant great mercy unto us.[^6015]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Being a shelter from diverse temptations and circumstances unto those who unhesitatingly have hope in thee, O all-pure Virgin: deliver us from cruel perils, praying unto thy Son together with His Apostles, and save all who sing of thee.[^6016]

*Canon to the Holy Apostles. A creation of Theophanes, Bishop of Nicaea:*[^6017]

*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Christ who crushes wars with a high arm shook horse and rider into the Red Sea; but He saved Israel, who sang a song of victory.[^6018]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*[^6019]

*Troparion 1:* Having been enriched, O glorious and divine Apostles, with the radiance of the primal gift of light of Him who deigned in the flesh to converse with men: destroy all darkness of my soul.[^6020]

*Troparion 2:* Having bent His divine bow, Christ sent you as arrows into the whole world, O Apostles: arrows crushing all the schemes of the crafty one, and healing the wounds of the faithful.[^6021]

*Troparion 3:* Having Wisdom Itself as teacher, ye made all the ends of the earth wise, O Apostles. Therefore make me wise to drive away every evil deed of the enemy.[^6022]

*Theotokion:* O only blessed one made glad of God, who by blessings didst make the human race rejoice: entreat Christ together with the divine Apostles, O Pure One, that He have mercy on us.[^6023]

*Another Canon to our Father among the Saints, Nicholas the Wonderworker. (Whose Acrostic is: A fifth song do I offer unto thee, O Nicholas). A creation of Joseph:*[^6024]

*Tone 5. The same Heirmos:*[^6025]

*Troparion 1:* Having appropriated thyself unto God in faith and love, O Father, thou didst fulfill His all-holy desires, through which thou wast holy in every way, O wise Hierarch Nicholas.[^6026]

*Troparion 2:* Having thee as intercessor before the Compassionate One, we who are held fast by temptations and sorrows flee unto thee: grant a hand saving us from every constraint.[^6027]

*Troparion 3:* Christ anointed thee as hierarch of Myra, sweetening us with the fragrance of miracles; therefore we pray thee, Nicholas, to deliver us from the stench...

...of sin.[^6028]

*Theotokion:* Of old the choir of Prophets named thee the divine mountain, O Virgin, and the impassable door; therefore we pray thee: Open unto us the divine doors of repentance, O Maiden.[^6029]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend what is uncontrollably weighed down: establish Thy Church, O Christ, upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O only Good One and Lover of mankind.[^6030]

*Troparion 1:* He who became poor in the flesh, being abundantly rich in goodness, enriched you, O glorious Apostles, who became poor for His sake, with all gifts, enriching the ends of the earth with divine and honorable knowledge.[^6031]

*Troparion 2:* I am wounded by the poisonous biting of the serpent, and possess a wounded heart; therefore I cry unto Thee, O Christ, who wast wounded for my sake: Heal and save me, I pray, through the supplications of Thine Apostles.[^6032]

*Troparion 3:* Having drawn me up with the net of your prayers, O All-blessed ones, from the deep of the malice of the censurer, and from the surging of thoughts, and from deadly passions, bring me saved unto the God of all.[^6033]

*Theotokion:* O thou who didst receive the heavenly rain, entreat Him together with the Apostles to stop the torrents of my passions, drying up the flood of my sin, and to save me who purely glorify thee.[^6034]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6035]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst blunt the arrows of the crafty one, O Venerable One, by the resistance of thy divine labors; but by thy prayers, O All-wise One, preserve unharmed from his malicious deed and violence those who sing of thee, O great Nicholas.[^6036]

*Troparion 2:* Having shown forth an Angelic life upon earth, now with the Angels thou dost ever stand before the Trinitarian throne, O Holy One: asking remission of our transgressions and temptations, O Father, chief hierarch Nicholas.[^6037]

*Troparion 3:* Drive away all the darkening of my mind, O Nicholas, by thy light-bearing prayers; calm the tempest of passions; guide me, O Father, unto the haven of dispassion, I pray, that in praise I may glorify thee.[^6038]

*Theotokion:* Standing at the right hand of Christ as Queen, arrayed in truth with golden woven borders, O thou who art graced of God: through thy prayers intercede for the heavenly kingdom for us, O Maiden all-immaculate.[^6039]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Understanding foresightedly Thy divine emptying, O Christ, Habakkuk cried out unto Thee with trembling: Unto the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed, art Thou come.[^6040]

*Troparion 1:* Being the Door, Jesus our God and Lord, having opened the knowledge of Himself unto the Apostles, opened the door unto all nations through their teachings.[^6041]

*Troparion 2:* O Son of God, Thou hast revealed Thine Apostles to be sons by participation of Thy heavenly Father; through their supplication make all of us sons of light.[^6042]

*Troparion 3:* Ye who are to sit most gloriously upon twelve thrones with the Judge and King: deliver me, O Apostles, from the fearful and dread judgment.[^6043]

*Theotokion:* O ark of divine sanctification, sanctify my soul; enlighten my mind, ever entreating Christ with the Apostles that He save me.[^6044]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6045]

*Troparion 1:* With passing things didst thou exchange future things; make us also partakers thereof by thy holy prayers, O Nicholas, delivering us from every worldly temptation.[^6046]

*Troparion 2:* Having been chief primate of Myra, O Nicholas, sweeten all the senses of my heart with the myrrh, O Holy One; and by thy prayers ever drive away foul-smelling passions therefrom.[^6047]

*Troparion 3:* Crush the visible wiles of the enemy, O Nicholas, which ever wage war against us; send our enemies into endless destruction.[^6048]

*Theotokion:* Holy Theotokos, help me, who am continually submerged by bodily pleasures, and ever lie groaning upon the bed of despondency.[^6049]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee do I rise early and unto Thee do I call: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Tender-hearted One.[^6050]

*Troparion 1:* Spiritual orators, honorable Apostles, in the upper room in the appearance of fire, dreadfully received the Holy Spirit who had come unto them.[^6051]

*Troparion 2:* Crushing impiety, heal with the dew of healing, O Apostles, my mind that is crushed by transgressions...

...heal with the dew of healing, O Apostles.[^6052]

*Troparion 3:* Christ sent you, O Apostles, as chosen arrows that crush the arrows of wickedness; therefore heal me who am wounded with the arrows of the enemy.[^6053]

*Theotokion:* Condemn me not, nor cast me away from Thy face, O Plenteous in mercy: She who purely bare Thee and the Apostolic choir entreat Thee.[^6054]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6055]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst shatter the idols of the images, O Holy One, and didst show the counsels of the heretics to be vain, O Nicholas, and didst deliver those led unto death.[^6056]

*Troparion 2:* Rising early unto the Lord from youth, O Venerable One, thou wast wholly enlightened by the outpourings of light from there. Therefore I pray: Disperse the clouds of my soul.[^6057]

*Troparion 3:* We pray thee, O Father Nicholas: At this hour be in the midst of all who call upon thee, and grant us our requests that lead unto salvation.[^6058]

*Theotokion:* He who is incomprehensible to the mind, O thou who art full of God's grace, becoming flesh-bearing, accepted thee as His enclosure, delivering men from the afflictions that hold them fast.[^6059]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Calm the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, and lead me up from corruption, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^6060]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stop the spreading corruption of malice in the souls of the nations, O Lover of mankind, with the salt of the teaching of Thy sacred disciples.[^6061]

*Troparion 2:* Thou knowest the depth of my wicked deeds, O Master Christ: give me Thy hand, and save me, O Lover of mankind, through the supplications of Thy sacred Apostles.[^6062]

*Troparion 3:* O most righteous Judge, in the day of the fearful judgment deliver me from trembling condemnation, through the supplications of Thy gloriously praised Apostles.[^6063]

*Theotokion:* Vouchsafe salvation unto me, O Lord, who am in despair because of the multitude of mine iniquities, through the supplications of Thy disciples and of Thy Mother.[^6064]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6065]

*Troparion 1:* Make the Master merciful through thy prayers, O Nicholas, unto all who honor thee, that He may grant remission of our transgressions.[^6066]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver from disease and worldly stumbling-blocks, temptations and sorrows, O Nicholas, those who have acquired thee as an intercessor before the Lord.[^6067]

*Troparion 3:* Christ the Master showed thee to be an excellent physician; therefore heal the diseases of those who piously approach thee, O Nicholas.[^6068]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast an unwedded parent, Mother of God, O Pure One; therefore in faith I pray thee: Disperse the despondency of my soul.[^6069]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame, and bedewed the Children, who sang in unison: O God, blessed art Thou.[^6070]

*Troparion 1:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers exalted you, and cast down all the power of the enemy, O disciples and God-seers of Christ.[^6071]

*Troparion 2:* Wash away the defilement of my heart with streams of compunction, through your supplications, O Apostles, teaching me to cry: O God, blessed art Thou.[^6072]

*Troparion 3:* With the fire of the Holy Spirit ye consumed the substance of all idolatry; therefore deliver me from the burning Gehenna, O disciples of God the Word.[^6073]

*Theotokion:* O thou who art the restoration of Adam's fall: raise me up who have fallen into the pit of malice, by thy prayers, O Virgin, and of the divine Apostles.[^6074]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6075]

*Troparion 1:* Having committed thyself entirely unto God, O Nicholas, save me entirely, who ever fall wretchedly into worldly passions, O divinely wise one.[^6076]

*Troparion 2:* O most divinely radiant luminary! Enlighten my mind that is continually darkened by the gloom of passions, and grant me to walk becomingly in life.[^6077]

*Troparion 3:* Stop every deceitful mouth opened against me, O Nicholas, by thy prayers; and deliver me from enemies visible and invisible.[^6078]

*Theotokion:* From thee did the never-setting Sun shine forth unto us, O all-radiant cloud, even Christ our God, enlightening those who were in the darkness of ignorance, O Theotokos.[^6079]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee the Creator of all, the Children in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^6080]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst spread out the Apostles as noetic clouds, O Word, dropping upon us the rain of all-wise and divine teachings, and watering us unto all ages.[^6081]

*Troparion 2:* O most excellent pillars of the Church, who uphold her by the teachings of faith: by your divine craftsmanship establish the rotted dwelling of my soul, O God-seers.[^6082]

*Troparion 3:* Groan, O my soul, and offer streams of tears from thy whole heart unto the Lord, crying: O only Compassionate One, save and cleanse me through the acceptable prayers of the all-glorious Apostles.[^6083]

*Theotokion:* O chosen Zion, royal city: make me a citizen of the city on high, entreating together with the divine disciples, O all-pure Virgin, thy timeless Son.[^6084]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6085]

*Troparion 1:* Having been great chief hierarch in Myra, O Nicholas, sweeten the senses of my soul with the myrrh, that I may escape the stench of passions, and receive the grace of the Comforter.[^6086]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst stop the stream of the blasphemies of Arius, O all-wise one, with the streams of thy holy tongue. Therefore I cry unto thee: Dry up the floods of my passions by thy prayers, O all-blessed Nicholas.[^6087]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver us from transgressions, through thy prayers, from demonic affliction, from pagan captivity, and from the most crafty evil harm of all men; that we may praise thee as our deliverer.[^6088]

*Triadikon:* Glorifying in faith unceasingly the Trinity in Monarchy, let us cry aloud: O Father, and Word, and All-Holy Spirit! We sing praises unto Thee unto all ages.[^6089]

*Theotokion:* God who was clothed in flesh for our sake from thee, O pure and all-immaculate one, showed thee forth unto all our race, O Virgin, as a divine intercessor. Therefore we the faithful sing aloud unto thee.[^6090]

*Then we sing: My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^6091]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah: a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; Whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^6092]

*Troparion 1:* Ye appeared noetically as heavens bestarred with the brightness of divine virtues, having Christ in your midst as the Sun, and ye renewed the ends of the earth, O All-wise ones; therefore are ye called blessed.[^6093]

*Troparion 2:* Bearing the wounds of Christ upon your divine bodies as all-comely adornment, O All-wise ones: heal by your intercessions unto the Lord my soul that is wounded by demonic arrows.[^6094]

*Troparion 3:* As Thou didst raise Lazarus, O Christ, through Thy disciples save me, O Word, O Lord, who lie in the nether pit of sin and have weighed down my soul with the sleep of grievous sloth.[^6095]

*Theotokion:* Direct the goings of my soul, O Word of God, unto the unerring paths of Thy commandments, having as intercessors the all-pure Virgin who bare Thee and Thine all-wise Apostles, O Plenteous in mercy.[^6096]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. The same Heirmos:*[^6097]

*Troparion 1:* Having become a divine hierarch, thou didst keep all the commandments of Christ; therefore thou wast a divine guardian unto the faithful, O Father Nicholas; preserve them from every temptation and affliction.[^6098]

*Troparion 2:* As once thou didst nourish thy city that was fainting with famine, as a good shepherd, O Venerable One: so also now nourish my soul with noetic bread, O Father Nicholas, who have acquired thee as a good intercessor.[^6099]

*Troparion 3:* Thee, the great sun ever abiding upon the height of the Church of Christ, O venerable shepherd, we faithfully beseech: by the light-bearing flashes of light, drive away the deep darkness of sin from our souls.[^6100]

*Troparion 4:* As it is written, the fearful day of Christ's coming is drawn near; bestir thyself, O my soul, cast away sloth, and fervently cry out unto Christ: Save me, O Lord, through the supplication of Thy Nicholas.[^6101]

*Theotokion:* The Prophet foresaw thee, O All-pure One, as a bright lampstand bearing the noetic candle, Christ, by Whom we who lie in darkness and passions have been enlightened: we call thee blessed, O Theotokos Ever-Virgin.[^6102]

*It is truly meet: Little Litany, Photogogikon, and the customary Psalms.*[^6103]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 5:*[^6104]

*Sticheron 1:* Having been eyewitnesses of the mysteries, O disciples of the Savior, ye proclaimed the Invisible One who hath no beginning, saying: In the beginning was the Word. Ye were not created before the Angels, nor were ye taught of men, but by the wisdom from on high; therefore, having boldness, pray for our souls.[^6105]

*Sticheron 2:* In hymns let us praise the Apostles of the Lord in unison; for having put on the armor of the Cross, they abolished the deception of idols and were revealed as victorious crown-bearers. Through their prayers and of all the Saints, O God, have mercy on us.[^6106]

*Sticheron 3:* When in torments, the Saints cried out rejoicing: These are for us a transaction unto the Master; for instead of the wounds upon our bodies, a radiant garment shall blossom forth for us in the resurrection; instead of dishonor, crowns; instead of prison bonds, Paradise; and instead of condemnation with evildoers, life with the Angels. Through their prayers, O Lord, save our souls.[^6107]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* We call thee blessed, O Virgin Theotokos, for from thee hath shone forth the righteous Sun, Christ, who hath great mercy.[^6108]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion of the Saint, if there be one; litany and the First Hour.*[^6109]

*On Thursday, The Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^6110]

*Troparion 1:* The thief on the Cross, believing Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^6111]

*Troparion 2:* Like radiant clouds ye traversed the earth, O divine disciples, sprinkling the living water and richly watering hearts melted by transgressions.[^6112]

*Troparion 3:* Like mystical rays of the Sun that shone forth from the pure Virgin, ye enlightened those sitting in the darkness of ignorance, O divine disciples of Christ our God.[^6113]

*Troparion 4:* Having endured with fervent soul the burnings of grievous torments, O All-wise ones, ye consumed the deception of idols and passed over unto divine comfort, O Saints.[^6114]

*Troparion 5:* That Thou mightest take up the coin buried in the depth of transgressions, O Christ, and bring it unto the Father, Thou didst make the Apostles preachers by the divine Spirit.[^6115]

*Glory... Triadikon:* O transcendent Trinity: Father unoriginate, co-unoriginate Son, and Holy Spirit, one Godhead! Through the prayers of the preachers of all, preserve Thy Church.[^6116]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Being the adornment of the Apostles, O thou who art full of God's joy, enlighten me who am darkened by worldly pleasures with the rays of repentance, that I may magnify thee.[^6117]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross

*On Thursday Evening:*[^6118]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^6119]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "O venerable father" (Преподо́бне ѻ҆́ч҃е):*[^6120]

*Sticheron 1:* Let all the trees of the forest rejoice, beholding the all-honorable Wood, filled with joy by the Master's Passion, shining forth grace like a flame of fire, pouring forth gifts like waters unto all, enlightening souls and thoughts, healing diseases, and driving away invisible passions: visibly conquering foreign nations, ever granting victories unto monarchs over barbarians, and unto the faithful blessing and great mercy.[^6121]

*Sticheron 2:* Worshipping in faith the place where the feet of the Lord stood, as the Prophet said, we glorify Christ who was crucified and crucified our transgressions together with Himself, who abolished the curse from the tree and reconciled unto the Father those standing far off in thoughts: kissing also His hand- and foot-nails, the spear and the reed, the sponge and the crown; we honor with reverence the insults, scoffs, and all else that He submitted to, whereby we were saved.[^6122]

*Sticheron 3:* With all our members let us be crucified together with Christ, and let us die unto the world; and desiring to walk in the footsteps of Christ the Master of the world, let us bear the divine Cross upon our shoulders, by the rejection...

...of the leaping of the flesh and of evil desires that draw the soul unto sin; thinking to stand before Him, and to behold Him nailed to the Cross, and with expiration committing His soul into the hands of the Father, that we may ever be inseparable with Him.[^6123]

*Other Stichera to the Theotokos, Tone and Podoben the same:*[^6124]

*Sticheron 1:* When thou didst nurture the unplanted cluster of grapes as a vine, beholding Him suspended upon the Wood and pierced with a spear in His divine ribs: What is this, she said, O my Son and my God? How dost Thou, who healest all diseases and passions, endure passions, being impassible according to Thy divine nature? What is this that the ungrateful people have rendered unto Thee, their Benefactor, instead of benefits? cried the All-pure One. But through His Passion pray unceasingly that I be delivered from passions, that I may glorify thee.[^6125]

*Sticheron 2:* Let us all weep, and stretch forth our hands, and beat our breasts, warmly weeping: and bending our knees, striking the ground with our faces earnestly, let us send up sighs unto the height, we who by unseemly deeds have provoked God by rejecting His commandments, (crying out) that in the future judgment He deliver from all torment those who grieved Thee and turn back unto Thee, through the supplications of Thy Mother: for whose sake Thou didst endure the Cross, having become incarnate, and make them partakers of Thy Kingdom.[^6126]

*Sticheron 3:* Unto whom hast thou become likened, O my soul, ever advancing in worse things, and foolishly adding more sores unto thy wounds, so that the wound is over the whole body: not thinking that the Judge is drawing near, before Whom thou shalt stand to receive the retribution and torments according to thy deeds? But turn back and fall down before the Virgin, crying: O Sovereign Lady, Sovereign Lady! Despise me not, who have angered the gracious God born of thee for the salvation of men, and crucified in the flesh.[^6127]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:*

*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ):*[^6128]

As a ransom for our sake and a great price, Thou wast pleased to give Thy most pure blood, O my sinless Christ, desiring that all attain salvation. Therefore, beholding Thee nailed, Thy Mother, weeping, tore her hair, saying: O Child, all-blameless Lamb! Desiring to deliver the world by Thy precious blood, how hast Thou set from mine eyes, O Savior, never-setting Sun? Granting enlightenment unto all, and peace, and great mercy.[^6129]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 5:*[^6130]

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, when of old under the Prophet Moses only the prefiguration of Thy Cross was shown, it conquered Thine enemies; but now, holding Thy Cross itself,...

...we ask for help: strengthen Thy Church, and grant victory unto our Emperor [Name],[^6131] as unto Constantine, for the sake of Thy manifold mercy, O Lover of mankind.[^6132]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, and as the eyes of a maiden unto the hands of her mistress, so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^6133]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy Cross, O Christ, even though it be wood visible in its essence, is nevertheless clothed with divine power: and appearing sensibly unto the world, it noetically works our salvation through wonders; worshipping it, we glorify Thee, O Savior: have mercy on us.[^6134]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the opulent, and the contempt of the proud.*[^6135]

*Martyrikon:* Caring nought for all earthly things, and manfully braving torments, ye failed not of your blessed hopes; but ye became heirs of the heavenly Kingdom, O all-praised Martyrs! Having boldness unto the God who loves mankind, ask peace for the world, and for our souls great mercy.[^6136]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding of old her Lamb and Son lifted up upon the Cross, the Virgin-Mother and all-blessed Maiden cried out weeping: Woe is me, O my Son! How dost Thou die, who art God by nature immortal?[^6137]

*Now dismissest Thou: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion of the Saint, litany, and dismissal.*[^6138]

*On Thursday at Compline:*[^6139]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^6140]
Christ who shatters battles with a high arm shook off horse and rider into the Red Sea; and He saved Israel as they sang a song of victory.[^6141]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^6142]

*Troparion 1:* We know thee beyond compare as another noetic Paradise, O Virgin Bride of God, surpassing the Paradise in Eden, O Mother of God: for thou hast budded forth incorruption unto men.[^6143]

*Troparion 2:* Adam was forbidden to find the tree of life in Paradise, having partaken of the tree of knowledge; but He who received flesh from thee, O Virgin, granted immortality.[^6144]

*Glory:* First was Adam formed from the earth by the most pure hands of...

...the Almighty; but from thee, O Virgin Theotokos, the new Adam was born without seed, the Creator of mankind.[^6145]

*Both now:* The utter abyss of immeasurable transgressions hath compassed me about, and drags me down into the deep of grievous despair. O thou who didst bear the abyss of tender-heartedness, make haste and save me.[^6146]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and didst suspend what is uncontrollably weighed down: establish Thy Church, O Christ, upon the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O only Good One and Lover of mankind.[^6147]

*Troparion 1:* He who made the earth to be the parent of living plants, which were not upon it before: forthwith from a woman that knew not man, being incarnate without seed as from the earth, Thou didst come, O immutable Word of God.[^6148]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art the hope and help of earthborn mortals, and joy, and protection, and refuge, O Sovereign Lady, Mother of Life; therefore we pray: Send down thy help unto all who sing unto thee.[^6149]

*Glory:* We who are sick and held fast by grievous temptations, O Creator, Compassionate God of all things, bring Thine all-holy tabernacle unto Thee in prayer, and we cry unto Thee: Loosen the afflictions of Thy servants.[^6150]

*Both now:* Grievously doth the wave of sin trouble me, dragging me down into the depth of transgressions, O All-pure One, and the tempest of adverse thoughts buffets my soul: O thou who didst bear the Helmsman, make haste to rescue thy servant.[^6151]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Understanding foresightedly Thy divine emptying, O Christ, Habakkuk cried out unto Thee with trembling: Unto the salvation of Thy people, to save Thine anointed, art Thou come.[^6152]

*Troparion 1:* Eve, submitting unto the serpent, bare sorrow unto women; but thou, O Virgin, having believed the divine utterances, didst blossom forth joy unto all the world.[^6153]

*Troparion 2:* First from the rib of Adam was Eve born, but now God is born from a Mother, the Bride of God; Whom incarnate the Virgin bare without an earthly father.[^6154]

*Glory:* Eve rejoices, for the Maiden showed her delivered from the ancient maternal condemnation, having...

...incorruptibly conceived the Judge, and borne Him, the Compassionate One.[^6155]

*Both now:* My life is filled with transgressions, and my mind is passionate, and my soul is condemned; therefore by thy tender compassion have mercy on me, and save me, O Sovereign Lady.[^6156]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou who clothest Thyself with light as with a garment, unto Thee do I rise early and unto Thee do I call: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as the only Tender-hearted One.[^6157]

*Troparion 1:* Having found thee as a pillar and cloud of light, O Virgin Mother of the Light-giver, we who walk through the deceitful wilderness escape from evils.[^6158]

*Troparion 2:* Rejoice, O thou who didst bear God in the flesh, who was born before the ages of the Father without flesh, and who forthwith was pleased to appear unto us.[^6159]

*Glory:* The sun was surpassed by thy brightness, O Mary; for Thou didst clasp in thine arms Him who adorned heaven with luminaries, and didst nourish Him from thy breasts.[^6160]

*Both now:* Condemn me not unto the unquenchable fire, O Christ my Savior, through the entreaties of the pure One who bare Thee.[^6161]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Calm the sea of passions raging with a soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, and lead me up from corruption, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^6162]

*Troparion 1:* Him who of old saved the Prophet Jonah in the whale, didst thou bear unharmed in thy womb, the marvelous God, O pure Ever-Virgin.[^6163]

*Troparion 2:* Bearing the Lord who rules the sea, O all-pure Virgin, as the Powers bear God incomprehensibly, thou didst lull to sleep the salty surge of deception.[^6164]

*Glory:* Having borne the Peace of all things, Christ, O All-pure One, through thy prayers unto Him graciously calm the unsteady tempest of passions that assaults me.[^6165]

*Both now:* In the day of sorrow, when I am about to be loosed from bodily bonds, stand by me, and snatch me away from demonic possession.[^6166]

*Then, Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... both now...*[^6167]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:* Beholding Thee upon the Cross, O Christ, Thy Mother, hanging voluntarily in the midst of thieves, torn as a mother in her womb, said: O sinless Son, how...

...unjustly on the Cross, like an evildoer, art Thou crucified? Desiring to bring the human race to life, as Thou art supremely good.[^6168]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The supremely exalted Lord of the fathers quenched the flame, and bedewed the Children, who sang in unison: O God, blessed art Thou.[^6169]

*Troparion 1:* God fulfilled His promise made unto the forefather Abraham, having become thy Son: and He blessed his seed among the nations, O Blessed One.[^6170]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art the undefiled ladder of Jacob; for God inscribed thee alone as Mother, for whose sake God gathered together his offspring.[^6171]

*Glory:* The Son, who is supremely exalted with the Father and the Spirit, having chosen thee, O Pure One, loved thee greatly as a dwelling for Himself, becoming incarnate.[^6172]

*Both now:* Ever entreat God, Whom thou didst bear, to save me who am in despair, and to obtain forgiveness for me who cry in faith: O God, blessed art Thou.[^6173]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto Thee the Creator of all, the Children in the furnace, having woven a universal choir, sang: All ye works, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^6174]

*Troparion 1:* Thy childbearing escapes examination, O Virgin, and is revealed through faith unto those who cry: All ye works of the Lord, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^6175]

*Troparion 2:* Having in no wise fallen from virginal glory, thou wast enriched with maternal honor, O unwedded One: He knows who visibly wrought these wonders. Whom we supremely exalt unto the ages.[^6176]

*Glory:* Behold, O Good and All-pure One, the affliction of my humble soul: and change my sickness quickly, that I may glorify thee unto the ages.[^6177]

*Both now:* Receive graciously Thy Mother, O Word, who prays Thee to save Thy people, O Good One, whom Thou didst acquire by Thy precious blood: that we may bless Thee unto all ages.[^6178]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, O Isaiah: a Virgin had in womb and bare a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man, Orient is His name; Whom magnifying, we call the Virgin blessed.[^6179]

*Troparion 1:* O Lady Theotokos, entreat the adored Trinity, of Whom thou didst impassibly bear One for us in the flesh, to bring peace unto those upon the earth, and for the remission of...

...transgressions to be granted unto those who sing of thee.[^6180]

*Troparion 2:* Though Emmanuel is understood to be one in Person, yet He is twofold in nature: for lo, He makes known two wills that are in Him, and two energies; Whose parent we confess to be the Theotokos.[^6181]

*Glory:* I weep for myself, thinking of the multitude of my sins, and the assaults of passions, the despondency of my soul, and the aberration of my mind: make me, who am in despair, worthy of salvation.[^6182]

*Both now:* Mine enemies having fallen upon me, O Lord, like robbers, stripped me of my high, radiant, and light-formed garment, and laid many wounds upon me: do thou, standing by, raise me up who am barely alive.[^6183]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparia and the rest.*[^6184]

*On Friday At Matins:*[^6185]

*After the 1st Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^6186]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The Place of the Skull became Paradise: for no sooner was the wood of the Cross set up, than immediately it blossomed forth the living Cluster, even Thee, O Savior, unto our gladness. Glory to Thee.[^6187]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^6188]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Let us the faithful sing and glorify our Savior and Deliverer who was crucified voluntarily as He knew and as He was well pleased: for He nailed the sins of men upon the Cross, delivering the human race from deception, and made them worthy of the Kingdom.[^6189]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Standing once by the Cross of the Lord, the pure Theotokos cried out weeping: Woe is me, O my divine Child! O my sweetest Light! How art Thou stretched out upon the Cross, who divinely stretched out heaven like a curtain, and by Thy command bringest up springs of waters from the sea?[^6190]

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^6191]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Having endured the crucifixion by Thy will, Thou didst deliver men from corruption, O Savior! We the faithful hymn and worship Thee, for Thou hast enlightened us by the power of the Cross: and all of us glorify Thee, O Lover of mankind, Compassionate One, as the Giver of life and the Lord.[^6192]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^6193]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Voluntarily, O Compassionate One, Thou didst endure the Cross, and as almighty God Thou didst destroy the ancient curse incurred for the sake of food; therefore, O Master Christ, we hymn and worship Thy divine and precious passions, glorifying unceasingly Thy dispensation which is beyond all speech.[^6194]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His Saints, the God of Israel.*[^6195]

*Martyrikon:* Today the memory of the Passion-bearers shines forth; for it hath a beam also from heaven: the Angelic choir keeps festival, and the human race feasts together. Therefore they pray unto the Lord, that our souls may find mercy.[^6196]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* By the Cross of thy Son, O thou who art graced of God, all deception of idols was abolished, and the demonic might was trampled down. Therefore we the faithful, as is our duty, ever hymn and bless thee, and confessing thee truly to be the Theotokos, we magnify thee.[^6197]

*After the 3rd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 5:*[^6198]

*Tone 5, Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*[^6199]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross and fallen asleep, O Jesus, King of all, the Angelic ranks were struck with awe, and forthwith the demonic hosts were defeated, and the bars of Hades were shattered; and the tyranny of death was cast down, and the dead who were in the tombs arose.[^6200]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Fleeing from the bitterness of the tree, Adam slipped into corruption through the envy of the serpent; but when Thou wast nailed, O Jesus, he enjoyed life, and for the sake of the tree of the Cross he is again settled in the heavens; and the serpent was abolished, and corruption was swallowed up, and all of us offer glory unto Thee.[^6201]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee, O Christ, hanging voluntarily upon the Cross in the midst of thieves, Thy Mother, torn in her maternal womb, said: O sinless Son, how art Thou unjustly nailed upon the Cross like an evildoer, desiring to bring the human race to life, as Thou art supremely good?[^6202]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Thou savest me from passions by Thy Passion, O my Christ).*
*A work of Joseph.*
*Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^6203]
Unto God the Savior, who guided the people in the sea with dry feet, and drowned Pharaoh with all his host: unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.[^6204]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^6205]

*Troparion 1:* Voluntarily, O Christ, Thou didst endure the passion that slays passions, and Thou slewest him who of old slew us in Paradise; therefore we glorify Thy goodness.[^6206]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, and the enemy fell down, and we who were fallen have been raised up, and have become dwellers of Paradise, O Christ, glorifying the majesty of Thy kingdom.[^6207]

*Martyrikon:* Being well armed with the shield of the Cross, ye drew yourselves up for every struggle, O all-wise Great Martyrs: and having conquered him, ye received glory.[^6208]

*Martyrikon:* As precious lambs, O sufferers, ye offered yourselves unto the Lamb slain for our sake, manifestly abolishing unclean sacrifices; therefore we call you blessed, O all-praised ones.[^6209]

*Theotokion:* A new Child unto us, the Ancient of days, hast thou brought forth, O pure Virgin, who renews aged human nature, O all-immaculate one, by His divine Passion.[^6210]

*Second Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*The same Heirmos.*[^6211]

*Troparion 1:* A fountain of tender mercy and a fervent protectress do I know thee to be, O all-immaculate Virgin Mother Mary, and I cry unto thee: Have mercy and take pity upon my humble soul.[^6212]

*Troparion 2:* Within thy womb the Son of God took up His abode, O pure one, and having assumed human nature, as He is supremely good, He delivered all from the corruption of the serpent.[^6213]

*Troparion 3:* Be thou my enlightenment and hope of salvation, O all-immaculate Birthgiver of God, loosing the bonds of my transgressions, and delivering me from future torment and condemnation.[^6214]

*Troparion 4:* Free my humble soul from evil thoughts, O Birthgiver of God, and make it a dwelling-place of God, that according to my duty I may ever glorify thee.[^6215]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* By the power of Thy Cross, O Christ, establish my mind, that I may sing and glorify Thy saving crucifixion.[^6216]

*Troparion 1:* Being lifted up upon the Tree, O Savior, Thou witherest the fruit of corruption, and Thou pourest forth unto us from Thy ribs the fountains of incorruption, O Master.[^6217]

*Troparion 2:* Being slain upon the Cross as a lamb, marking the thresholds of our souls with Thy divine blood, O Master; therefore with fear we glorify Thee.[^6218]

*Martyrikon:* Bound for the sake of Christ, O sufferers, and wounded in diverse ways, and cast unto beasts, ye remained immutable.[^6219]

*Martyrikon:* As clusters of the vine of life, the martyrs of our God poured forth the wine of suffering, gladdening the hearts of the faithful.[^6220]

*Theotokion:* Dying upon the Tree, O pure one, thy Son and Lord appeared as the Mediator of life unto us, glorifying those who sing to thee.[^6221]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6222]

*Troparion 1:* Lead me out by thy compassionate goodness, O Birthgiver of God, who have fallen into the pit of destruction of grievous sins.[^6223]

*Troparion 2:* Having arrived at the evening of life, and being held fast by perplexity, I cry unto thee, O all-hymned one: Appear as my helper.[^6224]

*Troparion 3:* Holy pure Theotokos, grant forgiveness of my transgressions, and ask for me salvation and eternal rejoicing.[^6225]

*Troparion 4:* Grant me a drop of tears, O pure one, that from my heart I may drive away perplexity, and sincerely hymn thee.[^6226]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard the report of the power of the Cross, that Paradise was opened thereby, and I cried out: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^6227]

*Troparion 1:* When Thou didst set upon the Cross, O Christ, Sun of righteousness, Thou didst shine forth unwaning light unto us who sing, O Word, of Thy dread dispensation.[^6228]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou, O Judge, didst stand once before the judgment-seat, O Christ, Thou didst condemn the unrighteous enemy, and Thou wast crucified in the midst of the unrighteous, justifying us.[^6229]

*Martyrikon:* Crowned victoriously, the sufferers of the Lord put the invisible enemy to shame, and cried out: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^6230]

*Martyrikon:* Coming together, ye faithful, let us honor the unfading flowers of the spiritual Paradise, the precious vessels of Christ, the passion-bearers.[^6231]

*Theotokion:* When thou didst behold Christ upon the Cross, whom thou didst bear, O pure one, thou wast amazed at His ineffable long-suffering; therefore with Him we glorify thee.[^6232]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6233]

*Troparion 1:* Concerning my evil deeds and manifold transgressions, who shall be able to entreat the Judge, unless thou, O pure one, the only protectress of those who sin?[^6234]

*Troparion 2:* Raise me up by thy power, O all-pure one, who am fallen by many transgressions and have enslaved my soul to sin, and release me from servitude by thy prayer.[^6235]

*Troparion 3:* As having borne the Creator and King of all, O all-immaculate, pure Theotokos, do thou deliver me from every defiled transgression.[^6236]

*Troparion 4:* I weep for myself when I come to contemplate my many transgressions and the unquenchable fire. I pray: Grant me time for repentance, O all-pure one.[^6237]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early, we cry unto Thee, O Lord: Save us! For Thou art our God; besides Thee we know no other.[^6238]

*Troparion 1:* The rocks, perceiving Thee lifted up upon the rock, O Christ, were split asunder, and the foundations of the earth were shaken.[^6239]

*Troparion 2:* The sun laid aside its brightness when Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree, O Sun of righteousness, long-suffering One.[^6240]

*Martyrikon:* Ye shine with the brightness of miracles, O holy ones, driving away the darkness of diseases by grace.[^6241]

*Martyrikon:* Your bodies were cut in pieces with swords, but the spirit of divine love ye kept uncut, O martyrs.[^6242]

*Theotokion:* Beholding the Savior lifted up upon the Cross, weeping thou didst cry aloud, O all-immaculate Virgin Mother.[^6243]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6244]

*Troparion 1:* When I have received the wound of sin in my whole body, heal me with the herb of thy tender mercy, O Lady.[^6245]

*Troparion 2:* Of old thou didst stop the flow of corruption by thy divine childbirth; and now stop the flow of my transgressions, O all-immaculate one.[^6246]

*Troparion 3:* Have mercy and take pity upon my soul, O Lady, and deliver it from condemnation and eternal torment.[^6247]

*Troparion 4:* Look down and hear my voice, O Lady; and I pray: Deliver me from eternal torment.[^6248]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The abyss surrounded me, the sea-monster became my tomb; but I cried unto Thee, the Lover of mankind, and Thy right hand saved me, O Lord.[^6249]

*Troparion 1:* The Cross was planted upon the earth, and the fall of the demons took place, and faith received the beginning of establishment, and wickedness was driven away from our midst.[^6250]

*Troparion 2:* The sun was extinguished when Thou didst kindle Thy flesh like a candle upon the Tree, O Lord; and the drachma was found which had been buried by dark passions.[^6251]

*Martyrikon:* When Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree, O Lover of mankind, Thou hadst a choir of martyrs following in Thy footsteps, imitating Thy Passion, the mediatress of dispassion.[^6252]

*Martyrikon:* Ye dried up deceitful streams by the pouring forth of your blood, and ye quenched the alien fire of the demons with divine dew, O crown-bearing martyrs.[^6253]

*Theotokion:* A sword pierced thy heart, O all-immaculate Virgin, when thou didst behold the Creator crucified, and His divine ribs pierced with a spear.[^6254]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6255]

*Troparion 1:* The abyss of transgressions hath encompassed me, and the depth of sin holds me fast, and brings me unto ruinous despair; O holy Lady, do thou now save me.[^6256]

*Troparion 2:* Raise me up who lie upon the bed of sins, O most holy Lady, and grant me saving illumination through repentance.[^6257]

*Troparion 3:* Cleanse me, O Lover of mankind, through the prayers of her who purely gave Thee birth, and deliver Thy world from every affliction; and make us worthy of eternal glory.[^6258]

*Troparion 4:* Continually I promise to depart from evil deeds, but always I lie, and grieve my Master. O all-pure Lady, grant me amendment.[^6259]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* O Thou Who didst save the hymn-singing youths in the fiery furnace: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^6260]

*Troparion 1:* That we might be delivered from pleasurable sin, Thou didst taste gall, O Christ, Thou vital Sweetness.[^6261]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou wast wounded upon the Tree, O Christ, the ancient wounds of Adam were healed.[^6262]

*Martyrikon:* By their voluntary impulses the passion-bearers separated themselves unto suffering, and appeared as victors.[^6263]

*Martyrikon:* Having honored God by painful dishonor, ye received the highest honor, O sufferers.[^6264]

*Theotokion:* After childbirth, O pure one, thou remainest even as before childbirth; for He that was born was God, that He might deify mankind.[^6265]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6266]

*Troparion 1:* O pure Birthgiver of God, despise me not who continually flee under thy shelter with faith.[^6267]

*Troparion 2:* Drying up the pasture of my passions, grant me to pour forth drops of tears, O Birthgiver of God.[^6268]

*Troparion 3:* Bound as I am with the chains of sin, loose me by thy prayers, O all-immaculate one, who didst bear the all-hymned God.[^6269]

*Troparion 4:* Unto thee I flee with faith, and upon thee, O pure one, do I call: Deliver me, O Virgin, from eternal fire.[^6270]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Who before the ages was begotten of the Father as Son and God, and in the last times was incarnate of the Virgin Mother: ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^6271]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou Who by the Tree didst heal the curse of the tree, and didst pour forth blessing unto men, O Supremely-good One: Thee, O Savior, do we hymn, and Thee do we glorify unto the ages.[^6272]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst humble the exalted serpent by Thy Cross, and being humbled Thou didst raise up him who had fallen grievously: Thee, O Savior, do we hymn, and supremely exalt unto all ages.[^6273]

*Martyrikon:* The destroyers of deception and champions of the divine faith, pillars of the Church and truly firm diamonds, the weapons of Christ: with faith let us honor the passion-bearers in hymns.[^6274]

*Martyrikon:* The glorious passion-bearers, having shone forth like the sun, dispersed the clouds of diseases by grace, and destroyed the gloom of impiety by their Trinitarian faith.[^6275]

*Theotokion:* A bridal escort was sent unto thee, O Virgin, even Gabriel, crying unto thee and saying: Rejoice, most radiant palace of Christ the King of all, wherein taking up His abode, He deified all mankind.[^6276]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6277]

*Troparion 1:* Being in perplexity both in heart and mind, not fearing the threat of Gehenna, I continually sin; but do thou, O Virgin, dispel my perplexity, and deliver me from the fire.[^6278]

*Troparion 2:* By the pleasures of my flesh I am often drawn away, and like a captive I am sold, continually angering God. O Theotokos, the only hope of the hopeless, do thou thyself have mercy on me.[^6279]

*Troparion 3:* For thy prayer is not put to shame, O all-immaculate one: for whatsoever thou wilt, thou bestowest, praying unto thy Son and God; therefore I pray unto thee: Have mercy and save my humble soul.[^6280]

*Troparion 4:* By bodily diseases, and passionate uprisings, and spiritual wounds I am grievously tormented; O thou who didst bear the only Benefactor, make me whole by thy prayers.[^6281]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who beyond mind and word art the Mother of God, who in time didst ineffably bear the Timeless One, we the faithful magnify with one accord.[^6282]

*Troparion 1:* The enemy's power and dominion were taken away, O only mighty Lord, when Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, and didst cover Thy fingers with blood upon it.[^6283]

*Troparion 2:* The lawless men who crucified Thee dug through Thy hands and Thy feet, O my Christ, and numbered Thy bones, and gave Thee gall with vinegar to drink.[^6284]

*Martyrikon:* With radiant lips ye proclaimed before the tormentors God Who became man, O passion-bearers, and ye inherited glory.[^6285]

*Martyrikon:* The enemies who wounded you received disease, striking you with wounds and diverse torments, O all-honored physicians of diseases, divine martyrs.[^6286]

*Theotokion:* Light shone forth unto us from thee, O pure one, even Jesus, and He enlightened all creation by His crucifixion, and drove away the demonic darkness.[^6287]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The same Heirmos.*[^6288]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me tears of repentance, O all-pure one, that I may weep for my grievous and unrighteous deeds before the arrival of the end of my life.[^6289]

*Troparion 2:* Why, O soul, dost thou grieve thy Master, doing unrighteous things, and dost not lift up thine eyes? Before the end, therefore, hasten and repent.[^6290]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver me from evil transgressions and sorrows; grant me mercy, O all-pure Virgin, and divine communion of incorruptible life.[^6291]

*Troparion 4:* Thee who ineffably didst bear God have we acquired as protectress, and an invincible wall, and salvation of souls, and a fountain of miracles.[^6292]

*It is truly meet: Little Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual psalms.*[^6293]

*On the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 5:*[^6294]

*Sticheron 1:* No sooner was the wood of Thy Cross set up, O Christ, than deception was driven away, and grace blossomed forth; for no longer is there the tyranny of condemnation, but the victory of salvation hath appeared unto us. For the Cross is our boast, the Cross is our rejoicing.[^6295]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: ... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^6296]

*Sticheron 2:* For our sake Thou wast led as a sheep to the sacrifice, O Christ, and as an innocent lamb, Emmanuel, unto voluntary slaughter, and Thou wast numbered with the transgressors. Come, ye families of the nations, sing and worship Him Who was suspended upon the Cross, the endless Life.[^6297]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us: ... And establish Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish Thou.*[^6298]

*Martyrikon:* Blessed is the host of the heavenly King! For though the passion-bearers were earth-born, yet they hastened to attain unto the angelic dignity, taking no heed for their bodies; and by reason of their sufferings they were counted worthy of the honor of the Bodiless Powers. Through their prayers, O Lord, save our souls.[^6299]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:*[^6300]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^6301]
Assuage the affliction of my much-afflicted soul, cried out the Immaculate One in sorrow unto Christ: For if Thou savest the earthly by Thy Passion, yet Thou tearest my soul, O Word. Thou art my sweetest Light, Thou art my Child and my Creator: I hymn Thy long-suffering.[^6302]

*It is good to give thanks: Trisagion; after Our Father: troparion, Litany, and the rest as usual.*[^6303]

*On Friday, the Beatitudes, Tone 5:*[^6304]

*Troparion 1:* The thief upon the cross, having believed Thee to be God, O Christ, confessed Thee purely from the heart, crying: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^6305]

*Troparion 2:* Having died upon the Cross on account of the tasting of food, Thou hast made us alive who were fallen, O compassionate Giver of life, and hast made us again, O Good One, to be dwellers of Paradise.[^6306]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast nailed upon the Cross, O Christ, the true Vine, and didst pour forth the drink of salvation, watering the hearts of all the faithful with grace.[^6307]

*Martyrikon:* Being beaten, O wise ones, and dismembered in your limbs, ye imaged the slaughter of the Master; therefore, O martyrs of Christ, ye are continually called blessed.[^6308]

*Glory: Triadikon:* We worship Thee with faith, the one God in three Persons, the indivisible and supremely divine essence, crying unto Thee: Glory to Thee, O consubstantial Trinity, our God![^6309]

*Both now: Theotokion:* With weeping the Lady smote herself, seeing upon the Tree of the Cross our Life, Who had died voluntarily; therefore with divine voices we all continually call her blessed.[^6310]

## Saturday: All Saints and the Departed[^6311]

*On Friday Evening.*[^6312]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Martyrs, Hierarchs, and Venerables, Tone 5:*[^6313]
*Tone 5, Podoben: "Rejoice, O container of ascetics" (Ра́дꙋйсѧ по́стникѡвъ):*[^6314]

*Sticheron 1:* The passion-bearers quenched the flame of cruel impiety with the streams of their blood, kindling the brightness of piety in all the world, O martyrs; and the falsely named gods, and their wicked craft, and their temples, they burned down utterly. They shone forth a most pure light unto those upon earth, enlightened by which we flee the darkness of godlessness and idolatrous deception, worshipping Christ, Who grants great mercy unto the world.[^6315]

*Sticheron 2:* Having easily destroyed the words and snares of the impious heretics, ye became weapons of the unoriginate Father, the co-unoriginate Son, and the Holy Spirit, in three Persons teaching the faithful with pious wisdom the unity of the Godhead, and confirming the Orthodox preaching. Therefore ye are called blessed, O all-sacred shepherds, for on life-bearing pastures ye tended the flock of Christ, for whose sake ye endured all pains and manifold tribulations.[^6316]
