[^5722]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Tone 5 Penitential Sticheron 1 at the Tuesday Matins Aposticha.
[^5723]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). LXX Ps. 89:14, 16. The printed text records the incipit and concluding clause with internal dashes, indicating the customary weekday Matins verse.
[^5724]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Tone 5 Penitential Sticheron 2 at the Tuesday Matins Aposticha, combining the Scriptural types of the barren fig tree (Matt. 21:19) and the Prodigal Son (Luke 15:11-32).
[^5725]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). LXX Ps. 89:17.
[^5726]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Martyrikon at the Tuesday Matins Aposticha, Tone 5.
[^5727]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Doxastikon / Theotokion at the Tuesday Matins Aposticha, Tone 5.
[^5728]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Concluding rubrics of Tuesday Matins: LXX Ps. 91:2 ('It is good to give praise'), Trisagion prayers, Troparion, litany, and dismissal into the First Hour.
[^5729]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Tuesday Beatitudes (Блаженна) in Tone 5, appointed for the weekday Divine Liturgy or Typika.
[^5730]: Physical Leaf p561 (Printed Folio 559). Troparion 1 of the Tuesday Beatitudes, Tone 5, invoking the repentance of the Good Thief (Luke 23:42).
[^5731]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Conclusion of Troparion 2 of the Tuesday Beatitudes, Tone 5, pleading for deliverance through the example of the penitent harlot (Luke 7:37-50).
[^5732]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Troparion 3 of the Tuesday Beatitudes, addressed to St. John the Forerunner.
[^5733]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Martyrikon of the Tuesday Beatitudes, evoking the cup of martyrdom (Matt. 20:22-23).
[^5734]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Triadikon of the Tuesday Beatitudes, Tone 5.
[^5735]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Concluding Theotokion of the Tuesday Beatitudes, Tone 5.
[^5736]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Tuesday evening rubric opening the liturgical day of Wednesday dedicated to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.
[^5737]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Lucernarium (На Господи воззвахъ) rubric: Stichera of the Cross in Tone 5.
[^5738]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Melodic model rubric: Tone 5, Podoben: 'Rejoice, O container of ascetics' (Радуйся постниковъ, Greek: Χαίροις ἀσκητικῶν).
[^5739]: Physical Leaf p562 (Printed Folio 560). Sticheron 1 of the Cross at Vespers, Tone 5.
[^5740]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Conclusion of Sticheron 2 of the Cross at Vespers, enumerating the instruments of the Passion: spear, sponge, and reed (Matt. 27:29, 48; John 19:29, 34).
[^5741]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Sticheron 3 of the Cross at Vespers, Tone 5, meditating on the cancellation of the handwriting of Adam's sin (Col. 2:14).
[^5742]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Rubric introducing the prosomoia Stichera to the Theotokos (Stavrotheotokia) at Vespers, sung to the same melody.
[^5743]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Stavrotheotokion Sticheron 1 at Vespers, portraying the maternal lamentation of the Virgin at the foot of the Cross.
[^5744]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Stavrotheotokion Sticheron 2 at Vespers, contemplating Christ's precious blood as the ransom for mankind (1 Pet. 1:18-19).
[^5745]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Stavrotheotokion Sticheron 3 at Vespers, praising the Theotokos as the living Cherubic throne.
[^5746]: Physical Leaf p563 (Printed Folio 561). Doxastikon / Stavrotheotokion at Vespers, Tone 5, depicting the Virgin as the Ewe-lamb following her Lamb to the slaughter (cf. Isa. 53:7).
[^5747]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Lucernarium conclusion rubrics: the ancient hymn 'O Gladsome Light' (Φῶς ἱλαρόν), daily Prokeimenon, and the evening prayer 'Vouchsafe, O Lord'.
[^5748]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Rubric for Tuesday evening Aposticha. The printed heading designates them as 'Penitential Stichera' (стїхи̑ры покаѧ́нны), though the hymnographic content is dedicated to the Precious Cross.
[^5749]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Sticheron 1 of the Cross at the Aposticha, commemorating Moses stretching out his hands in the form of a cross to defeat Amalek (Ex. 17:11-12) and Emperor Constantine's vision of the Cross. The parenthetical '(И҆́мⷶкъ)' (Name) is the variable liturgical slot for the ruling monarch (Emperor Franz Joseph I in the 1895 Austro-Hungarian context).
[^5750]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). LXX Ps. 122:1-2.
[^5751]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Sticheron 2 of the Cross at the Aposticha, Tone 5.
[^5752]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). LXX Ps. 122:3-4.
[^5753]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Martyrikon at the Tuesday evening Aposticha, Tone 5.
[^5754]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Melodic model rubric: Tone 5, Podoben: 'O venerable father' (Преподобне отче, Greek: Ὅσιε πάτερ).
[^5755]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Doxastikon / Stavrotheotokion at the Aposticha, Tone 5, contrasting Christ's outstretched hands on the Cross with Adam's illicit reaching for the forbidden fruit in Eden.
[^5756]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Concluding rubrics of Tuesday Vespers: Song of Simeon (Luke 2:29-32), Trisagion, Apolytikia, litany, and dismissal.
[^5757]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Tuesday Small Compline (Повечеріе) rubrical opening.
[^5758]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos at Tuesday Small Compline, Tone 5.
[^5759]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Ode 1 Heirmos of the Tuesday Small Compline Canon, based on the First Canticle of Moses (Ex. 15:1-19).
[^5760]: Physical Leaf p564 (Printed Folio 562). Theotokion refrain throughout the Small Compline Canon: 'Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us' (Прест҃а́ѧ Гпⷭ҇жѐ Бцⷢе, спасѝ на́съ).
[^5761]: Physical Leaf p565 (Printed Folio 563). Conclusion of Troparion 1, Ode 1 of the Tuesday Small Compline Canon.
[^5762]: Physical Leaf p565 (Printed Folio 563). Troparion 2, Ode 1 of the Tuesday Small Compline Canon.
[^5763]: Physical Leaf p565 (Printed Folio 563). Troparion 3 (Glory...), Ode 1 of the Tuesday Small Compline Canon.
[^5764]: Physical Leaf p565 (Printed Folio 563). Troparion 4 (Both now...), Ode 1 of the Tuesday Small Compline Canon.
